 succeeded in 160ms:
     1	//! src/tac/kmeans.zig — Lloyd's k-means with k-means++ init.
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: clusterer.
     4	//! See plan 02-tac-clustering.plan.md, paper §3.
     5	//!
     6	//! Used in two places:
     7	//!   1. As TAC's per-token clustering primitive (paper §3.3 final step).
     8	//!   2. As PQ codebook training primitive (paper §4) — indexer calls fit() per
     9	//!      subspace.
    10	//!
    11	//! Algorithm (paper §3 references the canonical Lloyd / k-means++):
    12	//!   - k-means++ seeding: D²(x)-weighted sampling for centroid 1..k-1.
    13	//!   - Lloyd's iterations: assign → update → check relative WCSS-delta < tol.
    14	//!   - Empty clusters re-seed with the farthest-from-any-centroid vector.
    15	//!     // paper-gap: paper just says Lloyd's; "farthest point" is the
    16	//!     // canonical fix-up and is fully deterministic.
    17	//!   - k > n: degenerate — every vector is its own centroid; remaining slots
    18	//!     copy the last vector and are flagged unused at assignment time.
    19	//!     // paper-gap: TAC's θ=39 floor normally prevents this, but PQ
    20	//!     // training and tiny test fixtures can hit it.
    21	
    22	const std = @import("std");
    23	const vec = @import("../util/vec.zig");
    24	const rng_mod = @import("../util/rng.zig");
    25	
    26	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    27	
    28	pub const KMeansParams = struct {
    29	    k: u32,
    30	    max_iters: u32 = 25,
    31	    tol: f32 = 1e-4,
    32	    seed: u64,
    33	    /// Worker threads for the per-vector assignment step (k-means' hot loop).
    34	    /// `1` short-circuits to the strictly-serial code path, byte-identical
    35	    /// to the pre-parallel implementation. `>=2` parallelises argmin across
    36	    /// a static chunk of vectors per thread; the cluster-sum accumulation
    37	    /// that feeds the centroid update runs serially in vector order, so the
    38	    /// produced centroids are **byte-equal** across any thread count for
    39	    /// the same seed (paper-strict reproducibility — see plan-07).
    40	    n_threads: u32 = 1,
    41	};
    42	
    43	pub const KMeansResult = struct {
    44	    centroids: []f32,
    45	    assignments: []u32,
    46	    wcss: f32,
    47	    iters_run: u32,
    48	
    49	    pub fn deinit(self: *KMeansResult, gpa: Allocator) void {
    50	        gpa.free(self.centroids);
    51	        gpa.free(self.assignments);
    52	        self.* = undefined;
    53	    }
    54	};
    55	
    56	pub const KMeansError = error{
    57	    KmeansBudgetTooSmall,
    58	    EmptyVectorSet,
    59	    DimMismatch,
    60	} || Allocator.Error || vec.VecError || rng_mod.RngError || std.Thread.SpawnError;
    61	
    62	/// Cluster `vectors` into `p.k` groups via Lloyd's algorithm, k-means++ seeded.
    63	///
    64	/// `vectors` is a flat row-major matrix of length `n * dim` where `n` is
    65	/// inferred. Centroids and assignments are heap-allocated on `gpa` and owned
    66	/// by the returned `KMeansResult`.
    67	pub fn fit(
    68	    vectors: []const f32,
    69	    dim: u32,
    70	    p: KMeansParams,
    71	    gpa: Allocator,
    72	) KMeansError!KMeansResult {
    73	    if (p.k == 0) return error.KmeansBudgetTooSmall;
    74	    if (dim == 0) return error.DimMismatch;
    75	    if (vectors.len == 0) return error.EmptyVectorSet;
    76	    if (vectors.len % @as(usize, dim) != 0) return error.DimMismatch;
    77	
    78	    const n: usize = vectors.len / @as(usize, dim);
    79	    const k: usize = p.k;
    80	    const d: usize = dim;
    81	
    82	    const centroids = try gpa.alloc(f32, k * d);
    83	    errdefer gpa.free(centroids);
    84	    const assignments = try gpa.alloc(u32, n);
    85	    errdefer gpa.free(assignments);
    86	
    87	    // ---- Degenerate: k >= n -> each vector its own centroid. ----
    88	    if (k >= n) {
    89	        @memcpy(centroids[0 .. n * d], vectors);
    90	        // Fill unused centroid slots with copies of the last vector. They
    91	        // are never the closest centroid (distance 0 wins on a real vector)
    92	        // and downstream callers see them but never assign to them.
    93	        var c: usize = n;
    94	        while (c < k) : (c += 1) {
    95	            @memcpy(centroids[c * d ..][0..d], vectors[(n - 1) * d ..][0..d]);
    96	        }
    97	        for (0..n) |i| assignments[i] = @intCast(i);
    98	        return .{
    99	            .centroids = centroids,
   100	            .assignments = assignments,
   101	            .wcss = 0.0,
   102	            .iters_run = 0,
   103	        };
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    var rng = rng_mod.Rng.init(p.seed);
   107	
   108	    // ---- k-means++ seeding (Arthur & Vassilvitskii 2007). ----
   109	    // paper §3 cites this as the standard init; D²-weighted sampling.
   110	    const min_dist_sq = try gpa.alloc(f32, n);
   111	    defer gpa.free(min_dist_sq);
   112	
   113	    // First centroid: uniform random pick.
   114	    {
   115	        const idx0 = rng.nextIndex(n);
   116	        @memcpy(centroids[0..d], vectors[idx0 * d ..][0..d]);
   117	        for (0..n) |i| {
   118	            min_dist_sq[i] = try vec.l2sq(
   119	                vectors[i * d ..][0..d],
   120	                centroids[0..d],
   121	            );
   122	        }
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    var c: usize = 1;
   126	    while (c < k) : (c += 1) {
   127	        const idx = try rng_mod.weightedSample(&rng, min_dist_sq);
   128	        @memcpy(centroids[c * d ..][0..d], vectors[idx * d ..][0..d]);
   129	        for (0..n) |i| {
   130	            const dist = try vec.l2sq(
   131	                vectors[i * d ..][0..d],
   132	                centroids[c * d ..][0..d],
   133	            );
   134	            if (dist < min_dist_sq[i]) min_dist_sq[i] = dist;
   135	        }
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    // ---- Lloyd's iterations. ----
   139	    const cluster_sums = try gpa.alloc(f32, k * d);
   140	    defer gpa.free(cluster_sums);
   141	    const cluster_counts = try gpa.alloc(u32, k);
   142	    defer gpa.free(cluster_counts);
   143	    // Per-vector min distance to its assigned centroid. Doubles as the
   144	    // WCSS-per-vector contribution that phase 2 (serial accumulation) reads
   145	    // in order, and as the empty-cluster re-seed key (`argmax(min_to_any)`).
   146	    const min_to_any = try gpa.alloc(f32, n);
   147	    defer gpa.free(min_to_any);
   148	
   149	    var prev_wcss: f32 = std.math.inf(f32);
   150	    var wcss: f32 = 0.0;
   151	    var iters_run: u32 = 0;
   152	
   153	    var iter: u32 = 0;
   154	    while (iter < p.max_iters) : (iter += 1) {
   155	        // ---- Assignment step. ----
   156	        wcss = 0.0;
   157	        @memset(cluster_sums, 0.0);
   158	        @memset(cluster_counts, 0);
   159	
   160	        // Phase 1: per-vector argmin. Optionally parallel across n_threads
   161	        // (plan-07). Pure per-vector so writes to `assignments` and
   162	        // `min_to_any` are byte-equal across thread counts.
   163	        try assignVectors(
   164	            vectors,
   165	            n,
   166	            d,
   167	            k,
   168	            centroids,
   169	            assignments,
   170	            min_to_any,
   171	            p.n_threads,
   172	            gpa,
   173	        );
   174	
   175	        // Phase 2: serial accumulation in vector order. Fixed reduction
   176	        // order keeps centroids byte-equal across n_threads (paper-strict).
   177	        for (0..n) |i| {
   178	            const a: usize = assignments[i];
   179	            wcss += min_to_any[i];
   180	            const v_i = vectors[i * d ..][0..d];
   181	            const sum_slot = cluster_sums[a * d ..][0..d];
   182	            for (0..d) |dd| sum_slot[dd] += v_i[dd];
   183	            cluster_counts[a] += 1;
   184	        }
   185	
   186	        iters_run = iter + 1;
   187	
   188	        // ---- Convergence check. ----
   189	        // Skip on first iter (prev_wcss == inf). Paper §3 does not pin a
   190	        // tolerance; relative delta is the standard choice.
   191	        if (iter > 0) {
   192	            const denom = if (prev_wcss > 0.0) prev_wcss else 1.0;
   193	            const rel_delta = (prev_wcss - wcss) / denom;
   194	            if (rel_delta < p.tol) break;
   195	        }
   196	
   197	        // ---- Update step. ----
   198	        for (0..k) |cc| {
   199	            if (cluster_counts[cc] > 0) {
   200	                const inv: f32 = 1.0 / @as(f32, @floatFromInt(cluster_counts[cc]));
   201	                const slot = centroids[cc * d ..][0..d];
   202	                const sum_slot = cluster_sums[cc * d ..][0..d];
   203	                for (0..d) |dd| slot[dd] = sum_slot[dd] * inv;
   204	            } else {
   205	                // Empty cluster — re-seed with farthest-from-any-centroid
   206	                // vector. paper-gap: paper does not specify; this is the
   207	                // canonical deterministic fix-up.
   208	                const far_idx = try vec.argmax(min_to_any);
   209	                @memcpy(
   210	                    centroids[cc * d ..][0..d],
   211	                    vectors[far_idx * d ..][0..d],
   212	                );
   213	                // Mark this vector as "claimed" so a second empty cluster in
   214	                // the same iteration doesn't pick the same point.
   215	                min_to_any[far_idx] = -std.math.inf(f32);
   216	            }
   217	        }
   218	
   219	        prev_wcss = wcss;
   220	    }

 succeeded in 144ms:

   230	/// Assign every vector to its closest centroid (paper §3 / Lloyd's
   231	/// assignment step). Writes `assignments[i] = argmin_c d²(v_i, μ_c)` and
   232	/// `min_to_any[i] = d²(v_i, μ_{a(i)})`.
   233	///
   234	/// `n_threads <= 1` runs serially. `n_threads >= 2` slices the n vectors
   235	/// into static contiguous chunks and computes argmin in parallel. Argmin is
   236	/// pure per-vector, so the writes to `assignments` and `min_to_any` are
   237	/// byte-equal across thread counts — the deterministic f32-reduction order
   238	/// is enforced by the caller's serial accumulation pass that consumes these
   239	/// arrays in order.
   240	fn assignVectors(
   241	    vectors: []const f32,
   242	    n: usize,
   243	    d: usize,
   244	    k: usize,
   245	    centroids: []const f32,
   246	    assignments: []u32,
   247	    min_to_any: []f32,
   248	    n_threads: u32,
   249	    gpa: Allocator,
   250	) KMeansError!void {
   251	    const real_threads: u32 = if (n_threads <= 1) 1 else @intCast(@min(@as(usize, n_threads), n));
   252	    if (real_threads <= 1) {
   253	        const dists = try gpa.alloc(f32, k);
   254	        defer gpa.free(dists);
   255	        try assignSlice(vectors, 0, n, d, k, centroids, assignments, min_to_any, dists);
   256	        return;
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    const Ctx = struct {
   260	        vectors: []const f32,
   261	        d: usize,
   262	        k: usize,
   263	        centroids: []const f32,
   264	        assignments: []u32,
   265	        min_to_any: []f32,
   266	        lo: usize,
   267	        hi: usize,
   268	        gpa: Allocator,
   269	        err_out: ?KMeansError,
   270	    };
   271	    const Runner = struct {
   272	        fn run(ctx: *Ctx) void {
   273	            const dists = ctx.gpa.alloc(f32, ctx.k) catch |err| {
   274	                ctx.err_out = err;
   275	                return;
   276	            };
   277	            defer ctx.gpa.free(dists);
   278	            assignSlice(
   279	                ctx.vectors,
   280	                ctx.lo,
   281	                ctx.hi,
   282	                ctx.d,
   283	                ctx.k,
   284	                ctx.centroids,
   285	                ctx.assignments,
   286	                ctx.min_to_any,
   287	                dists,
   288	            ) catch |err| {
   289	                ctx.err_out = err;
   290	                return;
   291	            };
   292	        }
   293	    };
   294	
   295	    const ctxs = try gpa.alloc(Ctx, real_threads);
   296	    defer gpa.free(ctxs);
   297	    const threads = try gpa.alloc(std.Thread, real_threads);
   298	    defer gpa.free(threads);
   299	
   300	    const chunk: usize = (n + real_threads - 1) / real_threads;
   301	    var t: u32 = 0;
   302	    while (t < real_threads) : (t += 1) {
   303	        const lo: usize = @as(usize, t) * chunk;
   304	        const hi: usize = @min(lo + chunk, n);
   305	        ctxs[t] = .{
   306	            .vectors = vectors,
   307	            .d = d,
   308	            .k = k,
   309	            .centroids = centroids,
   310	            .assignments = assignments,
   311	            .min_to_any = min_to_any,
   312	            .lo = lo,
   313	            .hi = hi,
   314	            .gpa = gpa,
   315	            .err_out = null,
   316	        };
   317	        threads[t] = try std.Thread.spawn(.{}, Runner.run, .{&ctxs[t]});
   318	    }
   319	    for (threads) |th| th.join();
   320	    for (ctxs) |c| if (c.err_out) |err| return err;
   321	}
   322	
   323	/// Per-slice argmin: writes `assignments[i]` and `min_to_any[i]` for
   324	/// `i in [lo, hi)`. `dists` is a `k`-length scratch buffer (caller-owned).
   325	fn assignSlice(
   326	    vectors: []const f32,
   327	    lo: usize,
   328	    hi: usize,
   329	    d: usize,
   330	    k: usize,
   331	    centroids: []const f32,
   332	    assignments: []u32,
   333	    min_to_any: []f32,
   334	    dists: []f32,
   335	) KMeansError!void {
   336	    var i: usize = lo;
   337	    while (i < hi) : (i += 1) {
   338	        const v_i = vectors[i * d ..][0..d];
   339	        for (0..k) |cc| {
   340	            dists[cc] = try vec.l2sq(v_i, centroids[cc * d ..][0..d]);
   341	        }
   342	        const a = try vec.argmin(dists[0..k]);
   343	        assignments[i] = @intCast(a);
   344	        min_to_any[i] = dists[a];
   345	    }
   346	}
   347

   349	// Tests — hand-checkable inputs, determinism, and Lloyd's monotonicity.
   350	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   357	    try testing.expectError(error.KmeansBudgetTooSmall, r);
   358	}

   390	test "fit: 3 well-separated 2D blobs recover blob means" {
   391	    // Three clusters of 6 points each, very tight, around (0,0), (10,0), (0,10).
   392	    var pts: [36]f32 = undefined;
   393	    const blob_centers = [_][2]f32{ .{ 0, 0 }, .{ 10, 0 }, .{ 0, 10 } };
   394	    var idx: usize = 0;
   395	    for (blob_centers) |bc| {
   396	        // 6 points per blob, jittered by tiny deterministic offsets.
   397	        const offsets = [_][2]f32{
   398	            .{ -0.05, 0.02 },  .{ 0.03, -0.04 }, .{ -0.01, -0.02 },
   399	            .{ 0.02, 0.05 },   .{ -0.03, 0.01 }, .{ 0.04, -0.03 },
   400	        };
   401	        for (offsets) |off| {
   402	            pts[idx * 2 + 0] = bc[0] + off[0];
   403	            pts[idx * 2 + 1] = bc[1] + off[1];
   404	            idx += 1;
   405	        }
   406	    }
   407	
   408	    var r = try fit(&pts, 2, .{ .k = 3, .max_iters = 50, .seed = 42 }, testing.allocator);
   409	    defer r.deinit(testing.allocator);
   410	
   411	    // Recovered centroids should be within 0.1 of the true blob centers.
   412	    // We don't know the order, so check each true center has *some* recovered
   413	    // centroid close to it.
   414	    for (blob_centers) |bc| {
   415	        var found = false;
   416	        for (0..3) |c| {
   417	            const cx = r.centroids[c * 2 + 0];
   418	            const cy = r.centroids[c * 2 + 1];
   419	            const dx = cx - bc[0];
   420	            const dy = cy - bc[1];
   421	            if (@sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < 0.1) {
   422	                found = true;
   423	                break;
   424	            }
   425	        }
   426	        try testing.expect(found);
   427	    }
   428	}

codex
The k-means parallelism itself keeps reductions serial, so the byte-equality claim is plausible at the algorithm level. Two implementation risks are emerging: worker-thread spawn failure paths don’t cleanly join already-started threads, and both PQ/k-means use caller allocators concurrently without requiring a thread-safe allocator.

   267	/// Outer-loop chunk size for the parallel build path. Each chunk's workers
   268	/// read a fixed `adj` snapshot, then a single-threaded commit pass appends in
   269	/// strict q-order. Smaller chunks = fresher snapshots = closer recall to the
   270	/// serial path; larger chunks = better speedup. 256 is the empirical sweet
   271	/// spot on the 26k Jira corpus (recall delta < 1%, ~3x speedup at 10 threads).
   272	const CHUNK_SIZE: u32 = 256;

   274	/// Per-layer plan for inserting one node q. Produced by a worker on a

   294	/// Build an HNSW over `n = centroids.len / dim` L2-normalised vectors.
   295	///
   296	/// Determinism: for fixed `(centroids, dim, seed, params)` — and `n_threads`
   297	/// is part of `params` — the produced CSR is byte-identical across runs.
   298	/// `n_threads=1` matches a reference serial implementation byte-for-byte;
   299	/// `n_threads >= 2` uses chunked deferred-commit which produces a
   300	/// different-but-deterministic graph (chunks don't see same-chunk commits —
   301	/// standard parallel HNSW contract). Recall stays high; the bundled tests
   302	/// measure recall@10 ≈ 0.988 at n_threads=10 vs ≥ 0.9 floor.
   303	pub fn build(
   304	    centroids: []const f32,
   305	    dim: u32,
   306	    seed: u64,
   307	    params: BuildParams,
   308	    gpa: Allocator,
   309	) HnswError!Hnsw {
   310	    if (dim == 0) return error.DimMismatch;
   311	    if (centroids.len % @as(usize, dim) != 0) return error.DimMismatch;
   312	    const n_usize = centroids.len / @as(usize, dim);
   313	    if (n_usize == 0) return error.EmptyCorpus;
   314	    if (n_usize > std.math.maxInt(u32)) return error.OutOfMemory;
   315	    const n: u32 = @intCast(n_usize);
   316	
   317	    const m: u32 = params.m;
   318	    const m_max: u32 = m;
   319	    const m_max0: u32 = 2 * m;
   320	    const ef_c: u32 = params.ef_construction;
   321	    // mL = 1 / ln(M) — Malkov §4.1, Eq. 7.
   322	    const m_l: f32 = 1.0 / @log(@as(f32, @floatFromInt(m)));
   323	
   324	    var rng = rng_mod.Rng.init(seed);

   327	    errdefer gpa.free(levels);
   328	    @memset(levels, 0);
   329

   331	    errdefer adj.deinit();

   335	    levels[0] = pickLevel(&rng, m_l);
   336	    max_level = levels[0];
   337	    try adj.ensureLevel(max_level);

   387	            &t_heuristic_ns,
   388	            &t_connect_ns,
   389	            gpa,
   390	        );
   391	    }
   392	
   393	    if (params.verbose) {
   394	        std.debug.print(

   396	            .{

   398	                params.n_threads,
   399	                @as(f64, @floatFromInt(nowNs() - t_build_start)) / 1e6,

   446	
   447	    adj.deinit();

   449	    return Hnsw{

   461	/// Serial insert loop — bit-identical to the pre-parallel build path.
   462	///
   463	/// Per-phase timing is accumulated into `t_*_ns` only when `verbose` is true;
   464	/// the timing calls are gated to keep the hot path branch-free at default
   465	/// settings.
   466	fn buildSerial(
   467	    centroids: []const f32,
   468	    dim: u32,
   469	    n: u32,
   470	    m: u32,
   471	    m_max: u32,
   472	    m_max0: u32,
   473	    ef_c: u32,
   474	    m_l: f32,
   475	    rng: *rng_mod.Rng,

   478	    entry_point_ptr: *NodeId,
   479	    max_level_ptr: *u8,
   480	    verbose: bool,
   481	    t_greedy_ns: *u64,
   482	    t_search_ns: *u64,
   483	    t_heuristic_ns: *u64,
   484	    t_connect_ns: *u64,
   485	    gpa: Allocator,
   486	) HnswError!void {
   487	    var entry_point = entry_point_ptr.*;
   488	    var max_level = max_level_ptr.*;
   489	
   490	    const visited_gen = try gpa.alloc(u32, n);
   491	    defer gpa.free(visited_gen);
   492	    @memset(visited_gen, 0);
   493	    var generation: u32 = 0;
   494	
   495	    var q: u32 = 1;
   496	    while (q < n) : (q += 1) {
   497	        const l_new = pickLevel(rng, m_l);
   498	        levels[q] = l_new;
   499	        try adj.ensureLevel(@max(l_new, max_level));
   500	
   501	        const q_vec = sliceVec(centroids, dim, q);
   502	
   503	        // Phase A: greedy descent through layers above l_new.
   504	        const t_a: u64 = if (verbose) nowNs() else 0;
   505	        var cur: NodeId = entry_point;
   506	        if (max_level > l_new) {
   507	            var L: u8 = max_level;
   508	            while (L > l_new) : (L -= 1) {
   509	                cur = greedySearchLayer(centroids, dim, adj, cur, q_vec, L);
   510	                if (L == 0) break;
   511	            }
   512	        }
   513	        if (verbose) t_greedy_ns.* += nowNs() - t_a;
   514	
   515	        // Phase B: at each layer ≤ min(max_level, l_new), do an ef-beam
   516	        // search from `cur`, pick neighbours via the heuristic, connect.
   517	        const top_layer: u8 = @min(max_level, l_new);
   518	        var L_signed: i32 = top_layer;
   519	        while (L_signed >= 0) : (L_signed -= 1) {
   520	            const L: u8 = @intCast(L_signed);
   521	            generation +%= 1;
   522	            if (generation == 0) {
   523	                @memset(visited_gen, 0);
   524	                generation = 1;
   525	            }
   526	
   527	            const t_s: u64 = if (verbose) nowNs() else 0;
   528	            var W = try searchLayer(
   529	                centroids,
   530	                dim,
   531	                adj,
   532	                cur,
   533	                q_vec,
   534	                ef_c,
   535	                L,
   536	                visited_gen,
   537	                generation,
   538	                gpa,
   539	            );
   540	            defer W.deinit(gpa);
   541	            if (verbose) t_search_ns.* += nowNs() - t_s;
   542	
   543	            // Drain W into a Cand buffer, sorted ascending — that's the
   544	            // candidate set for selectHeuristic. Also seed `cur` for the
   545	            // next layer down (best of W).
   546	            var cands_buf = try gpa.alloc(Cand, W.count());
   547	            defer gpa.free(cands_buf);
   548	            var idx: usize = 0;
   549	            while (W.pop()) |c| : (idx += 1) cands_buf[idx] = c;
   550	
   551	            // `cur` for next layer = best (highest-sim) candidate.
   552	            if (cands_buf.len > 0) {
   553	                var best_idx: usize = 0;
   554	                var best_sim: f32 = cands_buf[0].sim;
   555	                var k: usize = 1;
   556	                while (k < cands_buf.len) : (k += 1) {
   557	                    if (cands_buf[k].sim > best_sim) {
   558	                        best_sim = cands_buf[k].sim;
   559	                        best_idx = k;
   560	                    }

   560	                    }
   561	                }
   562	                cur = cands_buf[best_idx].node;
   563	            }
   564	
   565	            const cap_q: u32 = if (L == 0) m_max0 else m_max;
   566	            const t_h: u64 = if (verbose) nowNs() else 0;
   567	            const chosen = try selectHeuristic(
   568	                centroids,
   569	                dim,
   570	                q_vec,
   571	                cands_buf,
   572	                m,
   573	                gpa,
   574	            );

   576	            if (verbose) t_heuristic_ns.* += nowNs() - t_h;
   577	
   578	            // Wire q → chosen. Bidirectional with cap pruning on the other
   579	            // end via the heuristic.
   580	            const t_c: u64 = if (verbose) nowNs() else 0;
   581	            const q_neighbours = adj.neighbours(q, L);
   582	            try q_neighbours.ensureTotalCapacity(gpa, chosen.len);
   583	            for (chosen) |nbr_id| q_neighbours.appendAssumeCapacity(nbr_id);
   584	
   585	            for (chosen) |r_id| {
   586	                const r_neighbours = adj.neighbours(r_id, L);
   587	                try r_neighbours.append(gpa, q);
   588	                if (r_neighbours.items.len > cap_q) {
   589	                    try shrinkNeighbours(
   590	                        centroids,
   591	                        dim,
   592	                        r_id,
   593	                        r_neighbours,
   594	                        cap_q,
   595	                        gpa,
   596	                    );
   597	                }
   598	            }
   599	            if (verbose) t_connect_ns.* += nowNs() - t_c;
   600	        }
   601	
   602	        if (l_new > max_level) {
   603	            max_level = l_new;
   604	            entry_point = q;
   605	        }
   606	    }
   607	
   608	    entry_point_ptr.* = entry_point;
   609	    max_level_ptr.* = max_level;
   610	}
   611	
   612	/// Parallel insert loop — chunked deferred-commit. See module docstring for
   613	/// the determinism contract. n_threads must be >= 2; n_threads=1 should use
   614	/// buildSerial for bit-identical CSR.
   615	///
   616	/// Algorithm per chunk [q_lo, q_hi):
   617	///   1. Pick all l_new[q] for q ∈ [q_lo, q_hi) from the master Rng (serial,
   618	///      so the level sequence is identical across thread counts).
   619	///   2. ensureLevel(max(snapshot_max_level, max(l_new in chunk))) once, so
   620	///      worker reads see a stable adj layout.
   621	///   3. Spawn `n_threads` workers; each handles a contiguous slice of qs.
   622	///      Workers run greedy descent + searchLayer + selectHeuristic against
   623	///      the read-only adj snapshot. Output per-q is `chosen[L]` for each
   624	///      L ∈ [0..top_layer].
   625	///   4. Single-threaded commit pass walks q-order, applying q→chosen,
   626	///      chosen[i]→q, and shrinkNeighbours where over cap. Updates
   627	///      entry_point/max_level as a serial replay.
   628	fn buildParallel(
   629	    centroids: []const f32,
   630	    dim: u32,
   631	    n: u32,
   632	    m: u32,
   633	    m_max: u32,
   634	    m_max0: u32,
   635	    ef_c: u32,
   636	    m_l: f32,
   637	    n_threads_param: u32,
   638	    rng: *rng_mod.Rng,
   639	    levels: []u8,
   640	    adj: *Adj,
   641	    entry_point_ptr: *NodeId,
   642	    max_level_ptr: *u8,
   643	    verbose: bool,
   644	    t_greedy_ns: *u64,
   645	    t_search_ns: *u64,
   646	    t_heuristic_ns: *u64,
   647	    t_connect_ns: *u64,
   648	    gpa: Allocator,
   649	) HnswError!void {
   650	    var entry_point = entry_point_ptr.*;
   651	    var max_level = max_level_ptr.*;
   652	
   653	    // Per-worker scratch (visited_gen + arena). Allocated once and reused
   654	    // across chunks; arena reset per-chunk to bound peak memory.
   655	    const n_threads: u32 = n_threads_param;
   656	    const scratches = try gpa.alloc(WorkerScratch, n_threads);
   657	    defer gpa.free(scratches);
   658	    var scratches_inited: u32 = 0;
   659	    errdefer {
   660	        var i: u32 = 0;
   661	        while (i < scratches_inited) : (i += 1) {
   662	            gpa.free(scratches[i].visited_gen);
   663	            scratches[i].arena.deinit();
   664	        }
   665	    }
   666	    {
   667	        var t: u32 = 0;
   668	        while (t < n_threads) : (t += 1) {
   669	            const vgen = try gpa.alloc(u32, n);
   670	            @memset(vgen, 0);
   671	            scratches[t] = .{
   672	                .visited_gen = vgen,
   673	                .generation = 0,
   674	                .arena = std.heap.ArenaAllocator.init(gpa),
   675	            };
   676	            scratches_inited = t + 1;
   677	        }
   678	    }
   679	    defer {
   680	        var i: u32 = 0;
   681	        while (i < n_threads) : (i += 1) {
   682	            gpa.free(scratches[i].visited_gen);
   683	            scratches[i].arena.deinit();
   684	        }
   685	    }
   686	
   687	    // Per-chunk plan storage. Each slot is one InsertPlan; chosen_per_layer
   688	    // pointers live in the worker arena (recycled per chunk).
   689	    const plans = try gpa.alloc(InsertPlan, CHUNK_SIZE);
   690	    defer gpa.free(plans);
   691	
   692	    const threads = try gpa.alloc(std.Thread, n_threads);
   693	    defer gpa.free(threads);
   694	
   695	    var q_base: u32 = 1;
   696	    while (q_base < n) {
   697	        // Adaptive chunk sizing: when the graph is small (q_base low) the
   698	        // snapshot captures most of the graph anyway, but workers handling
   699	        // late-chunk q's would see almost no committed edges. Growing the
   700	        // chunk geometrically — chunk = min(CHUNK_SIZE, q_base) — keeps
   701	        // recall close to the serial path's because the snapshot is always
   702	        // a constant fraction (≥ 50%) of the graph at chunk-start. This
   703	        // preserves cross-thread determinism (any thread count uses the
   704	        // same chunk boundary because it's a function of q_base only).
   705	        const chunk_size: u32 = @min(CHUNK_SIZE, q_base);
   706	        const q_end: u32 = @min(q_base + chunk_size, n);
   707	        const chunk_len: u32 = q_end - q_base;
   708	
   709	        // (1) Pick all levels for this chunk serially (preserves Rng order).
   710	        var max_l_in_chunk: u8 = 0;
   711	        var i: u32 = 0;
   712	        while (i < chunk_len) : (i += 1) {
   713	            const l_new = pickLevel(rng, m_l);
   714	            levels[q_base + i] = l_new;
   715	            if (l_new > max_l_in_chunk) max_l_in_chunk = l_new;
   716	        }
   717	
   718	        // (2) Grow adj once for the chunk. ensureLevel reallocates rows[],
   719	        // so it must happen BEFORE worker dispatch.
   720	        try adj.ensureLevel(@max(max_l_in_chunk, max_level));
   721	
   722	        // Snapshot used by all workers in this chunk.
   723	        const snap_entry: NodeId = entry_point;
   724	        const snap_max_level: u8 = max_level;
   725	
   726	        // Reset per-worker arenas (fresh per-chunk).
   727	        {
   728	            var t: u32 = 0;
   729	            while (t < n_threads) : (t += 1) {
   730	                _ = scratches[t].arena.reset(.retain_capacity);
   731	            }
   732	        }
   733	
   734	        // (3) Dispatch workers. Each handles a contiguous slice of qs.
   735	        const Ctx = struct {
   736	            centroids: []const f32,
   737	            dim: u32,
   738	            adj: *Adj,
   739	            levels: []const u8,
   740	            ef_c: u32,
   741	            m: u32,
   742	            scratch: *WorkerScratch,
   743	            plans: []InsertPlan,
   744	            q_base: u32,
   745	            lo: u32,
   746	            hi: u32,
   747	            snap_entry: NodeId,
   748	            snap_max_level: u8,
   749	            err_out: ?HnswError,
   750	        };
   751	        const Runner = struct {
   752	            fn run(ctx: *Ctx) void {
   753	                runWorkerSlice(ctx) catch |err| {
   754	                    ctx.err_out = err;
   755	                };
   756	            }
   757	            fn runWorkerSlice(ctx: *Ctx) HnswError!void {
   758	                const arena = ctx.scratch.arena.allocator();
   759	                var qi: u32 = ctx.lo;
   760	                while (qi < ctx.hi) : (qi += 1) {
   761	                    const q_local: u32 = ctx.q_base + qi;
   762	                    const l_new = ctx.levels[q_local];
   763	                    const q_vec = sliceVec(ctx.centroids, ctx.dim, q_local);
   764	
   765	                    // Phase A: greedy descent through layers above l_new.
   766	                    var cur: NodeId = ctx.snap_entry;
   767	                    if (ctx.snap_max_level > l_new) {
   768	                        var L: u8 = ctx.snap_max_level;
   769	                        while (L > l_new) : (L -= 1) {
   770	                            cur = greedySearchLayer(ctx.centroids, ctx.dim, ctx.adj, cur, q_vec, L);
   771	                            if (L == 0) break;
   772	                        }
   773	                    }
   774	
   775	                    const top_layer: u8 = @min(ctx.snap_max_level, l_new);
   776	                    const chosen_per_layer = try arena.alloc([]u32, @as(usize, top_layer) + 1);
   777	
   778	                    var L_signed: i32 = top_layer;
   779	                    while (L_signed >= 0) : (L_signed -= 1) {
   780	                        const L: u8 = @intCast(L_signed);
   781	                        ctx.scratch.generation +%= 1;
   782	                        if (ctx.scratch.generation == 0) {
   783	                            @memset(ctx.scratch.visited_gen, 0);
   784	                            ctx.scratch.generation = 1;
   785	                        }
   786	
   787	                        var W = try searchLayer(
   788	                            ctx.centroids,
   789	                            ctx.dim,
   790	                            ctx.adj,
   791	                            cur,
   792	                            q_vec,
   793	                            ctx.ef_c,
   794	                            L,
   795	                            ctx.scratch.visited_gen,
   796	                            ctx.scratch.generation,
   797	                            arena,
   798	                        );
   799	                        defer W.deinit(arena);
   800	
   801	                        const cands_buf = try arena.alloc(Cand, W.count());
   802	                        var idx: usize = 0;
   803	                        while (W.pop()) |c| : (idx += 1) cands_buf[idx] = c;
   804	
   805	                        if (cands_buf.len > 0) {
   806	                            var best_idx: usize = 0;
   807	                            var best_sim: f32 = cands_buf[0].sim;
   808	                            var k: usize = 1;
   809	                            while (k < cands_buf.len) : (k += 1) {
   810	                                if (cands_buf[k].sim > best_sim) {
   811	                                    best_sim = cands_buf[k].sim;
   812	                                    best_idx = k;
   813	                                }
   814	                            }
   815	                            cur = cands_buf[best_idx].node;
   816	                        }
   817	
   818	                        const chosen = try selectHeuristic(
   819	                            ctx.centroids,
   820	                            ctx.dim,
   821	                            q_vec,
   822	                            cands_buf,
   823	                            ctx.m,
   824	                            arena,
   825	                        );
   826	                        chosen_per_layer[L] = chosen;
   827	                    }
   828	
   829	                    ctx.plans[qi] = .{
   830	                        .chosen_per_layer = chosen_per_layer,
   831	                        .top_layer = top_layer,
   832	                        .l_new = l_new,
   833	                    };
   834	                }
   835	            }
   836	        };
   837	
   838	        const ctxs = try gpa.alloc(Ctx, n_threads);
   839	        defer gpa.free(ctxs);
   840	
   841	        const slice_per_thread = (chunk_len + n_threads - 1) / n_threads;
   842	        var spawned: u32 = 0;
   843	        {
   844	            var t: u32 = 0;
   845	            while (t < n_threads) : (t += 1) {
   846	                const lo = t * slice_per_thread;
   847	                const hi = @min((t + 1) * slice_per_thread, chunk_len);
   848	                ctxs[t] = .{
   849	                    .centroids = centroids,
   850	                    .dim = dim,
   851	                    .adj = adj,
   852	                    .levels = levels,
   853	                    .ef_c = ef_c,
   854	                    .m = m,
   855	                    .scratch = &scratches[t],
   856	                    .plans = plans,
   857	                    .q_base = q_base,
   858	                    .lo = lo,
   859	                    .hi = hi,
   860	                    .snap_entry = snap_entry,
   861	                    .snap_max_level = snap_max_level,
   862	                    .err_out = null,
   863	                };
   864	                if (lo >= hi) continue; // empty slice, skip spawn
   865	                threads[spawned] = std.Thread.spawn(.{}, Runner.run, .{&ctxs[t]}) catch {
   866	                    // Join already-spawned and propagate.
   867	                    var j: u32 = 0;
   868	                    while (j < spawned) : (j += 1) threads[j].join();
   869	                    return error.ThreadSpawnFailed;
   870	                };
   871	                spawned += 1;
   872	            }
   873	        }
   874	        {
   875	            var j: u32 = 0;
   876	            while (j < spawned) : (j += 1) threads[j].join();
   877	        }
   878	        for (ctxs) |c| if (c.err_out) |err| return err;
   879	
   880	        // (4) Single-threaded commit pass in q-order.
   881	        const t_c: u64 = if (verbose) nowNs() else 0;
   882	        var ci: u32 = 0;
   883	        while (ci < chunk_len) : (ci += 1) {
   884	            const q_local: u32 = q_base + ci;
   885	            const plan = plans[ci];
   886	            var L_signed: i32 = plan.top_layer;
   887	            while (L_signed >= 0) : (L_signed -= 1) {
   888	                const L: u8 = @intCast(L_signed);
   889	                const chosen = plan.chosen_per_layer[L];
   890	                const cap_q: u32 = if (L == 0) m_max0 else m_max;
   891	
   892	                const q_neighbours = adj.neighbours(q_local, L);
   893	                try q_neighbours.ensureTotalCapacity(gpa, chosen.len);
   894	                for (chosen) |nbr_id| q_neighbours.appendAssumeCapacity(nbr_id);
   895	
   896	                for (chosen) |r_id| {
   897	                    const r_neighbours = adj.neighbours(r_id, L);
   898	                    try r_neighbours.append(gpa, q_local);
   899	                    if (r_neighbours.items.len > cap_q) {
   900	                        try shrinkNeighbours(
   901	                            centroids,
   902	                            dim,
   903	                            r_id,
   904	                            r_neighbours,
   905	                            cap_q,
   906	                            gpa,
   907	                        );
   908	                    }
   909	                }
   910	            }
   911	            if (plan.l_new > max_level) {
   912	                max_level = plan.l_new;
   913	                entry_point = q_local;
   914	            }
   915	        }
   916	        if (verbose) t_connect_ns.* += nowNs() - t_c;
   917	
   918	        // Phase A/B/heuristic timings are aggregated ad-hoc; for the parallel
   919	        // path we only report wall-clock (commit and the parallel section).
   920	        // The commit pass runs serially, so its time is counted accurately.

exec

     1	//! src/index/hnsw.zig — HNSW over centroids (paper §4).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: indexer. Plan: 03-index-pq-hnsw-storage.plan.md.
     4	//! Parallelism plan: post-hackathon-06-hnsw-parallel.plan.md (paper-strict).
     5	//!
     6	//! Paper-strict defaults: `M_hnsw=32`, `efc=1500`, runtime `ef_s = 1.5·κ_c`
     7	//! (computed by `constants.hnswEfSearch`).
     8	//!
     9	//! Reference algorithm: Malkov & Yashunin 2020, "Efficient and robust
    10	//! approximate nearest neighbor search using Hierarchical Navigable Small
    11	//! World graphs" (TPAMI). We follow Algorithms 1–4 with the paper's own
    12	//! parameter names — `M`, `M_max`, `M_max0`, `efConstruction`, `mL`.
    13	//!
    14	//! Distance metric is **similarity** (inner product). Tachiom centroids are
    15	//! L2-normalised, so dot product is cosine, and "closer" means "higher sim".
    16	//! Internal heaps:
    17	//!   - `cands`:   max-heap on similarity → best-first frontier expansion
    18	//!   - `result`:  min-heap on similarity → drop the worst when |result| > ef
    19	//!
    20	//! paper-gap: Tachiom §4 doesn't pin which Malkov variant is used for
    21	//! neighbour selection. We use the heuristic (Algorithm 4) — standard choice
    22	//! for cluster-rich graphs over centroid sets.
    23	//!
    24	//! ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	//! BUILD PARALLELISM — Layer-batched deferred-commit (paper-strict).
    26	//! ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27	//!
    28	//! The doc string on `build()` (below) promises:
    29	//!     "identical (centroids, dim, seed, params) → identical CSR".
    30	//!
    31	//! Per-insert work splits into:
    32	//!   * READ-ONLY phase on `adj`: greedySearchLayer + searchLayer + selectHeuristic
    33	//!     (uses ~95% of insertion CPU; pure reads against the existing graph snapshot).
    34	//!   * MUTATING phase on `adj`: append q→chosen, append chosen[i]→q, and
    35	//!     shrinkNeighbours() when an existing node overflows its cap.
    36	//!
    37	//! Two parallelism approaches considered:
    38	//!
    39	//!   (a) Per-node spinlocks on neighbour lists. Simplest, but **rejected**:
    40	//!       `r_neighbours.append(q)` order becomes non-deterministic across runs
    41	//!       even with same seed (worker scheduling decides which q wins the lock
    42	//!       first), so the serialised CSR diverges between threads=1 and threads=N.
    43	//!       That breaks the paper-strict reproducibility invariant.
    44	//!
    45	//!   (b) Layer-batched deferred-commit. Adopted. The algorithm:
    46	//!         1. Outer loop processes `q` in chunks of CHUNK_SIZE (=256) nodes.
    47	//!         2. Within a chunk, spawn `n_threads` workers; each worker handles a
    48	//!            slice of q values and runs greedy descent + searchLayer +
    49	//!            selectHeuristic against the **read-only** `adj` snapshot from
    50	//!            before the chunk started. Output per-q is a `TempInsertion`:
    51	//!              { q, l_new, top_layer, per-layer chosen[], cur_for_entry_update }.
    52	//!         3. After all workers join, a single-threaded commit pass walks the
    53	//!            TempInsertions in q-order and applies the mutations using the
    54	//!            existing append + shrinkNeighbours code. Because the commit pass
    55	//!            sees q-values in strictly ascending order, the resulting
    56	//!            adjacency lists are bit-identical to the serial path.
    57	//!
    58	//! Worker scratch:
    59	//!   * Each thread needs its own `visited_gen` + `generation` counter (the
    60	//!     serial path used a single shared buffer; per-worker copies are
    61	//!     allocated up-front and freed at chunk end).
    62	//!   * Each thread uses a thread-local arena for searchLayer scratch heaps so
    63	//!     we don't contend on the global allocator.
    64	//!
    65	//! Determinism guarantees:
    66	//!   * `pickLevel` is called once per q from the build's master Rng *before*
    67	//!     worker dispatch, so the level sequence is identical across thread counts.
    68	//!   * Each worker's selectHeuristic is deterministic (sort-by-sim with
    69	//!     stable ties; equal-sim entries kept in candidate-emission order which
    70	//!     itself is deterministic because searchLayer expands the frontier in a
    71	//!     fixed best-first order over a deterministic snapshot).
    72	//!   * The commit pass replays mutations strictly in q-order, so r_neighbours
    73	//!     receive incoming edges in the same order regardless of how many worker
    74	//!     threads computed the chosen[] sets.
    75	//!
    76	//! Read-only-snapshot caveat: a worker handling q' inside a chunk does NOT
    77	//! see edges committed by an earlier q < q' in the same chunk. The chunk
    78	//! size therefore trades parallelism for snapshot freshness — within a chunk
    79	//! every q reads the adj as it stood at chunk start. CHUNK_SIZE=256 keeps
    80	//! recall in line with the serial path (the recall@10 test on 1024 random
    81	//! vectors stays ≥ 0.9 for n_threads ∈ {1, 4, 10}).
    82	//!
    83	//! Determinism scope:
    84	//!   * `n_threads=1` short-circuits the chunked path entirely and uses the
    85	//!     pre-parallel serial code. Bit-identical to today's CSR for all seeds.
    86	//!   * `n_threads>=2` uses the chunked path. The CSR is deterministic across
    87	//!     any thread count ≥ 2 (the determinism test pins n_threads=2 vs
    88	//!     n_threads=10 to byte-equal), but it differs from the serial path
    89	//!     because chunk-internal reads cannot see mid-chunk commits. This is
    90	//!     the standard parallel-HNSW determinism contract.
    91	
    92	const std = @import("std");
    93	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    94	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    95	const vec = @import("../util/vec.zig");
    96	const rng_mod = @import("../util/rng.zig");
    97	
    98	/// Monotonic ns timestamp (Zig 0.16: std.time.Timer is gone behind std.Io).
    99	/// Mirrors src/index/storage.zig:nowNs.
   100	fn nowNs() u64 {
   101	    var ts: std.c.timespec = undefined;
   102	    _ = std.c.clock_gettime(std.c.CLOCK.MONOTONIC, &ts);
   103	    return @as(u64, @intCast(ts.sec)) * 1_000_000_000 + @as(u64, @intCast(ts.nsec));
   104	}
   105	
   106	pub const HnswError = error{
   107	    EmptyCorpus,
   108	    DimMismatch,
   109	    OutBufferTooSmall,
   110	    OutOfMemory,
   111	    /// Bubbled up from std.Thread.spawn in the parallel build path.
   112	    ThreadSpawnFailed,
   113	} || vec.VecError;
   114	
   115	const NodeId = u32;
   116	
   117	/// (similarity, node) pair used in both heaps. Similarity-ordering helpers
   118	/// live below — `lessSim` for a min-heap (worst-first), `greaterSim` for a
   119	/// max-heap (best-first).
   120	const Cand = struct { sim: f32, node: NodeId };
   121	
   122	fn lessSim(_: void, a: Cand, b: Cand) std.math.Order {
   123	    return std.math.order(a.sim, b.sim);
   124	}
   125	fn greaterSim(_: void, a: Cand, b: Cand) std.math.Order {
   126	    return std.math.order(b.sim, a.sim);
   127	}
   128	
   129	const MinHeap = std.PriorityQueue(Cand, void, lessSim);
   130	const MaxHeap = std.PriorityQueue(Cand, void, greaterSim);
   131	
   132	/// Frozen, query-ready HNSW. Adjacency is per-layer CSR for cache locality
   133	/// and zero-copy mmap mapping later (storage.zig wires that up in #14).
   134	///
   135	/// Lifetime: `centroids` is BORROWED — caller (or storage layer) owns the
   136	/// underlying buffer. The graph buffers (`node_levels`, `layer_offsets`,
   137	/// `layer_neighbours`) are heap-owned by this Hnsw and freed in `deinit`.
   138	pub const Hnsw = struct {
   139	    dim: u32,
   140	    n: u32,
   141	    entry_point: NodeId,
   142	    max_level: u8,
   143	    /// Top level each node lives in (0 ≤ node_levels[i] ≤ max_level).
   144	    node_levels: []u8,
   145	    /// `layer_offsets[L]` has length `n + 1`. Neighbours of node `i` at
   146	    /// layer `L` are `layer_neighbours[L][offsets[i] .. offsets[i+1]]`.
   147	    /// A node not present at layer `L` (because its top level < L) has
   148	    /// `offsets[i] == offsets[i+1]` (empty slice).
   149	    layer_offsets: [][]u32,
   150	    layer_neighbours: [][]u32,
   151	    centroids: []const f32,
   152	
   153	    pub fn deinit(self: *Hnsw, gpa: Allocator) void {
   154	        gpa.free(self.node_levels);
   155	        for (self.layer_offsets) |s| gpa.free(s);
   156	        for (self.layer_neighbours) |s| gpa.free(s);
   157	        gpa.free(self.layer_offsets);
   158	        gpa.free(self.layer_neighbours);
   159	        self.* = undefined;
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    inline fn centroid(self: *const Hnsw, id: NodeId) []const f32 {
   163	        const start: usize = @as(usize, id) * @as(usize, self.dim);
   164	        return self.centroids[start .. start + @as(usize, self.dim)];
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    inline fn neighbours(self: *const Hnsw, id: NodeId, layer: u8) []const u32 {
   168	        const off = self.layer_offsets[layer];
   169	        return self.layer_neighbours[layer][off[id] .. off[id + 1]];
   170	    }
   171	};
   172	
   173	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   174	// BUILD
   175	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   176	
   177	/// Per-node, per-layer growable adjacency. Used only during build; we freeze
   178	/// to CSR inside `build` before returning.
   179	const Adj = struct {
   180	    /// Flat, indexed by `node_id * (max_level_so_far + 1) + L`. We grow the
   181	    /// outer dimension as `max_level` grows — see `ensureLevel`.
   182	    rows: std.ArrayList(std.ArrayList(NodeId)),
   183	    n: u32,
   184	    max_level: u8,
   185	    gpa: Allocator,
   186	
   187	    fn init(gpa: Allocator, n: u32) !Adj {
   188	        var rows: std.ArrayList(std.ArrayList(NodeId)) = .empty;
   189	        try rows.ensureTotalCapacity(gpa, @as(usize, n));
   190	        var i: usize = 0;
   191	        while (i < n) : (i += 1) {
   192	            try rows.append(gpa, .empty);
   193	        }
   194	        return .{ .rows = rows, .n = n, .max_level = 0, .gpa = gpa };
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    fn deinit(self: *Adj) void {
   198	        for (self.rows.items) |*r| r.deinit(self.gpa);
   199	        self.rows.deinit(self.gpa);
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    /// Make sure `rows` has slots up to and including `level`. We store
   203	    /// adjacency as `rows[node*levels_per_node + L]`, but levels grow rarely
   204	    /// (geometric drop-off), so we just **reallocate row table** when the
   205	    /// graph's max level grows. Simplicity > speed during build for now.
   206	    fn ensureLevel(self: *Adj, level: u8) !void {
   207	        if (level <= self.max_level) return;
   208	        const new_per: usize = @as(usize, level) + 1;
   209	        const old_per: usize = @as(usize, self.max_level) + 1;
   210	        const new_total: usize = @as(usize, self.n) * new_per;
   211	        const old_total: usize = self.rows.items.len;
   212	        // Append empty ArrayLists, then permute so each node's old slots
   213	        // remain contiguous in their new position. We do an in-place rebuild.
   214	        try self.rows.ensureTotalCapacity(self.gpa, new_total);
   215	        // Step 1: append fresh empty lists for the new tail size.
   216	        while (self.rows.items.len < new_total) {
   217	            try self.rows.append(self.gpa, .empty);
   218	        }
   219	        // Step 2: from the highest node down, move old rows into new layout.
   220	        // Old layout: node n owns rows[n*old_per .. (n+1)*old_per].
   221	        // New layout: node n owns rows[n*new_per .. (n+1)*new_per].
   222	        var node: usize = self.n;
   223	        while (node > 0) {
   224	            node -= 1;
   225	            const old_base = node * old_per;
   226	            const new_base = node * new_per;
   227	            // The new layers above old_per are already empty .empty entries
   228	            // (appended above) — but they may currently sit at the end of
   229	            // rows[]. We need to move our `old_per` legitimate rows into
   230	            // [new_base .. new_base + old_per).
   231	            if (new_base != old_base) {
   232	                var l: usize = old_per;
   233	                while (l > 0) {
   234	                    l -= 1;
   235	                    self.rows.items[new_base + l] = self.rows.items[old_base + l];
   236	                    self.rows.items[old_base + l] = .empty;
   237	                }
   238	            }
   239	            // Higher new levels [old_per .. new_per) for this node should be
   240	            // empty. They currently are .empty (either freshly appended or
   241	            // we just zeroed them above when shifting).
   242	        }
   243	        _ = old_total; // silence unused warning on debug builds
   244	        self.max_level = level;
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    fn neighbours(self: *Adj, node: NodeId, level: u8) *std.ArrayList(NodeId) {
   248	        const per: usize = @as(usize, self.max_level) + 1;
   249	        return &self.rows.items[@as(usize, node) * per + @as(usize, level)];
   250	    }
   251	};
   252

   260	    /// the pre-parallel CSR, all 175 existing tests stay green). `>=2` uses

32://!   * READ-ONLY phase on `adj`: greedySearchLayer + searchLayer + selectHeuristic
34://!   * MUTATING phase on `adj`: append q→chosen, append chosen[i]→q, and
37://! Two parallelism approaches considered:
40://!       `r_neighbours.append(q)` order becomes non-deterministic across runs
42://!       first), so the serialised CSR diverges between threads=1 and threads=N.
45://!   (b) Layer-batched deferred-commit. Adopted. The algorithm:
46://!         1. Outer loop processes `q` in chunks of CHUNK_SIZE (=256) nodes.
47://!         2. Within a chunk, spawn `n_threads` workers; each worker handles a
48://!            slice of q values and runs greedy descent + searchLayer +
50://!            before the chunk started. Output per-q is a `TempInsertion`:
52://!         3. After all workers join, a single-threaded commit pass walks the
54://!            existing append + shrinkNeighbours code. Because the commit pass
59://!   * Each thread needs its own `visited_gen` + `generation` counter (the
61://!     allocated up-front and freed at chunk end).
62://!   * Each thread uses a thread-local arena for searchLayer scratch heaps so
66://!   * `pickLevel` is called once per q from the build's master Rng *before*
67://!     worker dispatch, so the level sequence is identical across thread counts.
68://!   * Each worker's selectHeuristic is deterministic (sort-by-sim with
70://!     itself is deterministic because searchLayer expands the frontier in a
71://!     fixed best-first order over a deterministic snapshot).
72://!   * The commit pass replays mutations strictly in q-order, so r_neighbours
74://!     threads computed the chosen[] sets.
76://! Read-only-snapshot caveat: a worker handling q' inside a chunk does NOT
77://! see edges committed by an earlier q < q' in the same chunk. The chunk
78://! size therefore trades parallelism for snapshot freshness — within a chunk
79://! every q reads the adj as it stood at chunk start. CHUNK_SIZE=256 keeps
80://! recall in line with the serial path (the recall@10 test on 1024 random
81://! vectors stays ≥ 0.9 for n_threads ∈ {1, 4, 10}).
84://!   * `n_threads=1` short-circuits the chunked path entirely and uses the
85://!     pre-parallel serial code. Bit-identical to today's CSR for all seeds.
86://!   * `n_threads>=2` uses the chunked path. The CSR is deterministic across
87://!     any thread count ≥ 2 (the determinism test pins n_threads=2 vs
88://!     n_threads=10 to byte-equal), but it differs from the serial path
89://!     because chunk-internal reads cannot see mid-chunk commits. This is
90://!     the standard parallel-HNSW determinism contract.

   935	/// Geometric layer assignment, Malkov §4.1 Eq. 7.
   936	fn pickLevel(rng: *rng_mod.Rng, m_l: f32) u8 {
   937	    // u ∈ (0, 1]. nextFloat returns [0,1) so flip 1 - u to dodge log(0).
   938	    const u: f32 = 1.0 - rng.nextFloat();
   939	    const lvl_f: f32 = @floor(-@log(u) * m_l);
   940	    if (lvl_f < 0.0) return 0;
   941	    if (lvl_f > 31.0) return 31; // u8 fits; in practice <10 for any realistic n
   942	    return @intFromFloat(lvl_f);
   943	}
   944	
   945	inline fn sliceVec(buf: []const f32, dim: u32, id: NodeId) []const f32 {
   946	    const start: usize = @as(usize, id) * @as(usize, dim);
   947	    return buf[start .. start + @as(usize, dim)];
   948	}
   949	
   950	fn similarity(centroids: []const f32, dim: u32, a: []const f32, b_id: NodeId) f32 {
   951	    const b = sliceVec(centroids, dim, b_id);
   952	    return vec.dot(a, b) catch unreachable; // we validated dim at entry
   953	}
   954	
   955	/// Greedy 1-step descent within a single layer. Returns the local maximum
   956	/// reachable from `entry`. Deterministic: ties resolve to lower node id
   957	/// because we only switch when strictly better.
   958	fn greedySearchLayer(
   959	    centroids: []const f32,
   960	    dim: u32,
   961	    adj: *Adj,
   962	    entry: NodeId,
   963	    q_vec: []const f32,
   964	    layer: u8,
   965	) NodeId {
   966	    var cur: NodeId = entry;
   967	    var cur_sim: f32 = similarity(centroids, dim, q_vec, cur);
   968	    while (true) {
   969	        var improved = false;
   970	        const list = adj.neighbours(cur, layer);
   971	        for (list.items) |e| {
   972	            const s = similarity(centroids, dim, q_vec, e);
   973	            if (s > cur_sim) {
   974	                cur_sim = s;
   975	                cur = e;
   976	                improved = true;
   977	            }
   978	        }
   979	        if (!improved) break;
   980	    }
   981	    return cur;
   982	}
   983	
   984	/// Malkov Algorithm 2 — beam search at a single layer. Returns a max-heap
   985	/// of size up to `ef`; caller must free.
   986	fn searchLayer(
   987	    centroids: []const f32,
   988	    dim: u32,
   989	    adj: *Adj,
   990	    entry: NodeId,
   991	    q_vec: []const f32,
   992	    ef: u32,
   993	    layer: u8,
   994	    visited_gen: []u32,
   995	    generation: u32,
   996	    gpa: Allocator,
   997	) HnswError!MaxHeap {
   998	    var cands: MaxHeap = .empty;
   999	    errdefer cands.deinit(gpa);
  1000	    var W: MinHeap = .empty;
  1001	    errdefer W.deinit(gpa);
  1002	
  1003	    const entry_sim = similarity(centroids, dim, q_vec, entry);
  1004	    visited_gen[entry] = generation;
  1005	    try cands.push(gpa, .{ .sim = entry_sim, .node = entry });
  1006	    try W.push(gpa, .{ .sim = entry_sim, .node = entry });
  1007	
  1008	    while (cands.pop()) |c| {
  1009	        // peek worst-of-W to prune
  1010	        const worst_w = W.peek() orelse break;
  1011	        if (c.sim < worst_w.sim and W.count() >= ef) {
  1012	            break;
  1013	        }
  1014	        const list = adj.neighbours(c.node, layer);
  1015	        for (list.items) |e| {
  1016	            if (visited_gen[e] == generation) continue;
  1017	            visited_gen[e] = generation;
  1018	            const s = similarity(centroids, dim, q_vec, e);
  1019	            const w_worst = W.peek();
  1020	            if (W.count() < ef or (w_worst != null and s > w_worst.?.sim)) {
  1021	                try cands.push(gpa, .{ .sim = s, .node = e });
  1022	                try W.push(gpa, .{ .sim = s, .node = e });
  1023	                if (W.count() > ef) _ = W.pop();
  1024	            }
  1025	        }
  1026	    }
  1027	
  1028	    cands.deinit(gpa);
  1029	
  1030	    // Convert W (min-heap) → max-heap on similarity for the caller. We do
  1031	    // this by draining and re-pushing — cheap (≤ ef items). W still holds
  1032	    // an allocated buffer after the drain so we must free it explicitly.
  1033	    var out: MaxHeap = .empty;
  1034	    errdefer out.deinit(gpa);
  1035	    while (W.pop()) |c| try out.push(gpa, c);
  1036	    W.deinit(gpa);
  1037	    return out;
  1038	}
  1039	
  1040	/// Malkov Algorithm 4 — heuristic neighbour selection. Pick from `cands`
  1041	/// (already in any order) the M closest-to-q nodes that are *also* closer
  1042	/// to q than to any already-chosen neighbour. Returned slice is heap-owned.
  1043	fn selectHeuristic(
  1044	    centroids: []const f32,
  1045	    dim: u32,
  1046	    q_vec: []const f32,
  1047	    cands: []const Cand,
  1048	    m: u32,
  1049	    gpa: Allocator,
  1050	) HnswError![]u32 {
  1051	    _ = q_vec; // candidates already carry sim(q, cand); no extra recomputation needed
  1052	    // Sort cands descending by similarity. Avoid mutating caller's slice.
  1053	    const sorted = try gpa.dupe(Cand, cands);
  1054	    defer gpa.free(sorted);
  1055	    std.mem.sort(Cand, sorted, {}, candDescSim);
  1056	
  1057	    var out: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
  1058	    errdefer out.deinit(gpa);
  1059	    try out.ensureTotalCapacity(gpa, m);
  1060	
  1061	    for (sorted) |cand| {
  1062	        if (out.items.len >= m) break;
  1063	        // Keep cand iff it's closer to q than to every already-chosen r.
  1064	        var keep = true;
  1065	        const cand_vec = sliceVec(centroids, dim, cand.node);
  1066	        for (out.items) |r_id| {
  1067	            const r_vec = sliceVec(centroids, dim, r_id);
  1068	            const sim_cr = vec.dot(cand_vec, r_vec) catch unreachable;
  1069	            if (sim_cr > cand.sim) {
  1070	                keep = false;
  1071	                break;
  1072	            }
  1073	        }
  1074	        if (keep) try out.append(gpa, cand.node);
  1075	    }
  1076	
  1077	    return out.toOwnedSlice(gpa);
  1078	}
  1079	
  1080	fn candDescSim(_: void, a: Cand, b: Cand) bool {
  1081	    return a.sim > b.sim;
  1082	}
  1083	
  1084	/// When a node's adjacency overflows `cap`, prune via the heuristic.
  1085	fn shrinkNeighbours(
  1086	    centroids: []const f32,
  1087	    dim: u32,
  1088	    node: NodeId,
  1089	    list: *std.ArrayList(NodeId),
  1090	    cap: u32,
  1091	    gpa: Allocator,
  1092	) HnswError!void {
  1093	    const node_vec = sliceVec(centroids, dim, node);
  1094	    var cands = try gpa.alloc(Cand, list.items.len);
  1095	    defer gpa.free(cands);
  1096	    for (list.items, 0..) |id, i| {
  1097	        const s = vec.dot(node_vec, sliceVec(centroids, dim, id)) catch unreachable;
  1098	        cands[i] = .{ .sim = s, .node = id };
  1099	    }
  1100	    const kept = try selectHeuristic(centroids, dim, node_vec, cands, cap, gpa);
  1101	    defer gpa.free(kept);
  1102	    list.clearRetainingCapacity();
  1103	    try list.appendSlice(gpa, kept);
  1104	}
  1105	
  1106	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1107	// SEARCH
  1108	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1109	
  1110	/// Search for the top-`k` most similar centroid IDs to `query`, with ef-beam
  1111	/// width `ef` at layer 0. Returns the count actually written (≤ `out.len`).
  1112	/// Output is sorted by descending similarity.
  1113	pub fn search(
  1114	    self: *const Hnsw,
  1115	    query: []const f32,
  1116	    k: u32,
  1117	    ef: u32,
  1118	    out: []NodeId,
  1119	    gpa: Allocator,
  1120	) HnswError!u32 {
  1121	    if (query.len != @as(usize, self.dim)) return error.DimMismatch;
  1122	    if (out.len < k) return error.OutBufferTooSmall;
  1123	    if (k == 0) return 0;
  1124	
  1125	    const ef_eff: u32 = @max(ef, k);
  1126	
  1127	    var cur: NodeId = self.entry_point;
  1128	    if (self.max_level > 0) {
  1129	        var L: u8 = self.max_level;
  1130	        while (L > 0) : (L -= 1) {
  1131	            cur = greedySearchLayerCsr(self, cur, query, L);
  1132	        }
  1133	    }
  1134	
  1135	    const visited_gen = try gpa.alloc(u32, self.n);
  1136	    defer gpa.free(visited_gen);
  1137	    @memset(visited_gen, 0);
  1138	
  1139	    var W = try searchLayerCsr(self, cur, query, ef_eff, 0, visited_gen, 1, gpa);
  1140	    defer W.deinit(gpa);
  1141	
  1142	    // W is max-heap on sim; drain to get descending-sim order.
  1143	    const want: u32 = @intCast(@min(@as(usize, k), W.count()));
  1144	    var written: u32 = 0;
  1145	    while (written < want) : (written += 1) {
  1146	        const c = W.pop() orelse break;
  1147	        out[written] = c.node;
  1148	    }
  1149	    return written;
  1150	}
  1151	
  1152	fn greedySearchLayerCsr(
  1153	    self: *const Hnsw,
  1154	    entry: NodeId,
  1155	    q_vec: []const f32,
  1156	    layer: u8,
  1157	) NodeId {
  1158	    var cur: NodeId = entry;
  1159	    var cur_sim: f32 = vec.dot(q_vec, self.centroid(cur)) catch unreachable;
  1160	    while (true) {
  1161	        var improved = false;
  1162	        for (self.neighbours(cur, layer)) |e| {
  1163	            const s = vec.dot(q_vec, self.centroid(e)) catch unreachable;
  1164	            if (s > cur_sim) {
  1165	                cur_sim = s;
  1166	                cur = e;
  1167	                improved = true;
  1168	            }
  1169	        }
  1170	        if (!improved) break;
  1171	    }
  1172	    return cur;
  1173	}
  1174	
  1175	fn searchLayerCsr(
  1176	    self: *const Hnsw,
  1177	    entry: NodeId,
  1178	    q_vec: []const f32,
  1179	    ef: u32,
  1180	    layer: u8,
  1181	    visited_gen: []u32,
  1182	    generation: u32,
  1183	    gpa: Allocator,
  1184	) HnswError!MaxHeap {
  1185	    var cands: MaxHeap = .empty;
  1186	    errdefer cands.deinit(gpa);
  1187	    var W: MinHeap = .empty;
  1188	    errdefer W.deinit(gpa);
  1189	
  1190	    const entry_sim = vec.dot(q_vec, self.centroid(entry)) catch unreachable;
  1191	    visited_gen[entry] = generation;
  1192	    try cands.push(gpa, .{ .sim = entry_sim, .node = entry });
  1193	    try W.push(gpa, .{ .sim = entry_sim, .node = entry });
  1194	
  1195	    while (cands.pop()) |c| {
  1196	        const w_worst = W.peek() orelse break;
  1197	        if (c.sim < w_worst.sim and W.count() >= ef) break;
  1198	
  1199	        for (self.neighbours(c.node, layer)) |e| {
  1200	            if (visited_gen[e] == generation) continue;
  1201	            visited_gen[e] = generation;
  1202	            const s = vec.dot(q_vec, self.centroid(e)) catch unreachable;
  1203	            const w_now = W.peek();
  1204	            if (W.count() < ef or (w_now != null and s > w_now.?.sim)) {
  1205	                try cands.push(gpa, .{ .sim = s, .node = e });
  1206	                try W.push(gpa, .{ .sim = s, .node = e });
  1207	                if (W.count() > ef) _ = W.pop();
  1208	            }
  1209	        }
  1210	    }
  1211	    cands.deinit(gpa);
  1212	
  1213	    // Convert min-heap to max-heap on sim. W still owns its backing buffer
  1214	    // after the drain loop, so we free it explicitly.
  1215	    var out: MaxHeap = .empty;
  1216	    errdefer out.deinit(gpa);
  1217	    while (W.pop()) |c| try out.push(gpa, c);
  1218	    W.deinit(gpa);
  1219	    return out;
  1220	}

  1223	// TESTS
  1224	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

     1	//! src/util/vec.zig — SIMD-vectorised f32 operations.
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: primitives-engineer.
     4	//! Underpins every distance/similarity computation in TAC clustering (§3),
     5	//! HNSW edge selection (§4), and the gather/refine query phases (§5).
     6	//!
     7	//! All functions operate on `f32` slices. SIMD width is derived from
     8	//! `std.simd.suggestVectorLength(f32)`, with a scalar tail for inputs whose
     9	//! length isn't a multiple of the lane count. Inputs are validated at the
    10	//! boundary; mismatched lengths return errors rather than panicking.
    11	
    12	const std = @import("std");
    13	
    14	/// Hardware-suggested SIMD lane count for f32. Falls back to 4 on targets
    15	/// where suggestVectorLength returns null (rare but possible for unknown CPUs).
    16	pub const lane_count: usize = std.simd.suggestVectorLength(f32) orelse 4;
    17	
    18	const Vec = @Vector(lane_count, f32);
    19	
    20	pub const VecError = error{
    21	    LengthMismatch,
    22	    EmptyInput,
    23	};
    24	
    25	/// Inner product ⟨a, b⟩.
    26	///
    27	/// paper §3.1: token similarities are dot products of L2-normalised f32
    28	/// embeddings (cosine), used everywhere from k-means assignment to the
    29	/// gather-phase ⟨q_i, c_j⟩ accumulator.
    30	///
    31	/// plan-11: dispatcher for the four dims that actually appear in this repo —
    32	/// 2 and 4 (PQ subspaces, since dim ∈ {64, 128} and PQ_M=32) and 64/128 (the
    33	/// jina-colbert-v2-64 and ColBERTv2.0 token embeddings). Marked `inline` so
    34	/// the caller's `a.len` propagates through the switch and LLVM DCEs every
    35	/// other arm; without `inline` the runtime branch on `a.len` was a net loss
    36	/// in the kmeans-assign hot loop (see plan-11 microbench).
    37	pub inline fn dot(a: []const f32, b: []const f32) VecError!f32 {
    38	    if (a.len != b.len) return error.LengthMismatch;
    39	    // dim=2 is hand-inlined here rather than going through `dotComptime(2,...)`:
    40	    // the indirection occasionally blocks LLVM from constant-folding the slice
    41	    // length all the way to two scalar mul-adds. Hand-inlining is unambiguous.
    42	    return switch (a.len) {
    43	        2 => a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1],
    44	        4 => dotComptime(4, a, b),
    45	        64 => dotComptime(64, a, b),
    46	        128 => dotComptime(128, a, b),
    47	        else => dotGeneric(a, b),
    48	    };
    49	}
    50	
    51	/// Comptime-specialized dot product. `dim` is known at compile time so the
    52	/// reduction loop fully unrolls, the tail branch disappears, and LLVM is free
    53	/// to schedule the lanes across multiple FMA pipes.
    54	///
    55	/// Private — reachable from the `dot` dispatcher (which has already validated
    56	/// `a.len == b.len == dim`) and from same-file parity tests. Boundary check
    57	/// is `std.debug.assert`, which is a no-op in ReleaseFast; making this
    58	/// non-`pub` keeps that contract enforceable since no out-of-file caller
    59	/// can pass mismatched lengths.
    60	inline fn dotComptime(comptime dim: u32, a: []const f32, b: []const f32) f32 {
    61	    std.debug.assert(a.len == dim and b.len == dim);
    62	
    63	    if (dim == 0) return 0.0;
    64	
    65	    // dim ≤ 4 → inline scalar unroll. Padding into a 4-wide @Vector adds two
    66	    // tiny stack stores + a reduce that's strictly slower than a flat scalar
    67	    // chain on this size class (the PQ subspace path).
    68	    if (dim <= 4) {
    69	        var s: f32 = 0.0;
    70	        inline for (0..dim) |i| s += a[i] * b[i];
    71	        return s;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    const lanes: comptime_int = comptime laneWidthFor(dim);
    75	    const V = @Vector(lanes, f32);
    76	
    77	    // dim > lanes: comptime-unrolled lane loop over @Vector(4) chunks.
    78	    comptime std.debug.assert(dim % lanes == 0);
    79	    const n_chunks: comptime_int = dim / lanes;
    80	    var acc: V = @splat(0.0);
    81	    inline for (0..n_chunks) |c| {
    82	        const off = c * lanes;
    83	        const va: V = a[off..][0..lanes].*;
    84	        const vb: V = b[off..][0..lanes].*;
    85	        acc += va * vb;
    86	    }
    87	    return @reduce(.Add, acc);
    88	}
    89	
    90	/// Generic runtime-`lane_count` dot product for unspecialized dims.
    91	inline fn dotGeneric(a: []const f32, b: []const f32) f32 {
    92	    const n = a.len;
    93	    var acc: Vec = @splat(0.0);
    94	    var i: usize = 0;
    95	    while (i + lane_count <= n) : (i += lane_count) {
    96	        const va: Vec = a[i..][0..lane_count].*;
    97	        const vb: Vec = b[i..][0..lane_count].*;
    98	        acc += va * vb;
    99	    }
   100	    var sum: f32 = @reduce(.Add, acc);
   101	    while (i < n) : (i += 1) sum += a[i] * b[i];
   102	    return sum;
   103	}
   104	
   105	/// Squared Euclidean distance ‖a − b‖².
   106	///
   107	/// paper §3.1: the spread `s_j = (1/n_j)·Σ ‖t_{j,i} − t̄_j‖²` is a sum of
   108	/// l2sq terms, and Lloyd's-step assignment minimises the same quantity.
   109	pub inline fn l2sq(a: []const f32, b: []const f32) VecError!f32 {
   110	    if (a.len != b.len) return error.LengthMismatch;
   111	    return switch (a.len) {
   112	        // dim=2 directly inline — same plan-11 reasoning as `dot`. PQ encode
   113	        // hits this path on every (subspace × centroid × token) triple.
   114	        2 => blk: {
   115	            const d0 = a[0] - b[0];
   116	            const d1 = a[1] - b[1];
   117	            break :blk d0 * d0 + d1 * d1;
   118	        },
   119	        4 => l2sqComptime(4, a, b),
   120	        64 => l2sqComptime(64, a, b),
   121	        128 => l2sqComptime(128, a, b),
   122	        else => l2sqGeneric(a, b),
   123	    };
   124	}
   125	
   126	/// Comptime-specialized squared L2 distance. Private; see `dotComptime`
   127	/// for the register/unroll strategy and the rationale for non-`pub`.
   128	inline fn l2sqComptime(comptime dim: u32, a: []const f32, b: []const f32) f32 {
   129	    std.debug.assert(a.len == dim and b.len == dim);
   130	
   131	    if (dim == 0) return 0.0;
   132	
   133	    if (dim <= 4) {
   134	        var s: f32 = 0.0;
   135	        inline for (0..dim) |i| {
   136	            const d = a[i] - b[i];
   137	            s += d * d;
   138	        }
   139	        return s;
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    const lanes: comptime_int = comptime laneWidthFor(dim);
   143	    const V = @Vector(lanes, f32);
   144	
   145	    comptime std.debug.assert(dim % lanes == 0);
   146	    const n_chunks: comptime_int = dim / lanes;
   147	    var acc: V = @splat(0.0);
   148	    inline for (0..n_chunks) |c| {
   149	        const off = c * lanes;
   150	        const va: V = a[off..][0..lanes].*;
   151	        const vb: V = b[off..][0..lanes].*;
   152	        const d = va - vb;
   153	        acc += d * d;
   154	    }
   155	    return @reduce(.Add, acc);
   156	}
   157	
   158	/// Generic runtime-`lane_count` l2sq for unspecialized dims.
   159	inline fn l2sqGeneric(a: []const f32, b: []const f32) f32 {
   160	    const n = a.len;
   161	    var acc: Vec = @splat(0.0);
   162	    var i: usize = 0;
   163	    while (i + lane_count <= n) : (i += lane_count) {
   164	        const va: Vec = a[i..][0..lane_count].*;
   165	        const vb: Vec = b[i..][0..lane_count].*;
   166	        const diff = va - vb;
   167	        acc += diff * diff;
   168	    }
   169	    var sum: f32 = @reduce(.Add, acc);
   170	    while (i < n) : (i += 1) {
   171	        const d = a[i] - b[i];
   172	        sum += d * d;
   173	    }
   174	    return sum;
   175	}
   176	
   177	/// L2-normalise `v` in place. Zero-length vectors are an error (callers should
   178	/// have at least one element); the all-zero vector is left untouched (no unit
   179	/// direction exists).
   180	///
   181	/// paper convention: ColBERT/Tachiom assume unit-norm token embeddings so
   182	/// cosine collapses to dot product.
   183	pub inline fn normalizeInPlace(v: []f32) VecError!void {
   184	    if (v.len == 0) return error.EmptyInput;
   185	    switch (v.len) {
   186	        64 => normalizeComptime(64, v),
   187	        128 => normalizeComptime(128, v),
   188	        else => normalizeGeneric(v),
   189	    }
   190	}
   191	
   192	/// Comptime-specialized in-place normalize. Private; see `dotComptime`.
   193	inline fn normalizeComptime(comptime dim: u32, v: []f32) void {
   194	    std.debug.assert(v.len == dim);
   195	    if (dim == 0) return;
   196	
   197	    var norm_sq: f32 = 0.0;
   198	
   199	    if (dim <= 4) {
   200	        inline for (0..dim) |i| norm_sq += v[i] * v[i];
   201	    } else {
   202	        const lanes: comptime_int = comptime laneWidthFor(dim);
   203	        const V = @Vector(lanes, f32);
   204	        comptime std.debug.assert(dim % lanes == 0);
   205	        const n_chunks: comptime_int = dim / lanes;
   206	        var acc: V = @splat(0.0);
   207	        inline for (0..n_chunks) |c| {
   208	            const off = c * lanes;
   209	            const x: V = v[off..][0..lanes].*;
   210	            acc += x * x;
   211	        }
   212	        norm_sq = @reduce(.Add, acc);
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    if (norm_sq == 0.0) return;
   216	    const inv_norm: f32 = 1.0 / @sqrt(norm_sq);
   217	
   218	    if (dim <= 4) {
   219	        inline for (0..dim) |i| v[i] *= inv_norm;
   220	    } else {
   221	        const lanes: comptime_int = comptime laneWidthFor(dim);
   222	        const V = @Vector(lanes, f32);
   223	        const splat_inv: V = @splat(inv_norm);
   224	        const n_chunks: comptime_int = dim / lanes;
   225	        inline for (0..n_chunks) |c| {
   226	            const off = c * lanes;
   227	            const x: V = v[off..][0..lanes].*;
   228	            v[off..][0..lanes].* = x * splat_inv;
   229	        }
   230	    }
   231	}
   232	
   233	/// Generic runtime-`lane_count` normalize for unspecialized dims.
   234	inline fn normalizeGeneric(v: []f32) void {
   235	    var acc: Vec = @splat(0.0);
   236	    var i: usize = 0;
   237	    while (i + lane_count <= v.len) : (i += lane_count) {
   238	        const x: Vec = v[i..][0..lane_count].*;
   239	        acc += x * x;
   240	    }
