   256	/// Pick a SIMD lane width for a comptime-known `dim`.
   257	///
   258	/// On Apple Silicon NEON is 128-bit (4×f32). We always pick 4 — empirically
   259	/// (plan-11 microbench) wider @Vector(16) accumulators lower to 4 stacked
   260	/// NEON regs and end up *slower* than a comptime-unrolled loop over a single
   261	/// 4-wide register. The win from comptime specialization is the unrolled,
   262	/// branch-free reduction, not a wider accumulator.
   263	fn laneWidthFor(comptime dim: u32) comptime_int {
   264	    _ = dim;
   265	    return 4;
   266	}
   267	
   268	/// Index of the smallest element. Empty input is an error rather than an
   269	/// `unreachable` because callers (e.g. assignment loops) should validate
   270	/// they have at least one centroid before asking.
   271	pub fn argmin(values: []const f32) VecError!usize {
   272	    if (values.len == 0) return error.EmptyInput;
   273	    var best: usize = 0;
   274	    var best_v: f32 = values[0];
   275	    var i: usize = 1;
   276	    while (i < values.len) : (i += 1) {
   277	        if (values[i] < best_v) {
   278	            best_v = values[i];
   279	            best = i;
   280	        }
   281	    }
   282	    return best;
   283	}
   284	
   285	/// Index of the largest element. Symmetric to `argmin`; useful for picking
   286	/// the highest-similarity centroid in the gather phase.
   287	pub fn argmax(values: []const f32) VecError!usize {
   288	    if (values.len == 0) return error.EmptyInput;
   289	    var best: usize = 0;
   290	    var best_v: f32 = values[0];
   291	    var i: usize = 1;
   292	    while (i < values.len) : (i += 1) {
   293	        if (values[i] > best_v) {
   294	            best_v = values[i];
   295	            best = i;
   296	        }
   297	    }
   298	    return best;
   299	}
   300	
   301	/// Compute `dst[i] = ‖query − points[i*dim..(i+1)*dim]‖²` for every row.
   302	/// `points` is a flat row-major matrix of length `n*dim`. Used by k-means
   303	/// assignment to evaluate one point against every centroid in a single pass.
   304	pub fn l2sqBatch(
   305	    query: []const f32,
   306	    points: []const f32,
   307	    dim: usize,
   308	    dst: []f32,
   309	) VecError!void {
   310	    if (query.len != dim) return error.LengthMismatch;
   311	    if (points.len != dst.len * dim) return error.LengthMismatch;
   312	    var i: usize = 0;
   313	    while (i < dst.len) : (i += 1) {
   314	        dst[i] = try l2sq(query, points[i * dim ..][0..dim]);
   315	    }
   316	}
   317	
   318	/// Batched in-place L2 normalise of `n` row-major vectors of `dim` floats.
   319	pub fn normalizeRowsInPlace(rows: []f32, dim: usize) VecError!void {
   320	    if (dim == 0) return error.EmptyInput;
   321	    if (rows.len % dim != 0) return error.LengthMismatch;
   322	    var i: usize = 0;
   323	    while (i < rows.len) : (i += dim) {
   324	        try normalizeInPlace(rows[i..][0..dim]);
   325	    }
   326	}

   334	test "dot: 3-d hand-checked" {
   335	    const a = [_]f32{ 1.0, 2.0, 3.0 };
   336	    const b = [_]f32{ 4.0, -5.0, 6.0 };
   337	    // 1*4 + 2*(-5) + 3*6 = 4 - 10 + 18 = 12
   338	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(f32, 12.0), try dot(&a, &b));
   339	}

   341	test "dot: longer than one SIMD lane" {
   342	    var a: [17]f32 = undefined;
   343	    var b: [17]f32 = undefined;
   344	    var expected: f32 = 0.0;
   345	    for (0..a.len) |i| {
   346	        a[i] = @floatFromInt(i + 1);
   347	        b[i] = 2.0;
   348	        expected += a[i] * b[i];
   349	    }
   350	    // 2*(1+2+...+17) = 2*153 = 306
   351	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(f32, 306.0), try dot(&a, &b));
   352	    try testing.expectEqual(expected, try dot(&a, &b));
   353	}

   468	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   469	// plan-11 — comptime-specialization parity tests.
   470	//
   471	// For each specialized dim ∈ {2, 4, 64, 128} verify the comptime kernel agrees
   472	// with a scalar reference within 1e-5 absolute. We test both hand-checkable
   473	// inputs and generated patterns (ramp + permutation).
   474	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   475	
   476	fn scalarDotRef(a: []const f32, b: []const f32) f32 {
   477	    var s: f32 = 0.0;
   478	    for (a, b) |x, y| s += x * y;
   479	    return s;
   480	}
   481	
   482	fn scalarL2sqRef(a: []const f32, b: []const f32) f32 {
   483	    var s: f32 = 0.0;
   484	    for (a, b) |x, y| {
   485	        const d = x - y;
   486	        s += d * d;
   487	    }
   488	    return s;
   489	}
   490	
   491	fn scalarNormSqRef(v: []const f32) f32 {
   492	    var s: f32 = 0.0;
   493	    for (v) |x| s += x * x;
   494	    return s;
   495	}
   496	
   497	test "plan-11 parity: dot dim=2 hand-checked" {
   498	    const a = [_]f32{ 1.0, 2.0 };
   499	    const b = [_]f32{ 3.0, 4.0 };
   500	    // 1*3 + 2*4 = 11
   501	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 11.0), try dot(&a, &b), 1e-5);
   502	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(scalarDotRef(&a, &b), dotComptime(2, &a, &b), 1e-5);
   503	}
   504	
   505	test "plan-11 parity: dot dim=4 hand-checked" {
   506	    const a = [_]f32{ 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0 };
   507	    const b = [_]f32{ 5.0, 6.0, 7.0, 8.0 };
   508	    // 5 + 12 + 21 + 32 = 70
   509	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 70.0), try dot(&a, &b), 1e-5);
   510	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(scalarDotRef(&a, &b), dotComptime(4, &a, &b), 1e-5);
   511	}

   514	    const a = [_]f32{ 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0 };
   515	    const b = [_]f32{ 5.0, 6.0, 7.0, 8.0 };

   517	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 64.0), try l2sq(&a, &b), 1e-5);
   518	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(scalarL2sqRef(&a, &b), l2sqComptime(4, &a, &b), 1e-5);
   519	}

   552	test "plan-11 parity: dot dim=128 ramp + permutation" {
   553	    var a: [128]f32 = undefined;
   554	    var b: [128]f32 = undefined;
   555	    for (0..128) |i| {
   556	        a[i] = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(i)) * 0.005 - 0.3;
   557	        b[i] = @as(f32, @floatFromInt((i * 13 + 1) % 128)) * 0.01;
   558	    }
   559	    const ref = scalarDotRef(&a, &b);
   560	    const got = try dot(&a, &b);
   561	    // dim=128 reduces 32 chunks of @Vector(4,f32) — the tree reduction order
   562	    // differs from the scalar reference left-fold, so the LSBs drift. 1e-5
   563	    // is tight enough to catch real algorithmic bugs (the result is O(1));
   564	    // anything below ~3e-6 starts failing on legitimate FMA reordering.

   568	}

     1	//! src/index/storage.zig — on-disk index format (paper §4 layout).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: indexer. Plan: 03-index-pq-hnsw-storage.plan.md.
     4	//!
     5	//! Per paper §4 doc layout (Pass 1 streams centroid IDs, Pass 2 PQ codes):
     6	//!
     7	//!   doc d:  [c_1 .. c_{n_d} : u32  |  PQ_{1,1} .. PQ_{n_d, M} : u8]
     8	//!
     9	//! On-disk format (single file, mmap-friendly, little-endian, every section
    10	//! starts on an 8-byte boundary). Section order:
    11	//!
    12	//!   1. Header (104 bytes, padded to 128).
    13	//!   2. Centroids       — `kappa * dim` f32, row-major.
    14	//!   3. HNSW graph      — serialised CSR per layer (see writeHnsw).
    15	//!   4. PQ codebooks    — `PQ_M * 256 * sub_dim` f32, row-major.
    16	//!   5. Inverted lists  — CSR (`offsets [kappa+1]u64`, `payload`).
    17	//!   6. Per-doc layouts — for each doc d: n_d u32, centroid_ids u32[n_d],
    18	//!                        pq_codes u8[n_d * PQ_M], pad to 8B.
    19	//!                        Preceded by `doc_index [n_docs+1]u64` byte-offsets
    20	//!                        into this section.
    21	//!   7. Doc token offsets — `[n_docs+1]u64`. Mirrors `TokenDump.doc_offsets`
    22	//!                          shape: `[lo, hi)` global token range per doc, so
    23	//!                          refine (paper §5.3) can slice `residual_norms`
    24	//!                          for a candidate in O(1).
    25	//!   8. Residual norms  — `n_tokens` f32 (paper §4 "homogeneous compression").
    26	//!   9. Footer          — crc32 u32 of bytes [0 .. footer_off), pad[4]u8.
    27	//!
    28	//! Validation on parse:
    29	//!   - magic == INDEX_MAGIC                   → else error.InvalidIndex
    30	//!   - version == INDEX_VERSION               → else error.UnsupportedVersion
    31	//!   - sub_dim * pq_M == dim                  → else error.InvalidIndex
    32	//!   - all *_off in bounds and monotonic      → else error.InvalidIndex
    33	//!   - footer crc32 matches recomputed value  → else error.IndexCorrupt
    34	
    35	const std = @import("std");
    36	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    37	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    38	const pq_mod = @import("pq.zig");
    39	const hnsw_mod = @import("hnsw.zig");
    40	const inverted_list = @import("inverted_list.zig");
    41	
    42	pub const StorageError = error{
    43	    InvalidIndex,
    44	    UnsupportedVersion,
    45	    IndexCorrupt,
    46	    BufferTooSmall,
    47	} || Allocator.Error;

    49	/// Header struct laid out to match the on-disk format. `extern` ensures
    50	/// field order and no implicit padding beyond what we declare; the explicit
    51	/// `_pad0` field pins the on-the-wire layout byte-for-byte.
    52	pub const Header = extern struct {
    53	    magic: [8]u8,
    54	    version: u32,
    55	    dim: u32,
    56	    kappa: u32,
    57	    pq_M: u32,
    58	    pq_bits: u8,
    59	    _pad0: [3]u8,
    60	    sub_dim: u32,
    61	    n_docs: u64,
    62	    n_tokens: u64,
    63	    centroids_off: u64,
    64	    hnsw_off: u64,
    65	    pq_off: u64,
    66	    ilist_off: u64,
    67	    doc_off: u64,
    68	    doc_tok_off: u64,
    69	    norms_off: u64,
    70	    footer_off: u64,
    71	};
    72	
    73	/// On-disk header region size — Header struct is 104 bytes; we pad to 128
    74	/// so the first content section is 16-byte aligned (cache-line friendly,
    75	/// SIMD friendly). Pinned in code so writer + parser agree.
    76	pub const header_region_size: usize = 128;
    77	
    78	comptime {
    79	    std.debug.assert(@sizeOf(Header) == 112);
    80	    std.debug.assert(header_region_size >= @sizeOf(Header));
    81	    std.debug.assert(header_region_size % 8 == 0);
    82	}

    84	/// Parsed view over an in-memory image. Slices alias `bytes` — the caller
    85	/// must keep `bytes` alive for the lifetime of the `Index`. No allocations
    86	/// beyond what's needed to materialise PQ / HNSW / InvertedLists structs.
    87	pub const Index = struct {
    88	    header: Header,
    89	    centroids: []const f32,
    90	    pq: pq_mod.PQ,
    91	    hnsw: hnsw_mod.Hnsw,
    92	    ilists: inverted_list.InvertedLists,
    93	    /// `n_docs+1` byte offsets into `doc_payload` (per-doc start).
    94	    doc_index: []const u64,
    95	    doc_payload: []const u8,
    96	    /// `n_docs+1` global token offsets — `[doc_token_offsets[d], doc_token_offsets[d+1])`
    97	    /// is the half-open token range of doc d, mirroring `TokenDump.doc_offsets`.
    98	    /// Refine slices `residual_norms` against this in O(1) per candidate doc.
    99	    doc_token_offsets: []const u64,
   100	    residual_norms: []const f32,
   101	
   102	    pub fn deinit(self: *Index, gpa: Allocator) void {
   103	        // PQ.codebooks are heap-owned (memcpy'd from the byte image so the
   104	        // mmap can be unmapped independently). HNSW likewise heap-owns its
   105	        // CSR copy; inverted_list flips owns_buffers on/off explicitly.
   106	        self.pq.deinit(gpa);
   107	        self.hnsw.deinit(gpa);
   108	        if (self.ilists.owns_buffers) self.ilists.deinit(gpa);
   109	        self.* = undefined;
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    pub fn docLayout(self: *const Index, d: u32) []const u8 {
   113	        const lo: usize = @intCast(self.doc_index[d]);
   114	        const hi: usize = @intCast(self.doc_index[d + 1]);
   115	        return self.doc_payload[lo..hi];
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    /// Half-open `[lo, hi)` global token range of doc `d`. Mirrors the
   119	    /// `TokenDump.doc_offsets` semantics. Use to slice `residual_norms`
   120	    /// (and any future per-token side table) for a candidate doc in O(1).
   121	    pub fn docTokenRange(self: *const Index, d: u32) [2]u64 {
   122	        return .{ self.doc_token_offsets[d], self.doc_token_offsets[d + 1] };
   123	    }
   124	};
   125	
   126	/// All the pieces a builder hands to `serialise`. The builder (task #15)
   127	/// assembles these from token_dump → tac → pq → hnsw → inverted_list, then
   128	/// calls `serialise` to materialise a single byte image.
   129	pub const BuildOutput = struct {
   130	    dim: u32,
   131	    kappa: u32,
   132	    sub_dim: u32,
   133	    n_docs: u64,
   134	    n_tokens: u64,
   135	    centroids: []const f32, // kappa * dim
   136	    /// `[]u32` token-→-centroid assignments, length `n_tokens`. Used to emit
   137	    /// the per-doc Pass 1 region. Same source slice that fed inverted_list.
   138	    assignments: []const u32,
   139	    /// `[]u8` flat PQ codes, length `n_tokens * PQ_M` (M codes per token).
   140	    /// Used to emit the per-doc Pass 2 region.
   141	    pq_codes: []const u8,
   142	    /// Doc offsets (CSR): `doc_offsets[d+1] - doc_offsets[d]` = n_d.
   143	    /// Persisted as the `doc_token_offsets` section so refine can slice
   144	    /// `residual_norms` per candidate doc in O(1).
   145	    doc_offsets: []const u64,
   146	    pq: *const pq_mod.PQ,
   147	    hnsw: *const hnsw_mod.Hnsw,
   148	    ilists: *const inverted_list.InvertedLists,
   149	    residual_norms: []const f32, // n_tokens floats, paper §4 side data
   150	};
   151	
   152	inline fn alignUp(x: u64, a: u64) u64 {
   153	    return (x + a - 1) / a * a;
   154	}

   164	pub const BuildParams = struct {
   165	    kappa_total: u32,
   166	    seed: u64,
   167	    /// HNSW construction params — paper-strict defaults.
   168	    hnsw: hnsw_mod.BuildParams = .{},
   169	    /// TAC defaults pulled from constants. Tests can override for tiny
   170	    /// fixtures where 39 vectors/centroid is impossibly demanding.
   171	    mu: u32 = constants.TAC_MU,
   172	    tau: u32 = constants.TAC_TAU,
   173	    epsilon: u32 = constants.TAC_EPSILON,
   174	    theta: u32 = constants.TAC_THETA,
   175	    /// Worker threads for the per-token Lloyd loop (paper §3 parallelism).
   176	    /// `1` is serial; >1 dispatches via std.Thread.spawn with static chunks.
   177	    n_threads: u32 = 1,
   178	    /// When true, stage timings are printed to stderr.
   179	    verbose: bool = false,
   180	};
   181	
   182	/// Monotonic ns timestamp (Zig 0.16: std.time.Timer is gone behind std.Io).
   183	fn nowNs() u64 {
   184	    var ts: std.c.timespec = undefined;
   185	    _ = std.c.clock_gettime(std.c.CLOCK.MONOTONIC, &ts);
   186	    return @as(u64, @intCast(ts.sec)) * 1_000_000_000 + @as(u64, @intCast(ts.nsec));
   187	}
   188	
   189	fn msSince(start_ns: u64) f64 {
   190	    return @as(f64, @floatFromInt(nowNs() - start_ns)) / 1e6;
   191	}
   192	
   193	/// Static-chunk parallel for-loop over [0, n_tokens). Each thread runs
   194	/// `worker(ctx, lo, hi)` for a disjoint chunk. n_threads <= 1 falls
   195	/// through to a serial call.
   196	fn parallelTokenLoop(
   197	    n_threads: u32,
   198	    n_tokens: u64,
   199	    comptime worker: anytype,
   200	    ctx: anytype,
   201	    gpa: Allocator,
   202	) !void {
   203	    if (n_threads <= 1 or n_tokens < 1024) {
   204	        try worker(ctx, 0, n_tokens);
   205	        return;
   206	    }
   207	    const ChunkErr = struct { e: ?@typeInfo(@typeInfo(@TypeOf(worker)).@"fn".return_type.?).error_union.error_set };
   208	    const Run = struct {
   209	        fn run(c: @TypeOf(ctx), lo: u64, hi: u64, out: *ChunkErr) void {
   210	            worker(c, lo, hi) catch |err| {
   211	                out.e = err;
   212	            };
   213	        }
   214	    };
   215	    const T = @min(n_threads, @as(u32, @intCast(n_tokens)));
   216	    const errs = try gpa.alloc(ChunkErr, T);
   217	    defer gpa.free(errs);
   218	    @memset(errs, .{ .e = null });
   219	
   220	    const threads = try gpa.alloc(std.Thread, T);
   221	    defer gpa.free(threads);
   222	    const chunk = (n_tokens + T - 1) / T;
   223	    for (0..T) |t| {
   224	        const lo: u64 = @as(u64, t) * chunk;
   225	        const hi: u64 = @min(lo + chunk, n_tokens);
   226	        threads[t] = try std.Thread.spawn(.{}, Run.run, .{ ctx, lo, hi, &errs[t] });
   227	    }
   228	    for (threads) |th| th.join();
   229	    for (errs) |c| if (c.e) |err| return err;
   230	}
   231	
   232	const ResidualsCtx = struct {
   233	    residuals: []f32,
   234	    residual_norms: []f32,
   235	    vectors: []const f32,
   236	    centroids: []const f32,
   237	    assignments: []const u32,
   238	    dim: u32,
   239	};
   240	
   241	fn residualsAndNormsChunk(c: ResidualsCtx, lo: u64, hi: u64) !void {
   242	    const dim = c.dim;
   243	    var i = lo;
   244	    while (i < hi) : (i += 1) {
   245	        const cid: u32 = c.assignments[@intCast(i)];
   246	        const v = c.vectors[@intCast(i * @as(u64, dim))..][0..dim];
   247	        const cent = c.centroids[@as(usize, cid) * dim ..][0..dim];
   248	        const r = c.residuals[@intCast(i * @as(u64, dim))..][0..dim];
   249	        var sum_sq: f32 = 0.0;
   250	        var dd: usize = 0;
   251	        while (dd < dim) : (dd += 1) {
   252	            const diff = v[dd] - cent[dd];
   253	            r[dd] = diff;
   254	            sum_sq += diff * diff;
   255	        }
   256	        const norm: f32 = @sqrt(sum_sq);
   257	        c.residual_norms[@intCast(i)] = norm;
   258	        if (norm > 0.0) {
   259	            const inv: f32 = 1.0 / norm;
   260	            dd = 0;
   261	            while (dd < dim) : (dd += 1) r[dd] *= inv;
   262	        }
   263	    }
   264	}
   265	
   266	const PqEncodeCtx = struct {
   267	    pq: *const pq_mod.PQ,
   268	    residuals: []const f32,
   269	    pq_codes: []u8,
   270	    dim: u32,
   271	};
   272	
   273	fn pqEncodeChunk(c: PqEncodeCtx, lo: u64, hi: u64) !void {
   274	    const dim = c.dim;
   275	    var i = lo;
   276	    while (i < hi) : (i += 1) {
   277	        const r = c.residuals[@intCast(i * @as(u64, dim))..][0..dim];
   278	        const slot = c.pq_codes[@intCast(i * @as(u64, constants.PQ_M))..][0..constants.PQ_M];
   279	        try c.pq.encode(r, slot);
   280	    }

 succeeded in 0ms:
   280	    }
   281	}

   283	/// In-memory bundle returned by `build`. The caller writes `bytes` to disk
   284	/// (or hands it to `parse` for a same-process round-trip). All transient
   285	/// build artefacts are released before return — only the byte image survives.
   286	pub const BuiltImage = struct {
   287	    bytes: []align(8) u8,
   288	
   289	    pub fn deinit(self: *BuiltImage, gpa: Allocator) void {
   290	        gpa.free(self.bytes);
   291	        self.* = undefined;
   292	    }
   293	};
   294	
   295	/// End-to-end Index.build (paper §3 + §4). Steps:
   296	///   1. (paper §3) TAC clusters the corpus → centroids + assignments.
   297	///   2. (paper §4) Residuals = vec − centroid; normalise; norms saved.
   298	///   3. (paper §4) PQ.train on residuals → 32-byte codes per vector.
   299	///   4. (paper §4) Encode every residual → flat code stream.
   300	///   5. (paper §4) HNSW.build over centroids (centroids re-normalised so
   301	///      inner product = cosine, per paper §11 convention).
   302	///   6. (paper §4) Inverted lists from (assignments, doc_offsets).
   303	///   7. Serialise: header + sections + footer crc32 → byte image.
   304	///
   305	/// paper-gap: TAC produces centroids that are means of unit-norm tokens but
   306	/// are NOT themselves unit-norm. We L2-normalise centroids before HNSW so
   307	/// dot product agrees with cosine at query time. The original (un-norm)
   308	/// centroids are NOT preserved — Tachiom only ever consumes ⟨q, c⟩ at
   309	/// query time, where re-norming would be redundant.
   310	pub fn build(
   311	    td: *const token_dump.TokenDump,
   312	    params: BuildParams,
   313	    gpa: Allocator,
   314	) (StorageError || tac.ClusteringError || pq_mod.PqError || hnsw_mod.HnswError ||
   315	    inverted_list.InvertedListError)!BuiltImage {
   316	    if (td.dim == 0 or td.dim % constants.PQ_M != 0) return error.InvalidIndex;
   317	
   318	    const dim: u32 = td.dim;
   319	    const sub_dim: u32 = dim / constants.PQ_M;
   320	    const n_tokens: u64 = td.n_tokens;
   321	    const n_docs: u64 = td.n_docs;
   322	
   323	    const verbose = params.verbose;
   324	
   325	    // ---- 1. TAC clustering (paper §3). ----
   326	    const t1_0 = nowNs();
   327	    var clu = try tac.clusterFlat(td.token_ids, td.vectors, dim, .{
   328	        .kappa_total = params.kappa_total,
   329	        .mu = params.mu,
   330	        .tau = params.tau,
   331	        .epsilon = params.epsilon,
   332	        .theta = params.theta,
   333	        .seed = params.seed,
   334	        .n_threads = params.n_threads,
   335	    }, gpa);
   336	    defer clu.deinit(gpa);
   337	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] tac.clusterFlat:  {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t1_0)});
   338	
   339	    const kappa: u32 = params.kappa_total;
   340	
   341	    // ---- 2. Residuals + per-vector norms (paper §4). ----
   342	    const t2_0 = nowNs();
   343	    const residuals = try gpa.alloc(f32, @intCast(n_tokens * @as(u64, dim)));
   344	    defer gpa.free(residuals);
   345	    const residual_norms = try gpa.alloc(f32, @intCast(n_tokens));
   346	    errdefer gpa.free(residual_norms);
   347	
   348	    try parallelTokenLoop(
   349	        params.n_threads,
   350	        n_tokens,
   351	        residualsAndNormsChunk,
   352	        ResidualsCtx{
   353	            .residuals = residuals,
   354	            .residual_norms = residual_norms,
   355	            .vectors = td.vectors,
   356	            .centroids = clu.centroids,
   357	            .assignments = clu.assignments,
   358	            .dim = dim,
   359	        },
   360	        gpa,
   361	    );
   362	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] residuals + norms: {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t2_0)});
   363	
   364	    // ---- 3. PQ training (paper §4). ----
   365	    const t3_0 = nowNs();
   366	    var pq = try pq_mod.train(residuals, dim, params.seed +% 0xa1b2c3d4, params.n_threads, gpa);
   367	    errdefer pq.deinit(gpa);
   368	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] pq.train:          {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t3_0)});
   369	
   370	    // ---- 4. Encode every residual into a flat M·n_tokens code stream. ----
   371	    const t4_0 = nowNs();
   372	    const total_codes: u64 = n_tokens * @as(u64, constants.PQ_M);
   373	    const pq_codes = try gpa.alloc(u8, @intCast(total_codes));
   374	    errdefer gpa.free(pq_codes);
   375	    try parallelTokenLoop(
   376	        params.n_threads,
   377	        n_tokens,
   378	        pqEncodeChunk,
   379	        PqEncodeCtx{
   380	            .pq = &pq,
   381	            .residuals = residuals,
   382	            .pq_codes = pq_codes,
   383	            .dim = dim,
   384	        },
   385	        gpa,
   386	    );
   387	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] pq.encode:         {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t4_0)});
   388	
   389	    // ---- 5. HNSW over (re-normalised) centroids (paper §4 + §11). ----
   390	    // paper-gap §3: TAC outputs raw means. We need unit-norm for HNSW dot ≡
   391	    // cosine. Make a working copy so we don't disturb `clu.centroids` for
   392	    // any downstream caller.
   393	    const t5_0 = nowNs();
   394	    const centroids_norm = try gpa.alloc(f32, kappa * dim);
   395	    errdefer gpa.free(centroids_norm);
   396	    @memcpy(centroids_norm, clu.centroids);
   397	    try vec_mod.normalizeRowsInPlace(centroids_norm, dim);
   398	
   399	    // Thread storage's n_threads + verbose through to the HNSW build,
   400	    // unless the caller already pinned hnsw.n_threads (>1) explicitly.
   401	    var hnsw_params = params.hnsw;
   402	    if (hnsw_params.n_threads <= 1) hnsw_params.n_threads = params.n_threads;
   403	    if (verbose) hnsw_params.verbose = true;
   404	    var hnsw_g = try hnsw_mod.build(
   405	        centroids_norm,
   406	        dim,
   407	        params.seed +% 0xdeadbeef,
   408	        hnsw_params,
   409	        gpa,
   410	    );
   411	    errdefer hnsw_g.deinit(gpa);
   412	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] hnsw.build:        {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t5_0)});
   413	
   414	    // ---- 6. Inverted lists (paper §4, doc-level grain). ----
   415	    const t6_0 = nowNs();
   416	    var ilists = try inverted_list.build(clu.assignments, td.doc_offsets, kappa, gpa);
   417	    errdefer ilists.deinit(gpa);
   418	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] inverted_list:     {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t6_0)});
   419	
   420	    // ---- 7. Serialise. ----
   421	    const t7_0 = nowNs();
   422	    const out = BuildOutput{
   423	        .dim = dim,
   424	        .kappa = kappa,
   425	        .sub_dim = sub_dim,
   426	        .n_docs = n_docs,
   427	        .n_tokens = n_tokens,
   428	        .centroids = centroids_norm,
   429	        .assignments = clu.assignments,
   430	        .pq_codes = pq_codes,
   431	        .doc_offsets = td.doc_offsets,
   432	        .pq = &pq,
   433	        .hnsw = &hnsw_g,
   434	        .ilists = &ilists,
   435	        .residual_norms = residual_norms,
   436	    };
   437	
   438	    const total = computeSize(out);
   439	    const buf = try gpa.alignedAlloc(u8, .@"8", @intCast(total));
   440	    errdefer gpa.free(buf);
   441	
   442	    _ = try serialise(out, buf);
   443	    if (verbose) std.debug.print("    [stage] serialise:         {d:8.1} ms\n", .{msSince(t7_0)});
   444	
   445	    // Drop transient build state — image is self-contained.
   446	    ilists.deinit(gpa);
   447	    hnsw_g.deinit(gpa);
   448	    pq.deinit(gpa);
   449	    gpa.free(centroids_norm);
   450	    gpa.free(pq_codes);
   451	    gpa.free(residual_norms);
   452	
   453	    return BuiltImage{ .bytes = buf };
   454	}
   455	
   456	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   457	// LE writers / readers
   458	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   459	
   460	inline fn writeU32Le(buf: []u8, off: usize, v: u32) void {
   461	    std.mem.writeInt(u32, buf[off..][0..4], v, .little);
   462	}
   463	inline fn writeU64Le(buf: []u8, off: usize, v: u64) void {
   464	    std.mem.writeInt(u64, buf[off..][0..8], v, .little);
   465	}
   466	inline fn readU32Le(buf: []const u8, off: usize) u32 {
   467	    return std.mem.readInt(u32, buf[off..][0..4], .little);
   468	}
   469	inline fn readU64Le(buf: []const u8, off: usize) u64 {
   470	    return std.mem.readInt(u64, buf[off..][0..8], .little);
   471	}
   472	
   473	/// Compute the total byte size needed to serialise `out`. Caller allocates
   474	/// a buffer of this size before calling `serialise`. Sizes are computed
   475	/// section by section, each padded up to 8-byte alignment.
   476	pub fn computeSize(out: BuildOutput) u64 {
   477	    var off: u64 = header_region_size;
   478	    // centroids
   479	    off += @as(u64, out.kappa) * @as(u64, out.dim) * @sizeOf(f32);
   480	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   481	    // hnsw — see writeHnsw
   482	    off += hnswSize(out.hnsw);
   483	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   484	    // pq codebooks
   485	    off += @as(u64, constants.PQ_M) * @as(u64, constants.PQ_CENTROIDS) *
   486	        @as(u64, out.sub_dim) * @sizeOf(f32);
   487	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   488	    // inverted lists
   489	    off += (@as(u64, out.kappa) + 1) * @sizeOf(u64);
   490	    off += out.ilists.offsets[out.ilists.kappa] * @sizeOf(u32);
   491	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   492	    // per-doc layouts: doc_index [n_docs+1]u64 + payload
   493	    off += (out.n_docs + 1) * @sizeOf(u64);
   494	    off += docPayloadSize(out);
   495	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   496	    // doc_token_offsets [n_docs+1]u64 (CSR token range per doc, paper §4 + §5.3)
   497	    off += (out.n_docs + 1) * @sizeOf(u64);
   498	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   499	    // residual norms
   500	    off += out.n_tokens * @sizeOf(f32);
   501	    off = alignUp(off, 8);
   502	    // footer crc32 + 4 bytes pad
   503	    off += 8;
   504	    return off;
   505	}
   506	
   507	fn hnswSize(g: *const hnsw_mod.Hnsw) u64 {
   508	    // header: max_level u8 + pad7 + entry_point u32 + pad4 + n u32 + pad4 = 24 bytes
   509	    var bytes: u64 = 24;
   510	    // node_levels [n]u8 padded to 8
   511	    bytes += @as(u64, g.n);
   512	    bytes = alignUp(bytes, 8);
   513	    // per layer: offsets [n+1]u32 padded, payload [last]u32 padded
   514	    var L: usize = 0;
   515	    while (L < g.layer_offsets.len) : (L += 1) {
   516	        bytes += @as(u64, g.n + 1) * @sizeOf(u32);
   517	        bytes = alignUp(bytes, 8);
   518	        const last_off: u32 = g.layer_offsets[L][g.n];
   519	        bytes += @as(u64, last_off) * @sizeOf(u32);
   520	        bytes = alignUp(bytes, 8);
   521	    }
   522	    return bytes;
   523	}
   524	
   525	fn docPayloadSize(out: BuildOutput) u64 {
   526	    // for each doc: u32 n_d + u32[n_d] centroids + u8[n_d * PQ_M] codes,
   527	    // each doc padded to 8.
   528	    var total: u64 = 0;
   529	    var d: u64 = 0;
   530	    while (d < out.n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   531	        const n_d: u64 = out.doc_offsets[d + 1] - out.doc_offsets[d];
   532	        var bytes: u64 = @sizeOf(u32) + n_d * @sizeOf(u32) + n_d * @as(u64, constants.PQ_M);
   533	        bytes = alignUp(bytes, 8);
   534	        total += bytes;
   535	    }
   536	    return total;
   537	}
   538	
   539	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   540	// SERIALISE
   541	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   542	
   543	/// Write the whole index image into `buf`. Caller-owned buffer; size must
   544	/// equal `computeSize(out)` (see test for expected usage). Returns the
   545	/// final image size for convenience.
   546	pub fn serialise(out: BuildOutput, buf: []u8) StorageError!u64 {
   547	    const total: u64 = computeSize(out);
   548	    if (buf.len < total) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   549	
   550	    // Reserve header region; we fill it last after offsets are known.
   551	    @memset(buf[0..header_region_size], 0);
   552	    var cur: u64 = header_region_size;
   553	
   554	    // ---- Centroids ----
   555	    const centroids_off = cur;
   556	    {
   557	        const n: u64 = @as(u64, out.kappa) * @as(u64, out.dim);
   558	        const bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(out.centroids[0..@intCast(n)]);
   559	        std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..bytes.len], bytes);
   560	        cur += @as(u64, bytes.len);
   561	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   562	    }
   563	
   564	    // ---- HNSW ----
   565	    const hnsw_off = cur;
   566	    cur += try writeHnsw(out.hnsw, buf[@intCast(cur)..]);
   567	    cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   568	
   569	    // ---- PQ codebooks ----
   570	    const pq_off = cur;
   571	    {
   572	        const bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(out.pq.codebooks);
   573	        std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..bytes.len], bytes);
   574	        cur += @as(u64, bytes.len);
   575	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   576	    }
   577	
   578	    // ---- Inverted lists ----
   579	    const ilist_off = cur;
   580	    {
   581	        const offs_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(out.ilists.offsets);
   582	        std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..offs_bytes.len], offs_bytes);
   583	        cur += @as(u64, offs_bytes.len);
   584	
   585	        const payload_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(out.ilists.payload);
   586	        std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..payload_bytes.len], payload_bytes);
   587	        cur += @as(u64, payload_bytes.len);
   588	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   589	    }
   590	
   591	    // ---- Per-doc layouts ----
   592	    const doc_off = cur;
   593	    cur += try writeDocLayouts(out, buf[@intCast(cur)..]);
   594	    cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   595	
   596	    // ---- Doc token offsets (paper §5.3 refine slicing) ----
   597	    const doc_tok_off = cur;
   598	    {
   599	        const dto_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(out.doc_offsets);
   600	        std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..dto_bytes.len], dto_bytes);
   601	        cur += @as(u64, dto_bytes.len);
   602	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   603	    }
   604	
   605	    // ---- Residual norms ----
   606	    const norms_off = cur;
   607	    {
   608	        const norms_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(out.residual_norms);
   609	        std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..norms_bytes.len], norms_bytes);
   610	        cur += @as(u64, norms_bytes.len);
   611	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   612	    }
   613	
   614	    const footer_off = cur;
   615	
   616	    // ---- Header ----
   617	    const hdr = Header{
   618	        .magic = constants.INDEX_MAGIC,
   619	        .version = constants.INDEX_VERSION,
   620	        .dim = out.dim,

exec

   621	        .kappa = out.kappa,
   622	        .pq_M = constants.PQ_M,
   623	        .pq_bits = @intCast(constants.PQ_BITS),
   624	        ._pad0 = .{ 0, 0, 0 },
   625	        .sub_dim = out.sub_dim,
   626	        .n_docs = out.n_docs,
   627	        .n_tokens = out.n_tokens,
   628	        .centroids_off = centroids_off,
   629	        .hnsw_off = hnsw_off,
   630	        .pq_off = pq_off,
   631	        .ilist_off = ilist_off,
   632	        .doc_off = doc_off,

   635	        .footer_off = footer_off,
   636	    };
   637	    const hdr_bytes = std.mem.asBytes(&hdr);
   638	    std.mem.copyForwards(u8, buf[0..hdr_bytes.len], hdr_bytes);
   639	    // bytes [hdr_bytes.len .. header_region_size) are already zero.
   640	
   641	    // ---- Footer crc32 ----
   642	    var hasher = std.hash.Crc32.init();
   643	    hasher.update(buf[0..@intCast(footer_off)]);
   644	    const crc: u32 = hasher.final();
   645	    writeU32Le(buf, @intCast(footer_off), crc);
   646	    writeU32Le(buf, @intCast(footer_off + 4), 0);
   647	    cur = footer_off + 8;
   648	
   649	    std.debug.assert(cur == total);
   650	    return total;
   651	}
   652	
   653	fn writeHnsw(g: *const hnsw_mod.Hnsw, buf: []u8) StorageError!u64 {
   654	    if (buf.len < 24) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   655	    buf[0] = g.max_level;
   656	    @memset(buf[1..8], 0);
   657	    writeU32Le(buf, 8, g.entry_point);
   658	    writeU32Le(buf, 12, 0);
   659	    writeU32Le(buf, 16, g.n);
   660	    writeU32Le(buf, 20, 0);
   661	    var cur: u64 = 24;
   662	
   663	    // node_levels
   664	    if (buf.len < cur + g.n) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   665	    @memcpy(buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..g.n], g.node_levels);
   666	    cur += @as(u64, g.n);
   667	    const padded = alignUp(cur, 8);
   668	    @memset(buf[@intCast(cur)..@intCast(padded)], 0);
   669	    cur = padded;
   670	
   671	    // per layer
   672	    var L: usize = 0;
   673	    while (L < g.layer_offsets.len) : (L += 1) {
   674	        const offs = g.layer_offsets[L];
   675	        const offs_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(offs);
   676	        if (buf.len < cur + offs_bytes.len) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   677	        @memcpy(buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..offs_bytes.len], offs_bytes);
   678	        cur += @as(u64, offs_bytes.len);
   679	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   680	
   681	        const pay = g.layer_neighbours[L];
   682	        const pay_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(pay);
   683	        if (buf.len < cur + pay_bytes.len) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   684	        @memcpy(buf[@intCast(cur)..][0..pay_bytes.len], pay_bytes);
   685	        cur += @as(u64, pay_bytes.len);
   686	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   687	    }
   688	    return cur;
   689	}
   690	
   691	fn writeDocLayouts(out: BuildOutput, buf: []u8) StorageError!u64 {
   692	    const idx_bytes: u64 = (out.n_docs + 1) * @sizeOf(u64);
   693	    if (buf.len < idx_bytes) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   694	    var cur: u64 = idx_bytes;
   695	    // First write doc_index (offsets into the payload region — relative to
   696	    // the start of the payload, which is the byte right after the index).
   697	    var d: u64 = 0;
   698	    var payload_off: u64 = 0;
   699	    while (d < out.n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   700	        writeU64Le(buf, @intCast(d * 8), payload_off);
   701	        const n_d: u64 = out.doc_offsets[d + 1] - out.doc_offsets[d];
   702	        const tok_lo: u64 = out.doc_offsets[d];
   703	        // Pass 1: centroid IDs
   704	        const pass1_bytes: u64 = n_d * @sizeOf(u32);
   705	        const pass2_bytes: u64 = n_d * @as(u64, constants.PQ_M);
   706	        const this_doc_bytes_unpadded: u64 = @sizeOf(u32) + pass1_bytes + pass2_bytes;
   707	        const this_doc_bytes: u64 = alignUp(this_doc_bytes_unpadded, 8);
   708	        if (buf.len < cur + this_doc_bytes) return error.BufferTooSmall;
   709	
   710	        // Write n_d (u32) at cur, then pass1, then pass2, then zero-pad.
   711	        writeU32Le(buf, @intCast(cur), @intCast(n_d));
   712	        var write_off: u64 = cur + 4;
   713	
   714	        // Pass 1: centroid IDs from assignments[tok_lo .. tok_lo + n_d]
   715	        const assigns_slice = out.assignments[@intCast(tok_lo) .. @intCast(tok_lo + n_d)];
   716	        const a_bytes = std.mem.sliceAsBytes(assigns_slice);
   717	        @memcpy(buf[@intCast(write_off)..][0..a_bytes.len], a_bytes);
   718	        write_off += @as(u64, a_bytes.len);
   719	
   720	        // Pass 2: PQ codes from pq_codes[tok_lo*M .. (tok_lo+n_d)*M]
   721	        const codes_lo: u64 = tok_lo * @as(u64, constants.PQ_M);
   722	        const codes_hi: u64 = (tok_lo + n_d) * @as(u64, constants.PQ_M);
   723	        const codes_slice = out.pq_codes[@intCast(codes_lo)..@intCast(codes_hi)];
   724	        @memcpy(buf[@intCast(write_off)..][0..codes_slice.len], codes_slice);
   725	        write_off += @as(u64, codes_slice.len);
   726	
   727	        // Zero-pad up to this_doc_bytes from cur.
   728	        @memset(buf[@intCast(write_off)..@intCast(cur + this_doc_bytes)], 0);
   729	
   730	        payload_off += this_doc_bytes;
   731	        cur += this_doc_bytes;
   732	    }
   733	    writeU64Le(buf, @intCast(out.n_docs * 8), payload_off);
   734	    return cur;
   735	}
   736	
   737	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   738	// PARSE
   739	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   740	
   741	/// Parse a serialised index back into structured form. Allocates copies of
   742	/// the PQ codebooks, HNSW CSR arrays, and inverted-list buffers because the
   743	/// downstream types own their memory — that keeps mmap unmaps decoupled
   744	/// from the lifetime of those structs. Doc-index, doc-payload, centroids,
   745	/// and residual-norms slices alias `bytes` (no copy) since they're plain
   746	/// arrays read at query time.
   747	///
   748	/// `bytes` must be 8-byte aligned at offset 0 — both `gpa.alloc(u8, ...)`
   749	/// and any mmap region trivially are. With `header_region_size = 128` and
   750	/// every section padded to 8B, the natural alignments line up.
   751	pub fn parse(bytes: []align(8) const u8, gpa: Allocator) StorageError!Index {
   752	    if (bytes.len < header_region_size + 8) return error.InvalidIndex;
   753	
   754	    // Read header.
   755	    var hdr: Header = undefined;
   756	    @memcpy(std.mem.asBytes(&hdr), bytes[0..@sizeOf(Header)]);
   757	
   758	    if (!std.mem.eql(u8, &hdr.magic, &constants.INDEX_MAGIC)) {
   759	        return error.InvalidIndex;
   760	    }
   761	    if (hdr.version != constants.INDEX_VERSION) return error.UnsupportedVersion;
   762	    if (hdr.pq_M != constants.PQ_M) return error.InvalidIndex;
   763	    if (hdr.pq_bits != @as(u8, @intCast(constants.PQ_BITS))) return error.InvalidIndex;
   764	    if (hdr.sub_dim * hdr.pq_M != hdr.dim) return error.InvalidIndex;
   765	    if (hdr.dim == 0 or hdr.kappa == 0) return error.InvalidIndex;
   766	
   767	    // Section offsets must be monotonic and within bounds.
   768	    const offs = [_]u64{
   769	        hdr.centroids_off, hdr.hnsw_off,    hdr.pq_off,
   770	        hdr.ilist_off,     hdr.doc_off,     hdr.doc_tok_off,
   771	        hdr.norms_off,     hdr.footer_off,
   772	    };
   773	    if (offs[0] != header_region_size) return error.InvalidIndex;
   774	    var i: usize = 1;
   775	    while (i < offs.len) : (i += 1) {
   776	        if (offs[i] < offs[i - 1]) return error.InvalidIndex;
   777	        if (offs[i] > bytes.len) return error.InvalidIndex;
   778	    }
   779	    if (hdr.footer_off + 8 > bytes.len) return error.InvalidIndex;
   780	
   781	    // CRC check.
   782	    const stored_crc: u32 = readU32Le(bytes, @intCast(hdr.footer_off));
   783	    var hasher = std.hash.Crc32.init();
   784	    hasher.update(bytes[0..@intCast(hdr.footer_off)]);
   785	    if (hasher.final() != stored_crc) return error.IndexCorrupt;
   786	
   787	    // ---- Centroids (alias) ----
   788	    const centroids_bytes = bytes[@intCast(hdr.centroids_off)..@intCast(hdr.hnsw_off)];
   789	    const centroids_n: usize = @intCast(@as(u64, hdr.kappa) * @as(u64, hdr.dim));
   790	    if (centroids_bytes.len < centroids_n * @sizeOf(f32)) return error.InvalidIndex;
   791	    // The buffer is 8-byte aligned (parameter constraint) and centroids_off
   792	    // = 128, also 8-aligned, so the resulting f32 slice is 4-aligned. We
   793	    // use @alignCast to communicate this to the type system.
   794	    const centroids_aligned: []align(@alignOf(f32)) const u8 = @alignCast(centroids_bytes[0 .. centroids_n * @sizeOf(f32)]);
   795	    const centroids_slice = std.mem.bytesAsSlice(f32, centroids_aligned);
   796	
   797	    // ---- HNSW (heap-copy CSR, borrow centroids) ----
   798	    var hnsw_view = try parseHnsw(bytes[@intCast(hdr.hnsw_off)..@intCast(hdr.pq_off)], hdr.dim, centroids_slice, gpa);
   799	    errdefer hnsw_view.deinit(gpa);
   800	
   801	    // ---- PQ (heap-copy codebooks) ----
   802	    var pq_view = try parsePq(bytes[@intCast(hdr.pq_off)..@intCast(hdr.ilist_off)], hdr.dim, hdr.sub_dim, gpa);
   803	    errdefer pq_view.deinit(gpa);
   804	
   805	    // ---- Inverted lists (heap-copy so deinit is uniform) ----
   806	    var ilists_view = try parseInvertedLists(
   807	        bytes[@intCast(hdr.ilist_off)..@intCast(hdr.doc_off)],
   808	        hdr.kappa,
   809	        gpa,
   810	    );
   811	    errdefer if (ilists_view.owns_buffers) ilists_view.deinit(gpa);
   812	
   813	    // ---- Per-doc index + payload (alias slices) ----
   814	    const doc_section = bytes[@intCast(hdr.doc_off)..@intCast(hdr.doc_tok_off)];
   815	    const doc_index_bytes_len: usize = @intCast((hdr.n_docs + 1) * @sizeOf(u64));
   816	    if (doc_section.len < doc_index_bytes_len) return error.InvalidIndex;
   817	    const doc_index_aligned: []align(@alignOf(u64)) const u8 = @alignCast(doc_section[0..doc_index_bytes_len]);
   818	    const doc_index_slice = std.mem.bytesAsSlice(u64, doc_index_aligned);
   819	    const doc_payload_slice = doc_section[doc_index_bytes_len..];
   820	
   821	    // ---- Doc token offsets (alias) ----
   822	    const doc_tok_bytes = bytes[@intCast(hdr.doc_tok_off)..@intCast(hdr.norms_off)];
   823	    const doc_tok_bytes_len: usize = @intCast((hdr.n_docs + 1) * @sizeOf(u64));
   824	    if (doc_tok_bytes.len < doc_tok_bytes_len) return error.InvalidIndex;
   825	    const doc_tok_aligned: []align(@alignOf(u64)) const u8 = @alignCast(doc_tok_bytes[0..doc_tok_bytes_len]);
   826	    const doc_token_offsets_slice = std.mem.bytesAsSlice(u64, doc_tok_aligned);
   827	
   828	    // ---- Residual norms (alias) ----
   829	    const norms_bytes = bytes[@intCast(hdr.norms_off)..@intCast(hdr.footer_off)];
   830	    const norms_n: usize = @intCast(hdr.n_tokens);
   831	    if (norms_bytes.len < norms_n * @sizeOf(f32)) return error.InvalidIndex;
   832	    const norms_aligned: []align(@alignOf(f32)) const u8 = @alignCast(norms_bytes[0 .. norms_n * @sizeOf(f32)]);
   833	    const norms_slice = std.mem.bytesAsSlice(f32, norms_aligned);
   834	
   835	    return Index{
   836	        .header = hdr,
   837	        .centroids = centroids_slice,
   838	        .pq = pq_view,
   839	        .hnsw = hnsw_view,
   840	        .ilists = ilists_view,
   841	        .doc_index = doc_index_slice,
   842	        .doc_payload = doc_payload_slice,
   843	        .doc_token_offsets = doc_token_offsets_slice,
   844	        .residual_norms = norms_slice,
   845	    };
   846	}
   847	
   848	fn parseHnsw(
   849	    section: []const u8,
   850	    dim: u32,
   851	    centroids: []const f32,
   852	    gpa: Allocator,
   853	) StorageError!hnsw_mod.Hnsw {
   854	    if (section.len < 24) return error.InvalidIndex;
   855	    const max_level: u8 = section[0];
   856	    const entry_point: u32 = readU32Le(section, 8);
   857	    const n: u32 = readU32Le(section, 16);
   858	    var cur: u64 = 24;
   859	
   860	    if (n == 0) return error.InvalidIndex;
   861	    if (section.len < cur + n) return error.InvalidIndex;
   862	    const node_levels = try gpa.alloc(u8, n);
   863	    errdefer gpa.free(node_levels);
   864	    @memcpy(node_levels, section[@intCast(cur)..][0..n]);
   865	    cur += @as(u64, n);
   866	    cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   867	
   868	    const total_layers: usize = @as(usize, max_level) + 1;
   869	    const layer_offsets = try gpa.alloc([]u32, total_layers);
   870	    errdefer gpa.free(layer_offsets);
   871	    const layer_neighbours = try gpa.alloc([]u32, total_layers);
   872	    errdefer gpa.free(layer_neighbours);
   873	    var allocated: usize = 0;
   874	    errdefer {
   875	        var k: usize = 0;
   876	        while (k < allocated) : (k += 1) {
   877	            gpa.free(layer_offsets[k]);
   878	            gpa.free(layer_neighbours[k]);
   879	        }
   880	    }
   881	
   882	    var L: usize = 0;
   883	    while (L < total_layers) : (L += 1) {
   884	        const offs_bytes_len: usize = @intCast(@as(u64, n + 1) * @sizeOf(u32));
   885	        if (section.len < cur + offs_bytes_len) return error.InvalidIndex;
   886	        const offs = try gpa.alloc(u32, n + 1);
   887	        errdefer gpa.free(offs);
   888	        @memcpy(std.mem.sliceAsBytes(offs), section[@intCast(cur)..][0..offs_bytes_len]);
   889	        cur += @as(u64, offs_bytes_len);
   890	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   891	
   892	        const last_off: u32 = offs[n];
   893	        const pay_bytes_len: usize = @intCast(@as(u64, last_off) * @sizeOf(u32));
   894	        if (section.len < cur + pay_bytes_len) return error.InvalidIndex;
   895	        const pay = try gpa.alloc(u32, last_off);
   896	        errdefer gpa.free(pay);
   897	        @memcpy(std.mem.sliceAsBytes(pay), section[@intCast(cur)..][0..pay_bytes_len]);
   898	        cur += @as(u64, pay_bytes_len);
   899	        cur = alignUp(cur, 8);
   900	
   901	        layer_offsets[L] = offs;
   902	        layer_neighbours[L] = pay;
   903	        allocated = L + 1;
   904	    }
   905	
   906	    return hnsw_mod.Hnsw{
   907	        .dim = dim,
   908	        .n = n,
   909	        .entry_point = entry_point,
   910	        .max_level = max_level,
   911	        .node_levels = node_levels,
   912	        .layer_offsets = layer_offsets,
   913	        .layer_neighbours = layer_neighbours,
   914	        .centroids = centroids,
   915	    };
   916	}
   917	
   918	fn parsePq(
   919	    section: []const u8,
   920	    dim: u32,
   921	    sub_dim: u32,
   922	    gpa: Allocator,
   923	) StorageError!pq_mod.PQ {
   924	    const n_floats: usize = @as(usize, constants.PQ_M) *
   925	        @as(usize, constants.PQ_CENTROIDS) *
   926	        @as(usize, sub_dim);
   927	    const need: usize = n_floats * @sizeOf(f32);
   928	    if (section.len < need) return error.InvalidIndex;
   929	    const codebooks = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_floats);
   930	    errdefer gpa.free(codebooks);
   931	    @memcpy(std.mem.sliceAsBytes(codebooks), section[0..need]);
   932	    return pq_mod.PQ{
   933	        .dim = dim,
   934	        .sub_dim = sub_dim,
   935	        .codebooks = codebooks,
   936	    };
   937	}

   940	    section: []const u8,

codex
The HNSW file contradicts the README rather than hiding it: HNSW documents “n_threads=1 is serial, n_threads>=2 is deterministic but different.” Since `storage.build` forwards the global thread count into HNSW, the full `.tac` image is not byte-equal across 1 vs 4/10 even if PQ and k-means are.

    18	    var gpa_state: std.heap.DebugAllocator(.{}) = .init;
    19	    defer _ = gpa_state.deinit();
    20	    const gpa = gpa_state.allocator();
    21	
    22	    var threaded = std.Io.Threaded.init(gpa, .{});
    23	    defer threaded.deinit();
    24	    const io = threaded.io();
    25	    const cwd = std.Io.Dir.cwd();
    26	
    27	    var iter = try std.process.Args.Iterator.initAllocator(m.args, gpa);
    28	    defer iter.deinit();
    29	    _ = iter.next(); // program name
    30	
    31	    const cmd = iter.next() orelse {
    32	        printUsage();
    33	        return;
    34	    };
    35	
    36	    if (std.mem.eql(u8, cmd, "index")) {
    37	        try cmdIndex(&iter, gpa, io, cwd);
    38	    } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, cmd, "search")) {
    39	        try cmdSearch(&iter, gpa, io, cwd);
    40	    } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, cmd, "eval")) {
    41	        try cmdEval(&iter, gpa, io, cwd);
    42	    } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, cmd, "bench")) {
    43	        try cmdBench(&iter, gpa, io, cwd);
    44	    } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, cmd, "--help") or std.mem.eql(u8, cmd, "-h")) {
    45	        printUsage();
    46	    } else {
    47	        std.debug.print("unknown subcommand: {s}\n\n", .{cmd});
    48	        printUsage();
    49	        return error.UnknownSubcommand;
    50	    }
    51	}
    52	
    53	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54	// `tac index <tokens.bin> <out.tac> [--kappa N] [--seed N]`
    55	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56	
    57	fn cmdIndex(iter: *std.process.Args.Iterator, gpa: Allocator, io: std.Io, cwd: std.Io.Dir) !void {
    58	    var tokens_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
    59	    var out_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
    60	    var kappa: u32 = 4096;
    61	    var seed: u64 = 42;
    62	    var mu: u32 = tac.constants.TAC_MU;
    63	    var tau: u32 = tac.constants.TAC_TAU;
    64	    var epsilon: u32 = tac.constants.TAC_EPSILON;
    65	    var theta: u32 = tac.constants.TAC_THETA;
    66	    var n_threads: u32 = 1;
    67	    var positional: u32 = 0;
    68	
    69	    while (iter.next()) |arg| {
    70	        if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--kappa")) {
    71	            kappa = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    72	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--seed")) {
    73	            seed = try std.fmt.parseInt(u64, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    74	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--threads")) {
    75	            n_threads = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    76	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--mu")) {
    77	            mu = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    78	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--tau")) {
    79	            tau = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    80	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--epsilon")) {
    81	            epsilon = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    82	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--theta")) {
    83	            theta = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
    84	        } else if (std.mem.startsWith(u8, arg, "--")) {
    85	            std.debug.print("unknown flag: {s}\n", .{arg});
    86	            return error.UnknownFlag;
    87	        } else if (positional == 0) {
    88	            tokens_path = arg;
    89	            positional += 1;
    90	        } else if (positional == 1) {
    91	            out_path = arg;
    92	            positional += 1;
    93	        } else return error.TooManyPositional;
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    const tp = tokens_path orelse return usage("tac index <tokens.bin> <out.tac> [--kappa N] [--seed N]");
    97	    const op = out_path orelse return usage("tac index <tokens.bin> <out.tac> [--kappa N] [--seed N]");
    98	
    99	    std.debug.print("tac index: tokens={s} out={s} kappa={d} seed={d}\n", .{ tp, op, kappa, seed });
   100	
   101	    const t_load_0 = nowNs();
   102	    const td_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, tp, gpa);
   103	    defer gpa.free(td_raw);
   104	    const td_bytes: []align(8) const u8 = @alignCast(td_raw);
   105	    const td = try tac.io.token_dump.parseBytes(td_bytes);
   106	    const t_load_1 = nowNs();
   107	    std.debug.print(
   108	        "  loaded {d} docs / {d} tokens / dim={d} in {d:.1} ms\n",
   109	        .{ td.n_docs, td.n_tokens, td.dim, msBetween(t_load_0, t_load_1) },
   110	    );
   111	
   112	    if (mu != tac.constants.TAC_MU or tau != tac.constants.TAC_TAU or
   113	        epsilon != tac.constants.TAC_EPSILON or theta != tac.constants.TAC_THETA)
   114	    {
   115	        std.debug.print(
   116	            "  paper-relax: μ={d} (default {d}), τ={d} (default {d}), ε={d} (default {d}), θ={d} (default {d})\n",
   117	            .{ mu, tac.constants.TAC_MU, tau, tac.constants.TAC_TAU, epsilon, tac.constants.TAC_EPSILON, theta, tac.constants.TAC_THETA },
   118	        );
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    if (n_threads > 1) {
   122	        std.debug.print("  parallel build: {d} threads\n", .{n_threads});
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    const t_build_0 = nowNs();
   126	    var image = try tac.index.storage.build(&td, .{
   127	        .kappa_total = kappa,
   128	        .seed = seed,
   129	        .mu = mu,
   130	        .tau = tau,
   131	        .epsilon = epsilon,
   132	        .theta = theta,
   133	        .n_threads = n_threads,
   134	        .verbose = true,
   135	    }, gpa);
   136	    defer image.deinit(gpa);
   137	    const t_build_1 = nowNs();
   138	    std.debug.print(
   139	        "  built index in {d:.1} ms: {d} bytes ({d:.2} MB)\n",
   140	        .{
   141	            msBetween(t_build_0, t_build_1),
   142	            image.bytes.len,
   143	            @as(f64, @floatFromInt(image.bytes.len)) / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   144	        },
   145	    );
   146	
   147	    const t_write_0 = nowNs();
   148	    try writeFile(io, cwd, op, image.bytes);
   149	    const t_write_1 = nowNs();
   150	    std.debug.print("  wrote {s} in {d:.1} ms\n", .{ op, msBetween(t_write_0, t_write_1) });
   151	}
   152	
   153	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   154	// `tac search <index.tac> <queries.bin> [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F] [--top-k N]`
   155	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   156	
   157	fn cmdSearch(iter: *std.process.Args.Iterator, gpa: Allocator, io: std.Io, cwd: std.Io.Dir) !void {
   158	    var index_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   159	    var queries_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   160	    var kappa_c: u32 = 80;
   161	    var kappa_d: u32 = 1000;
   162	    var alpha: ?f32 = null;
   163	    var top_k: u32 = 10;
   164	    var positional: u32 = 0;
   165	
   166	    while (iter.next()) |arg| {
   167	        if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--kappa-c")) {
   168	            kappa_c = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   169	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--kappa-d")) {
   170	            kappa_d = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   171	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--alpha")) {
   172	            alpha = try std.fmt.parseFloat(f32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg);
   173	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--top-k")) {
   174	            top_k = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   175	        } else if (std.mem.startsWith(u8, arg, "--")) {
   176	            std.debug.print("unknown flag: {s}\n", .{arg});
   177	            return error.UnknownFlag;
   178	        } else if (positional == 0) {
   179	            index_path = arg;
   180	            positional += 1;
   181	        } else if (positional == 1) {
   182	            queries_path = arg;
   183	            positional += 1;
   184	        } else return error.TooManyPositional;
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    const ip = index_path orelse return usage("tac search <index.tac> <queries.bin> [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F] [--top-k N]");
   188	    const qp = queries_path orelse return usage("tac search <index.tac> <queries.bin> [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F] [--top-k N]");
   189	
   190	    const idx_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, ip, gpa);
   191	    defer gpa.free(idx_raw);
   192	    const idx_bytes: []align(8) u8 = @alignCast(idx_raw);
   193	    var index = try tac.index.storage.parse(idx_bytes, gpa);
   194	    defer index.deinit(gpa);
   195	    std.debug.print("loaded index: dim={d}\n", .{index.header.dim});
   196	
   197	    const q_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, qp, gpa);
   198	    defer gpa.free(q_raw);
   199	    const q_bytes: []align(8) const u8 = @alignCast(q_raw);
   200	    const queries = try tac.io.token_dump.parseBytes(q_bytes);
   201	    std.debug.print("loaded queries: n={d} dim={d}\n", .{ queries.n_docs, queries.dim });
   202	
   203	    if (queries.dim != index.header.dim) {
   204	        std.debug.print("dim mismatch: queries dim={d} != index dim={d}\n", .{ queries.dim, index.header.dim });
   205	        return error.DimMismatch;
   206	    }
   207	
   208	    const sp: tac.retrieval.search.SearchParams = .{
   209	        .kappa_c = kappa_c,
   210	        .kappa_d = kappa_d,
   211	        .alpha = alpha,
   212	        .top_k = top_k,
   213	    };
   214	
   215	    var total_ns: u64 = 0;
   216	    var n_run: u32 = 0;
   217	
   218	    var d: u64 = 0;
   219	    while (d < queries.n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   220	        const range = queries.docTokenRange(d);

   591	const SweepCtx = struct {
   592	    writer: *std.Io.Writer,
   593	    dataset: []const u8,
   594	    git_sha: []const u8,
   595	    n_done: u32,
   596	};
   597	
   598	fn sweepRowToWriter(row: tac.retrieval.bench.SweepRow, ctx: *anyopaque) anyerror!void {
   599	    const c: *SweepCtx = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ctx));
   600	    try tac.retrieval.bench.formatCsvRow(c.writer, c.dataset, c.git_sha, row);
   601	    try c.writer.writeByte('\n');
   602	    try c.writer.flush();
   603	    c.n_done += 1;
   604	    std.debug.print(
   605	        "  cell {d}: kappa_c={d} kappa_d={d} alpha={d} q={d:.4} avg={d:.2}ms\n",
   606	        .{
   607	            c.n_done,
   608	            row.kappa_c,
   609	            row.kappa_d,
   610	            row.alpha_x100,
   611	            row.quality,
   612	            row.avg_total_ms,
   613	        },
   614	    );
   615	}
   616	
   617	fn runBenchSweep(
   618	    index: *const tac.index.storage.Index,
   619	    pack: tac.retrieval.bench.QueryPack,
   620	    metric: tac.retrieval.bench.MetricKind,
   621	    dataset_label: []const u8,
   622	    writer: *std.Io.Writer,
   623	    gpa: Allocator,
   624	) !void {
   625	    try writer.writeAll(tac.retrieval.bench.csv_header);
   626	    try writer.writeByte('\n');
   627	    var ctx: SweepCtx = .{
   628	        .writer = writer,
   629	        .dataset = dataset_label,
   630	        .git_sha = "unknown",
   631	        .n_done = 0,
   632	    };
   633	    try tac.retrieval.bench.runSweep(index, pack, metric, sweepRowToWriter, &ctx, gpa);
   634	}

   636	const StdoutCtx = struct {
   637	    dataset: []const u8,
   638	    n_done: u32,
   639	};
   640	
   641	fn sweepRowToStdout(row: tac.retrieval.bench.SweepRow, ctx: *anyopaque) anyerror!void {
   642	    const c: *StdoutCtx = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ctx));
   643	    var buf: [512]u8 = undefined;
   644	    var w = std.Io.Writer.fixed(&buf);
   645	    try tac.retrieval.bench.formatCsvRow(&w, c.dataset, "unknown", row);
   646	    std.debug.print("{s}\n", .{w.buffered()});
   647	    c.n_done += 1;
   648	}

   650	fn runBenchSweepStdout(
   651	    index: *const tac.index.storage.Index,
   652	    pack: tac.retrieval.bench.QueryPack,
   653	    metric: tac.retrieval.bench.MetricKind,
   654	    dataset_label: []const u8,
   655	    gpa: Allocator,
   656	) !void {
   657	    std.debug.print("{s}\n", .{tac.retrieval.bench.csv_header});
   658	    var ctx: StdoutCtx = .{ .dataset = dataset_label, .n_done = 0 };
   659	    try tac.retrieval.bench.runSweep(index, pack, metric, sweepRowToStdout, &ctx, gpa);
   660	}

   662	/// Resolve qids for a queries.bin: prefer `<queries_path>.qids` (little-endian
   663	/// u32 sidecar emitted by tools/encode.py --mode queries), fall back to
   664	/// 1-indexed sequential qids when the sidecar is absent. The fallback matches
   665	/// the convention used by the tac search debug output and the user-built
   666	/// fixtures (`1\t<doc_id>` for the first query).
   667	fn loadOrSyntheticQids(
   668	    io: std.Io,
   669	    cwd: std.Io.Dir,
   670	    queries_path: []const u8,
   671	    n_queries: u64,
   672	    gpa: Allocator,
   673	) ![]u32 {
   674	    const sidecar_path = try std.fmt.allocPrint(gpa, "{s}.qids", .{queries_path});
   675	    defer gpa.free(sidecar_path);
   676	
   677	    const file_or_err = cwd.openFile(io, sidecar_path, .{});
   678	    if (file_or_err) |f_| {
   679	        var f = f_;
   680	        defer f.close(io);
   681	        const stat = try f.stat(io);
   682	        const size: usize = std.math.cast(usize, stat.size) orelse return error.FileTooLarge;
   683	        const expected: usize = @intCast(n_queries * @sizeOf(u32));
   684	        if (size != expected) {
   685	            std.debug.print(
   686	                "qids sidecar {s}: size={d} bytes but queries.n_docs={d} (expected {d})\n",
   687	                .{ sidecar_path, size, n_queries, expected },
   688	            );
   689	            return error.QidCountMismatch;
   690	        }
   691	        const buf = try gpa.alloc(u8, size);
   692	        defer gpa.free(buf);
   693	        var read_buf: [16 * 1024]u8 = undefined;
   694	        var fr = f.reader(io, &read_buf);
   695	        const reader = &fr.interface;
   696	        try reader.readSliceAll(buf);
   697	        const qids = try gpa.alloc(u32, @intCast(n_queries));
   698	        var i: usize = 0;
   699	        while (i < qids.len) : (i += 1) {
   700	            qids[i] = std.mem.readInt(u32, buf[i * 4 ..][0..4], .little);
   701	        }
   702	        return qids;
   703	    } else |err| switch (err) {
   704	        error.FileNotFound => {
   705	            const qids = try gpa.alloc(u32, @intCast(n_queries));
   706	            var i: u32 = 0;
   707	            while (i < qids.len) : (i += 1) qids[i] = i + 1;
   708	            return qids;
   709	        },
   710	        else => return err,
   711	    }
   712	}
   713	
   714	/// Slurp a file as a fresh byte buffer (no alignment requirement). Used for
   715	/// qrels.tsv where the parser walks the bytes by hand.
   716	fn slurpFileBytes(io: std.Io, dir: std.Io.Dir, path: []const u8, gpa: Allocator) ![]u8 {
   717	    var f = try dir.openFile(io, path, .{});
   718	    defer f.close(io);
   719	    const stat = try f.stat(io);
   720	    const size: usize = std.math.cast(usize, stat.size) orelse return error.FileTooLarge;
   721	    const buf = try gpa.alloc(u8, size);
   722	    errdefer gpa.free(buf);
   723	    var read_buf: [16 * 1024]u8 = undefined;
   724	    var fr = f.reader(io, &read_buf);
   725	    const reader = &fr.interface;
   726	    try reader.readSliceAll(buf);
   727	    return buf;
   728	}

   731	// Helpers

   733	
   734	/// Read a file into an 8-byte-aligned buffer. Matches the pattern proven
   735	/// in benchmarks/common/runner.zig::slurp under Zig 0.16's std.Io.
   736	fn slurpAligned8(io: std.Io, dir: std.Io.Dir, path: []const u8, gpa: Allocator) ![]align(8) u8 {
   737	    var f = try dir.openFile(io, path, .{});
   738	    defer f.close(io);
   739	    const stat = try f.stat(io);
   740	    const size: usize = std.math.cast(usize, stat.size) orelse return error.FileTooLarge;
   741	    const buf = try gpa.alignedAlloc(u8, .@"8", size);
   742	    errdefer gpa.free(buf);
   743	    var read_buf: [16 * 1024]u8 = undefined;
   744	    var fr = f.reader(io, &read_buf);
   745	    const reader = &fr.interface;
   746	    try reader.readSliceAll(buf);
   747	    return buf;
   748	}
   749	
   750	fn writeFile(io: std.Io, dir: std.Io.Dir, path: []const u8, bytes: []const u8) !void {
   751	    var f = try dir.createFile(io, path, .{ .truncate = true });
   752	    defer f.close(io);
   753	    var write_buf: [16 * 1024]u8 = undefined;
   754	    var fw = f.writer(io, &write_buf);
   755	    const writer = &fw.interface;
   756	    try writer.writeAll(bytes);
   757	    try writer.flush();
   758	}
   759	
   760	/// Monotonic ns timestamp via posix clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC).
   761	/// Zig 0.16 moved std.time.Timer/Instant behind the std.Io abstraction;
   762	/// going direct to libc avoids threading an Io instance through the CLI.
   763	fn nowNs() u64 {
   764	    var ts: std.c.timespec = undefined;
   765	    _ = std.c.clock_gettime(std.c.CLOCK.MONOTONIC, &ts);
   766	    return @as(u64, @intCast(ts.sec)) * 1_000_000_000 +
   767	        @as(u64, @intCast(ts.nsec));
   768	}
   769	
   770	fn msBetween(start_ns: u64, end_ns: u64) f64 {
   771	    return @as(f64, @floatFromInt(end_ns - start_ns)) / 1e6;
   772	}
   773	
   774	fn usage(line: []const u8) error{MissingArg} {
   775	    std.debug.print("usage: {s}\n", .{line});
   776	    return error.MissingArg;
   777	}

   779	fn printUsage() void {
   780	    std.debug.print(
   781	        \\tac — Tachiom (arxiv 2604.28142v1) reimplementation in Zig.
   782	        \\
   783	        \\Usage:
   784	        \\  tac index  <tokens.bin> <out.tac> [--kappa N] [--seed N]
   785	        \\                                    [--mu N] [--tau N] [--epsilon N] [--theta N]
   786	        \\  tac search <index.tac> <queries.bin> [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F] [--top-k N]
   787	        \\  tac eval   <index.tac> <queries.bin> <qrels.tsv>
   788	        \\                                       [--metric mrr|success] [--k N]
   789	        \\                                       [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F]
   790	        \\  tac bench  <index.tac> <queries.bin> <qrels.tsv> [--metric mrr|success]
   791	        \\                                       [--out results.csv] [--dataset NAME]
   792	        \\
   793	        \\Defaults: kappa=4096, seed=42, kappa-c=80, kappa-d=1000, top-k=10,
   794	        \\metric=mrr, k=10. `tac bench` runs the paper §6 (κ_c × κ_d × α) sweep.
   795	        \\
   796	        \\qrels.tsv format (any of):
   797	        \\  qid<TAB>doc_id
   798	        \\  qid<TAB>doc_id<TAB>rel
   799	        \\  qid<TAB>iter<TAB>doc_id<TAB>rel        (TREC; iter ignored)
   800	        \\
   801	        \\qids: tac eval/bench reads <queries.bin>.qids (little-endian u32
   802	        \\sidecar) when present; otherwise falls back to 1-indexed sequential
   803	        \\qids matching hand-built smoke fixtures.
   804	        \\
   805	        \\Paper-strict μ=128, τ=256, ε=4, θ=39 (override only when corpus
   806	        \\vocabulary diversity exceeds n_tokens/θ — e.g. Czech multilingual).
   807	        \\
   808	    , .{});
   809	}
