   254	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   255	// `tac eval <index.tac> <queries.bin> <qrels.tsv>
   256	//           [--metric {mrr,success}] [--k N]
   257	//           [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F]`
   258	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   259	
   260	const MetricChoice = enum { mrr, success };
   261	
   262	fn cmdEval(iter: *std.process.Args.Iterator, gpa: Allocator, io: std.Io, cwd: std.Io.Dir) !void {
   263	    var index_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   264	    var queries_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   265	    var qrels_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   266	    var metric: MetricChoice = .mrr;
   267	    var k: u32 = 10;
   268	    var kappa_c: u32 = 80;
   269	    var kappa_d: u32 = 1000;
   270	    var alpha: ?f32 = null;
   271	    var min_rel: i32 = 1;
   272	    var positional: u32 = 0;
   273	
   274	    while (iter.next()) |arg| {
   275	        if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--metric")) {
   276	            const v = iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg;
   277	            if (std.mem.eql(u8, v, "mrr")) {
   278	                metric = .mrr;
   279	            } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, v, "success")) {
   280	                metric = .success;
   281	            } else {
   282	                std.debug.print("unknown metric: {s} (expected mrr|success)\n", .{v});
   283	                return error.UnknownMetric;
   284	            }
   285	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--k")) {
   286	            k = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   287	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--kappa-c")) {
   288	            kappa_c = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   289	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--kappa-d")) {
   290	            kappa_d = try std.fmt.parseInt(u32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   291	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--alpha")) {
   292	            alpha = try std.fmt.parseFloat(f32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg);
   293	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--min-rel")) {
   294	            min_rel = try std.fmt.parseInt(i32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   295	        } else if (std.mem.startsWith(u8, arg, "--")) {
   296	            std.debug.print("unknown flag: {s}\n", .{arg});
   297	            return error.UnknownFlag;
   298	        } else if (positional == 0) {
   299	            index_path = arg;
   300	            positional += 1;
   301	        } else if (positional == 1) {
   302	            queries_path = arg;
   303	            positional += 1;
   304	        } else if (positional == 2) {
   305	            qrels_path = arg;
   306	            positional += 1;
   307	        } else return error.TooManyPositional;
   308	    }
   309	
   310	    const ip = index_path orelse return usage(eval_usage_line);
   311	    const qp = queries_path orelse return usage(eval_usage_line);
   312	    const rp = qrels_path orelse return usage(eval_usage_line);
   313	
   314	    // Load index.
   315	    const idx_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, ip, gpa);
   316	    defer gpa.free(idx_raw);
   317	    const idx_bytes: []align(8) u8 = @alignCast(idx_raw);
   318	    var index = try tac.index.storage.parse(idx_bytes, gpa);
   319	    defer index.deinit(gpa);
   320	
   321	    // Load queries (token dump). For `tac eval` we map each query by its
   322	    // 0-based index in the dump → qid = index+1, mirroring the tac search
   323	    // output convention. The qids sidecar is optional here; if it exists we
   324	    // honour it, otherwise we fall back to 1-indexed sequential qids.
   325	    const q_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, qp, gpa);
   326	    defer gpa.free(q_raw);
   327	    const q_bytes: []align(8) const u8 = @alignCast(q_raw);
   328	    const queries = try tac.io.token_dump.parseBytes(q_bytes);
   329	
   330	    if (queries.dim != index.header.dim) {
   331	        std.debug.print(
   332	            "dim mismatch: queries dim={d} != index dim={d}\n",
   333	            .{ queries.dim, index.header.dim },
   334	        );
   335	        return error.DimMismatch;
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    const qids = try loadOrSyntheticQids(io, cwd, qp, queries.n_docs, gpa);
   339	    defer gpa.free(qids);
   340	
   341	    // Load qrels (CLI variant: 2/3/4 columns).
   342	    const qrels_raw = try slurpFileBytes(io, cwd, rp, gpa);
   343	    defer gpa.free(qrels_raw);
   344	    var qrels_tbl = try tac.eval.qrels.parseBytes(qrels_raw, min_rel, gpa);
   345	    defer qrels_tbl.deinit(gpa);
   346	
   347	    // Run retrieval per query, score with the chosen metric.
   348	    const sp: tac.retrieval.search.SearchParams = .{
   349	        .kappa_c = kappa_c,
   350	        .kappa_d = kappa_d,
   351	        .alpha = alpha,
   352	        .top_k = k,
   353	    };
   354	
   355	    var n_scored: u32 = 0;
   356	    var n_skipped_no_qrels: u32 = 0;
   357	    var score_sum: f64 = 0.0;
   358	    const t0 = nowNs();
   359	
   360	    var d: u64 = 0;
   361	    while (d < queries.n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   362	        const range = queries.docTokenRange(d);
   363	        const n_q: u32 = @intCast(range[1] - range[0]);
   364	        if (n_q == 0) continue;
   365	
   366	        const qid = qids[@intCast(d)];
   367	        const rels = qrels_tbl.forQuery(qid);
   368	        if (rels.len == 0) {
   369	            n_skipped_no_qrels += 1;
   370	            continue;
   371	        }
   372	
   373	        const start_off: usize = @intCast(range[0] * queries.dim);
   374	        const end_off: usize = @intCast(range[1] * queries.dim);
   375	        const query_tokens = queries.vectors[start_off..end_off];
   376	
   377	        const hits = try tac.retrieval.search.search(&index, query_tokens, n_q, sp, gpa);
   378	        defer gpa.free(hits);
   379	
   380	        const want: usize = @min(hits.len, @as(usize, k));
   381	        const ranking = try gpa.alloc(u32, want);
   382	        defer gpa.free(ranking);
   383	        for (hits[0..want], 0..) |h, i| ranking[i] = h.doc_id;
   384	
   385	        const m: f32 = switch (metric) {
   386	            .mrr => tac.eval.metrics.mrrAt(ranking, rels, k),
   387	            .success => tac.eval.metrics.successAt(ranking, rels, k),
   388	        };
   389	        score_sum += m;
   390	        n_scored += 1;
   391	    }
   392	
   393	    const t1 = nowNs();
   394	    const elapsed_ms = msBetween(t0, t1);
   395	
   396	    const metric_label: []const u8 = switch (metric) {
   397	        .mrr => "mrr",
   398	        .success => "success",
   399	    };
   400	
   401	    if (n_scored == 0) {
   402	        std.debug.print(
   403	            "tac eval: no queries scored — checked {d} queries, none had matching qrels (qids in queries.bin.qids vs qrels.tsv don't overlap?)\n",
   404	            .{queries.n_docs},
   405	        );
   406	        return error.NoOverlap;
   407	    }
   408	
   409	    const avg: f64 = score_sum / @as(f64, @floatFromInt(n_scored));
   410	    std.debug.print(
   411	        "tac eval: n_queries={d} metric={s}@{d} score={d:.4} elapsed={d:.1}ms (skipped_no_qrels={d}, kappa_c={d}, kappa_d={d})\n",
   412	        .{ n_scored, metric_label, k, avg, elapsed_ms, n_skipped_no_qrels, kappa_c, kappa_d },
   413	    );
   414	}
   415	
   416	const eval_usage_line: []const u8 =
   417	    "tac eval <index.tac> <queries.bin> <qrels.tsv> [--metric mrr|success] [--k N] [--kappa-c N] [--kappa-d N] [--alpha F]";
   418	
   419	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   420	// `tac bench <index.tac> <queries.bin> <qrels.tsv> [--metric mrr|success] [--out PATH]`
   421	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   422	
   423	fn cmdBench(iter: *std.process.Args.Iterator, gpa: Allocator, io: std.Io, cwd: std.Io.Dir) !void {
   424	    var index_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   425	    var queries_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   426	    var qrels_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   427	    var out_path: ?[]const u8 = null;
   428	    var metric: MetricChoice = .mrr;
   429	    var dataset_label: []const u8 = "tac-bench";
   430	    var min_rel: i32 = 1;
   431	    var positional: u32 = 0;
   432	
   433	    while (iter.next()) |arg| {
   434	        if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--out")) {
   435	            out_path = iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg;
   436	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--metric")) {
   437	            const v = iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg;
   438	            if (std.mem.eql(u8, v, "mrr")) {
   439	                metric = .mrr;
   440	            } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, v, "success")) {
   441	                metric = .success;
   442	            } else {
   443	                std.debug.print("unknown metric: {s} (expected mrr|success)\n", .{v});
   444	                return error.UnknownMetric;
   445	            }
   446	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--dataset")) {
   447	            dataset_label = iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg;
   448	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, arg, "--min-rel")) {
   449	            min_rel = try std.fmt.parseInt(i32, iter.next() orelse return error.MissingArg, 10);
   450	        } else if (std.mem.startsWith(u8, arg, "--")) {
   451	            std.debug.print("unknown flag: {s}\n", .{arg});
   452	            return error.UnknownFlag;
   453	        } else if (positional == 0) {
   454	            index_path = arg;
   455	            positional += 1;
   456	        } else if (positional == 1) {
   457	            queries_path = arg;
   458	            positional += 1;
   459	        } else if (positional == 2) {
   460	            qrels_path = arg;
   461	            positional += 1;
   462	        } else return error.TooManyPositional;
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    const ip = index_path orelse return usage(bench_usage_line);
   466	    const qp = queries_path orelse return usage(bench_usage_line);
   467	    const rp = qrels_path orelse return usage(bench_usage_line);
   468	
   469	    // Load index.
   470	    const idx_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, ip, gpa);
   471	    defer gpa.free(idx_raw);
   472	    const idx_bytes: []align(8) u8 = @alignCast(idx_raw);
   473	    var index = try tac.index.storage.parse(idx_bytes, gpa);
   474	    defer index.deinit(gpa);
   475	
   476	    // Load queries.
   477	    const q_raw = try slurpAligned8(io, cwd, qp, gpa);
   478	    defer gpa.free(q_raw);
   479	    const q_bytes: []align(8) const u8 = @alignCast(q_raw);
   480	    const queries = try tac.io.token_dump.parseBytes(q_bytes);
   481	
   482	    if (queries.dim != index.header.dim) {
   483	        std.debug.print(
   484	            "dim mismatch: queries dim={d} != index dim={d}\n",
   485	            .{ queries.dim, index.header.dim },
   486	        );
   487	        return error.DimMismatch;
   488	    }
   489	
   490	    const qids = try loadOrSyntheticQids(io, cwd, qp, queries.n_docs, gpa);
   491	    defer gpa.free(qids);
   492	
   493	    // Load qrels.
   494	    const qrels_raw = try slurpFileBytes(io, cwd, rp, gpa);
   495	    defer gpa.free(qrels_raw);
   496	    var qrels_tbl = try tac.eval.qrels.parseBytes(qrels_raw, min_rel, gpa);
   497	    defer qrels_tbl.deinit(gpa);
   498	
   499	    // Build a bench.QueryPack from the queries with matching qrels.
   500	    var pack_n_q: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
   501	    defer pack_n_q.deinit(gpa);
   502	    var pack_q_offsets: std.ArrayList(u64) = .empty;
   503	    defer pack_q_offsets.deinit(gpa);
   504	    var pack_tokens: std.ArrayList(f32) = .empty;
   505	    defer pack_tokens.deinit(gpa);
   506	    var pack_qrels: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
   507	    defer pack_qrels.deinit(gpa);
   508	    var pack_qrels_lo: std.ArrayList(u64) = .empty;
   509	    defer pack_qrels_lo.deinit(gpa);
   510	
   511	    try pack_q_offsets.append(gpa, 0);
   512	    try pack_qrels_lo.append(gpa, 0);
   513	
   514	    var d: u64 = 0;
   515	    while (d < queries.n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   516	        const range = queries.docTokenRange(d);
   517	        const n_q: u32 = @intCast(range[1] - range[0]);
   518	        if (n_q == 0) continue;
   519	        const qid = qids[@intCast(d)];
   520	        const rels = qrels_tbl.forQuery(qid);

exec

     1	//! benchmarks/microbench_vec.zig — kernel-level timing for src/util/vec.zig.
     2	//!
     3	//! Establishes baseline / post-specialization GFLOPs for the dot/l2sq/normalize
     4	//! kernels at the dims that actually appear in this codebase: 2 and 4 (PQ
     5	//! subspaces), 64 (jina-colbert-v2-64), 128 (ColBERTv2.0). We also bench dim=96
     6	//! as an unspecialized fallback control.
     7	//!
     8	//! Usage: `zig build -Doptimize=ReleaseFast run-microbench_vec`
     9	//!
    10	//! plan-11 (post-hackathon): we want to know how much the comptime
    11	//! specialization buys us over the runtime-`lane_count` generic path before
    12	//! claiming a perf win.
    13	
    14	const std = @import("std");
    15	const tac = @import("tac");
    16	const vec = tac.util.vec;
    17	
    18	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    19	
    20	/// Monotonic ns timestamp; Zig 0.16 moved std.time.Timer/Instant behind std.Io,
    21	/// so we go direct to libc (same pattern as src/main.zig:763).
    22	fn nowNs() u64 {
    23	    var ts: std.c.timespec = undefined;
    24	    _ = std.c.clock_gettime(std.c.CLOCK.MONOTONIC, &ts);
    25	    return @as(u64, @intCast(ts.sec)) * 1_000_000_000 +
    26	        @as(u64, @intCast(ts.nsec));
    27	}
    28	
    29	/// One bench result row.
    30	const Row = struct {
    31	    name: []const u8,
    32	    dim: usize,
    33	    iters: usize,
    34	    ns_per_op: f64,
    35	    gflops: f64,
    36	};
    37	
    38	fn fillRamp(buf: []f32, offset: f32) void {
    39	    for (buf, 0..) |*x, i| {
    40	        x.* = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(i)) * 0.001 + offset;
    41	    }
    42	}
    43	
    44	fn benchDot(allocator: Allocator, comptime label: []const u8, dim: usize, iters: usize) !Row {
    45	    // Allocate a 1024-row pool so we exercise different inputs each iteration;
    46	    // a single pair lets LLVM hoist `vec.dot(a,b)` out of the loop now that the
    47	    // dispatcher is `inline`. Cycling through cache-resident rows defeats LICM
    48	    // without measuring memory bandwidth.
    49	    const pool_rows: usize = 1024;
    50	    const buf = try allocator.alloc(f32, dim * pool_rows);
    51	    defer allocator.free(buf);
    52	    fillRamp(buf, 0.1);
    53	
    54	    var warm: usize = 0;
    55	    var sink: f32 = 0.0;
    56	    while (warm < @max(iters / 100, 1)) : (warm += 1) {
    57	        const i = warm & (pool_rows - 1);
    58	        const j = (warm + 1) & (pool_rows - 1);
    59	        sink += try vec.dot(buf[i * dim ..][0..dim], buf[j * dim ..][0..dim]);
    60	    }
    61	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(sink);
    62	
    63	    const t0 = nowNs();
    64	    var k: usize = 0;
    65	    var acc: f32 = 0.0;
    66	    while (k < iters) : (k += 1) {
    67	        const i = k & (pool_rows - 1);
    68	        const j = (k + 1) & (pool_rows - 1);
    69	        acc += try vec.dot(buf[i * dim ..][0..dim], buf[j * dim ..][0..dim]);
    70	    }
    71	    const ns: u64 = nowNs() - t0;
    72	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(acc);
    73	
    74	    const ns_per_op = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(ns)) / @as(f64, @floatFromInt(iters));
    75	    // dot = dim multiplies + (dim-1) adds ≈ 2*dim FLOPs.
    76	    const flops_per_op = 2.0 * @as(f64, @floatFromInt(dim));
    77	    const gflops = flops_per_op / ns_per_op;
    78	    return .{ .name = label, .dim = dim, .iters = iters, .ns_per_op = ns_per_op, .gflops = gflops };
    79	}
    80	
    81	fn benchL2sq(allocator: Allocator, comptime label: []const u8, dim: usize, iters: usize) !Row {
    82	    const pool_rows: usize = 1024;
    83	    const buf = try allocator.alloc(f32, dim * pool_rows);
    84	    defer allocator.free(buf);
    85	    fillRamp(buf, 0.1);
    86	
    87	    var warm: usize = 0;
    88	    var sink: f32 = 0.0;
    89	    while (warm < @max(iters / 100, 1)) : (warm += 1) {
    90	        const i = warm & (pool_rows - 1);
    91	        const j = (warm + 1) & (pool_rows - 1);
    92	        sink += try vec.l2sq(buf[i * dim ..][0..dim], buf[j * dim ..][0..dim]);
    93	    }
    94	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(sink);
    95	
    96	    const t0 = nowNs();
    97	    var k: usize = 0;
    98	    var acc: f32 = 0.0;
    99	    while (k < iters) : (k += 1) {
   100	        const i = k & (pool_rows - 1);
   101	        const j = (k + 1) & (pool_rows - 1);
   102	        acc += try vec.l2sq(buf[i * dim ..][0..dim], buf[j * dim ..][0..dim]);
   103	    }
   104	    const ns: u64 = nowNs() - t0;
   105	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(acc);
   106	
   107	    const ns_per_op = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(ns)) / @as(f64, @floatFromInt(iters));
   108	    // l2sq = dim subtracts + dim multiplies + (dim-1) adds ≈ 3*dim FLOPs.
   109	    const flops_per_op = 3.0 * @as(f64, @floatFromInt(dim));
   110	    const gflops = flops_per_op / ns_per_op;
   111	    return .{ .name = label, .dim = dim, .iters = iters, .ns_per_op = ns_per_op, .gflops = gflops };
   112	}
   113	
   114	fn benchNormalize(allocator: Allocator, comptime label: []const u8, dim: usize, iters: usize) !Row {
   115	    // We mutate in place; reset every iteration would dominate. Instead, fill
   116	    // once, time the normalize, then refill. Refill cost is amortised across
   117	    // iters but not zero — so this is a slight pessimism. Acceptable for a
   118	    // relative comparison.
   119	    const v = try allocator.alloc(f32, dim);
   120	    defer allocator.free(v);
   121	
   122	    const t0 = nowNs();
   123	    var k: usize = 0;
   124	    while (k < iters) : (k += 1) {
   125	        // Refill is part of the timed loop so successive normalize calls
   126	        // see a non-unit vector. Subtract a small ramp-fill cost in analysis
   127	        // (it's identical across baseline + specialized so cancels for ratio).
   128	        fillRamp(v, 0.5);
   129	        try vec.normalizeInPlace(v);
   130	    }
   131	    const ns: u64 = nowNs() - t0;
   132	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(v[0]);
   133	
   134	    const ns_per_op = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(ns)) / @as(f64, @floatFromInt(iters));
   135	    // normalize: 2*dim multiplies + ~dim adds + 1 sqrt + 1 divide + dim multiplies
   136	    // ≈ 4*dim FLOPs (sqrt/div ignored).
   137	    const flops_per_op = 4.0 * @as(f64, @floatFromInt(dim));
   138	    const gflops = flops_per_op / ns_per_op;
   139	    return .{ .name = label, .dim = dim, .iters = iters, .ns_per_op = ns_per_op, .gflops = gflops };
   140	}
   141	
   142	fn printRow(row: Row) void {
   143	    std.debug.print(
   144	        "  {s:<14} dim={d:<4}  iters={d:<10}  {d:>9.2} ns/op   {d:>7.3} GFLOP/s\n",
   145	        .{ row.name, row.dim, row.iters, row.ns_per_op, row.gflops },
   146	    );
   147	}
   148	
   149	/// Mimic the inner argmin loop that kmeans.fit (assignment phase) does on
   150	/// every point: for each centroid, compute l2sq(point, centroid); track
   151	/// argmin. n_centroids=256, n_points=1000 — small enough to fit in cache,
   152	/// large enough to amortize loop overhead. Reports median ns per point.
   153	fn benchKmeansAssign(allocator: Allocator, comptime label: []const u8, dim: usize, k: usize, n_points: usize) !Row {
   154	    const points = try allocator.alloc(f32, dim * n_points);
   155	    defer allocator.free(points);
   156	    const cents = try allocator.alloc(f32, dim * k);
   157	    defer allocator.free(cents);
   158	
   159	    // Deterministic synthetic fill — we just need data, not realism.
   160	    for (points, 0..) |*p, i| p.* = @sin(@as(f32, @floatFromInt(i)) * 0.013) * 0.5;
   161	    for (cents, 0..) |*c, i| c.* = @cos(@as(f32, @floatFromInt(i)) * 0.017) * 0.5;
   162	
   163	    // Warm-up.
   164	    var warm_sink: usize = 0;
   165	    {
   166	        var p: usize = 0;
   167	        while (p < n_points) : (p += 1) {
   168	            const point = points[p * dim ..][0..dim];
   169	            var best_d: f32 = std.math.inf(f32);
   170	            var best_c: usize = 0;
   171	            var c: usize = 0;
   172	            while (c < k) : (c += 1) {
   173	                const d = try vec.l2sq(point, cents[c * dim ..][0..dim]);
   174	                if (d < best_d) {
   175	                    best_d = d;
   176	                    best_c = c;
   177	                }
   178	            }
   179	            warm_sink +%= best_c;
   180	        }
   181	    }
   182	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(warm_sink);
   183	
   184	    const t0 = nowNs();
   185	    var sink: usize = 0;
   186	    var p: usize = 0;
   187	    while (p < n_points) : (p += 1) {
   188	        const point = points[p * dim ..][0..dim];
   189	        var best_d: f32 = std.math.inf(f32);
   190	        var best_c: usize = 0;
   191	        var c: usize = 0;
   192	        while (c < k) : (c += 1) {
   193	            const d = try vec.l2sq(point, cents[c * dim ..][0..dim]);
   194	            if (d < best_d) {
   195	                best_d = d;
   196	                best_c = c;
   197	            }
   198	        }
   199	        sink +%= best_c;
   200	    }
   201	    const ns: u64 = nowNs() - t0;
   202	    std.mem.doNotOptimizeAway(sink);
   203	
   204	    const ns_per_op = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(ns)) / @as(f64, @floatFromInt(n_points));
   205	    // Per point: k * (3*dim) FLOPs.
   206	    const flops_per_op = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(k)) * 3.0 * @as(f64, @floatFromInt(dim));
   207	    const gflops = flops_per_op / ns_per_op;
   208	    return .{ .name = label, .dim = dim, .iters = n_points, .ns_per_op = ns_per_op, .gflops = gflops };
   209	}
   210	
   211	/// Mirrors pq.encode's exact call shape: l2sq(sub, codebook[m, c]) with
   212	/// `sub_dim` read from a struct field (not a stack-local). Tests whether
   213	/// plan-11's `pub inline fn` dispatcher is enough or if we need exact-dim
   214	/// helpers exposed for callers that hold dim in a runtime field.
   215	const MockPQ = struct {
   216	    sub_dim: u32,
   217	    codebook: []const f32,
   218	};
   219	
   220	fn benchPqEncodePattern(allocator: Allocator, comptime label: []const u8, sub_dim: u32, n_subspaces: usize, n_centroids: usize, n_tokens: usize) !Row {
   221	    const dim = @as(usize, sub_dim) * n_subspaces;
   222	    const cb_len = @as(usize, sub_dim) * n_centroids * n_subspaces;
   223	    const codebook = try allocator.alloc(f32, cb_len);
   224	    defer allocator.free(codebook);
   225	    for (codebook, 0..) |*x, i| x.* = @sin(@as(f32, @floatFromInt(i)) * 0.011);
   226	    const residuals = try allocator.alloc(f32, dim * n_tokens);
   227	    defer allocator.free(residuals);
   228	    for (residuals, 0..) |*x, i| x.* = @cos(@as(f32, @floatFromInt(i)) * 0.013);
   229	
   230	    const pq = MockPQ{ .sub_dim = sub_dim, .codebook = codebook };
   231	    // Indirection through a heap pointer mirrors `*const PQ` in pq.zig:59.
   232	    const pq_ptr: *const MockPQ = &pq;
   233	
   234	    var warm_sink: usize = 0;
   235	    {
   236	        var t: usize = 0;
   237	        while (t < n_tokens / 100 + 1) : (t += 1) {
   238	            const r = residuals[t * dim ..][0..dim];
   239	            var m: usize = 0;
   240	            while (m < n_subspaces) : (m += 1) {

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! src/retrieval/bench.zig — benchmark plumbing & smoke test (paper §7-9).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever.
     4	//!
     5	//! The real per-dataset harnesses live under `benchmarks/` and operate on
     6	//! disk-backed inputs (encoded queries.bin + qrels.tsv from `tools/encode.py`).
     7	//! This in-tree module hosts the reusable sweep core and a synthetic-fixture
     8	//! smoke test that exercises the full search → metrics → latency pipeline
     9	//! on every `zig build test`, guarding against integration regressions.
    10	//!
    11	//! Sweep grid is fixed by the paper:
    12	//!   κ_c ∈ {15, 20, 40, 80, 100, 120}      (paper §6, retrieval grid)
    13	//!   κ_d ∈ {250, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000}
    14	//!   α   ∈ {null, 0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50}
    15	//!
    16	//! Single-thread per paper §9.
    17	
    18	const std = @import("std");
    19	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    20	
    21	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    22	const storage = @import("../index/storage.zig");
    23	const search_mod = @import("search.zig");
    24	const gather = @import("gather.zig");
    25	const prune = @import("prune.zig");
    26	const refine = @import("refine.zig");
    27	const metrics = @import("../eval/metrics.zig");
    28	const latency = @import("../eval/latency.zig");
    29	
    30	/// Monotonic ns timestamp via posix `clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)`. Zig
    31	/// 0.16 moved std.time.Timer behind the std.Io abstraction; this avoids
    32	/// threading an Io instance through the benchmark plumbing.
    33	fn nowNs() u64 {
    34	    var ts: std.c.timespec = undefined;
    35	    _ = std.c.clock_gettime(std.c.CLOCK.MONOTONIC, &ts);
    36	    return @as(u64, @intCast(ts.sec)) * 1_000_000_000 +
    37	        @as(u64, @intCast(ts.nsec));
    38	}
    39	
    40	const Stopwatch = struct {
    41	    last: u64,
    42	    fn start() Stopwatch {
    43	        return .{ .last = nowNs() };
    44	    }
    45	    fn lap(self: *Stopwatch) u64 {
    46	        const now = nowNs();
    47	        const d = now - self.last;
    48	        self.last = now;
    49	        return d;
    50	    }
    51	    fn read(self: *const Stopwatch) u64 {
    52	        return nowNs() - self.last;
    53	    }
    54	};
    55	
    56	/// Operating point row: one (κ_c, κ_d, α) cell × (n_queries, dataset).
    57	pub const SweepRow = struct {
    58	    kappa_c: u32,
    59	    kappa_d: u32,
    60	    alpha_x100: i16, // -1 sentinel for null, else round(alpha*100)
    61	    n_queries: u32,
    62	    /// Dataset metric: MRR@10 (MS MARCO) or Success@5 (LoTTE) — caller picks
    63	    /// the metric function; the row carries the result undifferentiated.
    64	    quality: f32,
    65	    avg_total_ms: f64,
    66	    p50_total_ms: f64,
    67	    p95_total_ms: f64,
    68	    avg_gather_ms: f64,
    69	    avg_prune_ms: f64,
    70	    avg_table_ms: f64,
    71	    avg_refine_ms: f64,
    72	};
    73	
    74	pub const QueryPack = struct {
    75	    /// `n_q[i]` = query length (number of tokens) for query i.
    76	    n_q: []const u32,
    77	    /// `q_offsets[q+1] - q_offsets[q]` = `n_q[q] * dim`. Float offsets into
    78	    /// `tokens` for query q.
    79	    q_offsets: []const u64,
    80	    /// All query tokens, row-major. Length sum_i n_q[i] * dim.
    81	    tokens: []const f32,
    82	    /// Per-query relevance lists (qrels). `qrels_lo[q+1] - qrels_lo[q]` = #
    83	    /// relevant doc IDs for query q; `qrels[qrels_lo[q]..qrels_lo[q+1]]` are
    84	    /// those doc IDs.
    85	    qrels: []const u32,
    86	    qrels_lo: []const u64,
    87	
    88	    pub fn nQueries(self: QueryPack) usize {
    89	        return self.n_q.len;
    90	    }
    91	};
    92	
    93	pub const MetricKind = enum { mrr_at_10, success_at_5 };
    94	
    95	/// Run one (κ_c, κ_d, α) cell across all queries. Returns the aggregated
    96	/// row. `per_token_max_buf` and `centroid_buf` are caller-provided scratch
    97	/// shared across all queries (avoid reallocating per query).
    98	pub fn runCell(
    99	    index: *const storage.Index,
   100	    pack: QueryPack,
   101	    kappa_c: u32,
   102	    kappa_d: u32,
   103	    alpha: ?f32,
   104	    metric: MetricKind,
   105	    gpa: Allocator,
   106	) !SweepRow {
   107	    const n_queries = pack.nQueries();
   108	    var quality_sum: f64 = 0.0;
   109	    var timings: std.ArrayList(latency.StageTimings) = .empty;
   110	    defer timings.deinit(gpa);
   111	    try timings.ensureTotalCapacity(gpa, n_queries);
   112	
   113	    const top_k: u32 = switch (metric) {
   114	        .mrr_at_10 => 10,
   115	        .success_at_5 => 5,
   116	    };
   117	
   118	    var q: usize = 0;
   119	    while (q < n_queries) : (q += 1) {
   120	        const lo: usize = @intCast(pack.q_offsets[q]);
   121	        const hi: usize = @intCast(pack.q_offsets[q + 1]);
   122	        const q_tokens = pack.tokens[lo..hi];
   123	        const n_q_q: u32 = pack.n_q[q];
   124	
   125	        // Time the full search call as a single bucket. Per-stage breakdown
   126	        // is approximated below by re-running each stage with explicit
   127	        // timers — this matches what the paper reports (avg per-stage ms).
   128	        var t = latency.StageTimings{};
   129	        const timer_total = Stopwatch.start();
   130	
   131	        // Per-stage timing: gather/prune/table/refine with explicit borders.
   132	        var sub_timer = Stopwatch.start();
   133	
   134	        const candidates = try gather.gather(index, q_tokens, n_q_q, .{ .kappa_c = kappa_c }, gpa);
   135	        defer gpa.free(candidates);
   136	        t.gather_ns = sub_timer.lap();
   137	
   138	        const survivors = prune.prune(candidates, .{ .kappa_d = kappa_d, .alpha = alpha });
   139	        t.prune_ns = sub_timer.lap();
   140	
   141	        if (survivors.len > 0) {
   142	            const table_len: usize =
   143	                @as(usize, constants.PQ_M) * @as(usize, constants.PQ_CENTROIDS) * @as(usize, n_q_q);
   144	            const table = try gpa.alloc(f32, table_len);
   145	            defer gpa.free(table);
   146	            try index.pq.buildDistanceTable(q_tokens, n_q_q, table);
   147	            t.table_build_ns = sub_timer.lap();
   148	
   149	            const per_token_max = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_q_q);
   150	            defer gpa.free(per_token_max);
   151	
   152	            // Score every survivor and collect ranking up to top_k.
   153	            const refined = try gpa.alloc(search_mod.ScoredDoc, survivors.len);
   154	            defer gpa.free(refined);
   155	            for (survivors, 0..) |s, k| {
   156	                refined[k] = .{
   157	                    .doc_id = s.doc_id,
   158	                    .score = try refine.refine(index, table, q_tokens, n_q_q, s, per_token_max),
   159	                };
   160	            }
   161	            std.sort.pdq(search_mod.ScoredDoc, refined, {}, scoredDescending);
   162	            t.refine_ns = sub_timer.lap();
   163	
   164	            // Build a u32 ranking up to top_k for the metric.
   165	            const want: usize = @min(refined.len, @as(usize, top_k));
   166	            const ranking = try gpa.alloc(u32, want);
   167	            defer gpa.free(ranking);
   168	            for (refined[0..want], 0..) |r, idx| ranking[idx] = r.doc_id;
   169	
   170	            const qr_lo: usize = @intCast(pack.qrels_lo[q]);
   171	            const qr_hi: usize = @intCast(pack.qrels_lo[q + 1]);
   172	            const qrels_q = pack.qrels[qr_lo..qr_hi];
   173	            const m: f32 = switch (metric) {
   174	                .mrr_at_10 => metrics.mrrAt(ranking, qrels_q, 10),
   175	                .success_at_5 => metrics.successAt(ranking, qrels_q, 5),
   176	            };
   177	            quality_sum += m;
   178	        }
   179	
   180	        t.total_ns = timer_total.read();
   181	        try timings.append(gpa, t);
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    const rep = latency.report(timings.items);
   185	    const alpha_x100: i16 = if (alpha) |a| @intFromFloat(@round(a * 100.0)) else -1;
   186	    return .{
   187	        .kappa_c = kappa_c,
   188	        .kappa_d = kappa_d,
   189	        .alpha_x100 = alpha_x100,
   190	        .n_queries = @intCast(n_queries),
   191	        .quality = @floatCast(quality_sum / @as(f64, @floatFromInt(n_queries))),
   192	        .avg_total_ms = rep.avg_total_ms,
   193	        .p50_total_ms = rep.p50_total_ms,
   194	        .p95_total_ms = rep.p95_total_ms,
   195	        .avg_gather_ms = rep.avg_gather_ms,
   196	        .avg_prune_ms = rep.avg_prune_ms,
   197	        .avg_table_ms = rep.avg_table_ms,
   198	        .avg_refine_ms = rep.avg_refine_ms,
   199	    };
   200	}
   201	
   202	fn scoredDescending(_: void, a: search_mod.ScoredDoc, b: search_mod.ScoredDoc) bool {
   203	    return a.score > b.score;
   204	}
   205	
   206	/// Paper §6 retrieval grid.
   207	pub const kappa_c_grid: []const u32 = &.{ 15, 20, 40, 80, 100, 120 };
   208	pub const kappa_d_grid: []const u32 = &.{ 250, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000 };
   209	pub const alpha_grid: []const ?f32 = &.{ null, 0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50 };
   210	
   211	/// Run the full Cartesian sweep, calling `out_callback` for every cell. The
   212	/// callback is the integration boundary with the CSV writer in the
   213	/// per-dataset harness — we keep this module IO-free so it's testable.
   214	pub fn runSweep(
   215	    index: *const storage.Index,
   216	    pack: QueryPack,
   217	    metric: MetricKind,
   218	    out_callback: *const fn (row: SweepRow, ctx: *anyopaque) anyerror!void,
   219	    ctx: *anyopaque,
   220	    gpa: Allocator,
   221	) !void {
   222	    for (kappa_c_grid) |kc| {
   223	        for (kappa_d_grid) |kd| {
   224	            for (alpha_grid) |a| {
   225	                const row = try runCell(index, pack, kc, kd, a, metric, gpa);
   226	                try out_callback(row, ctx);
   227	            }
   228	        }
   229	    }
   230	}
   231	
   232	/// Format one CSV row (no trailing newline; caller adds it). Caller-provided
   233	/// `dataset` and `git_sha` strings are prepended for traceability.
   234	pub fn formatCsvRow(
   235	    writer: *std.Io.Writer,
   236	    dataset: []const u8,
   237	    git_sha: []const u8,
   238	    row: SweepRow,
   239	) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
   240	    try writer.print(
   241	        "{s},{d},{d},{},{d},{d:.6},{d:.4},{d:.4},{d:.4},{d:.4},{d:.4},{d:.4},{d:.4},{s}",
   242	        .{
   243	            dataset,
   244	            row.kappa_c,
   245	            row.kappa_d,
   246	            row.alpha_x100, // -1 = "no CP" sentinel
   247	            row.n_queries,
   248	            row.quality,
   249	            row.avg_total_ms,
   250	            row.p50_total_ms,
   251	            row.p95_total_ms,
   252	            row.avg_gather_ms,
   253	            row.avg_prune_ms,
   254	            row.avg_table_ms,
   255	            row.avg_refine_ms,
   256	            git_sha,
   257	        },
   258	    );
   259	}

     1	//! benchmarks/common/runner.zig — compose qrels + queries + index into a
     2	//! `tac.retrieval.bench.runSweep` invocation. Per-dataset entry points
     3	//! (msmarco_v1.zig, lotte_pooled.zig) call `runDatasetSweep`.
     4	
     5	const std = @import("std");
     6	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
     7	
     8	const tac = @import("tac");
     9	const qrels_mod = @import("qrels.zig");
    10	const queries_mod = @import("queries.zig");
    11	
    12	pub const RunError = error{
    13	    NoOverlap,
    14	    ReadFailed,
    15	} || queries_mod.QueriesError || Allocator.Error;
    16	
    17	pub const RunArgs = struct {
    18	    dataset: []const u8, // "msmarco-v1", "lotte-pooled"
    19	    index_path: []const u8,
    20	    queries_path: []const u8,
    21	    qids_path: []const u8,
    22	    qrels_path: []const u8,
    23	    out_csv_path: []const u8,
    24	    git_sha: []const u8,
    25	    metric: tac.retrieval.bench.MetricKind,
    26	    /// rel >= min_rel ⇒ relevant.
    27	    min_rel: i32 = 1,
    28	    /// Pin the benchmark thread to this core (paper §9). Best-effort per OS.
    29	    pin_core: ?u32 = 0,
    30	};
    31	
    32	const CsvCtx = struct {
    33	    writer: *std.Io.Writer,
    34	    dataset: []const u8,
    35	    git_sha: []const u8,
    36	};
    37	
    38	fn writeRowCallback(row: tac.retrieval.bench.SweepRow, ctx: *anyopaque) anyerror!void {
    39	    const c: *CsvCtx = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ctx));
    40	    try tac.retrieval.bench.formatCsvRow(c.writer, c.dataset, c.git_sha, row);
    41	    try c.writer.writeByte('\n');
    42	    try c.writer.flush();
    43	}

    45	/// Read an entire file into a freshly-allocated buffer of exact size.
    46	/// `align_8` requests an 8-aligned allocation (needed for token_dump and
    47	/// storage parsing).
    48	fn slurp(io: std.Io, dir: std.Io.Dir, path: []const u8, gpa: Allocator, comptime align_8: bool) ![]u8 {
    49	    var f = try dir.openFile(io, path, .{});
    50	    defer f.close(io);
    51	    const stat = try f.stat(io);
    52	    const size: usize = std.math.cast(usize, stat.size) orelse return error.ReadFailed;
    53	    const buf: []u8 = if (align_8)
    54	        try gpa.alignedAlloc(u8, .@"8", size)
    55	    else
    56	        try gpa.alloc(u8, size);
    57	    errdefer gpa.free(buf);
    58	
    59	    var read_buf: [16 * 1024]u8 = undefined;
    60	    var fr = f.reader(io, &read_buf);
    61	    const reader = &fr.interface;
    62	    reader.readSliceAll(buf) catch return error.ReadFailed;
    63	    return buf;
    64	}
    65	
    66	/// End-to-end harness: open inputs, fold them into a `bench.QueryPack`, run
    67	/// the paper §6 grid sweep, write a CSV.
    68	pub fn runDatasetSweep(args: RunArgs, gpa: Allocator) !void {
    69	    if (args.pin_core) |c| tac.eval.latency.pinToCore(c);
    70	
    71	    var threaded = std.Io.Threaded.init(gpa, .{});
    72	    defer threaded.deinit();
    73	    const io = threaded.io();
    74	    const cwd = std.Io.Dir.cwd();
    75	
    76	    // ---- Index ----
    77	    const idx_bytes_raw = try slurp(io, cwd, args.index_path, gpa, true);
    78	    defer gpa.free(idx_bytes_raw);
    79	    const idx_bytes: []align(8) u8 = @alignCast(idx_bytes_raw);
    80	    var index = try tac.index.storage.parse(idx_bytes, gpa);
    81	    defer index.deinit(gpa);
    82	
    83	    // ---- Queries + qids ----
    84	    const q_bytes_raw = try slurp(io, cwd, args.queries_path, gpa, true);
    85	    errdefer gpa.free(q_bytes_raw);
    86	    const q_bytes: []align(8) u8 = @alignCast(q_bytes_raw);
    87	    const qids_bytes = try slurp(io, cwd, args.qids_path, gpa, false);
    88	    defer gpa.free(qids_bytes);
    89	    var enc = try queries_mod.loadFromBytes(q_bytes, qids_bytes, gpa);
    90	    defer enc.deinit(gpa);
    91	
    92	    // ---- Qrels ----
    93	    const qrels_bytes = try slurp(io, cwd, args.qrels_path, gpa, false);
    94	    defer gpa.free(qrels_bytes);
    95	    var qrels = try qrels_mod.parseBytes(qrels_bytes, args.min_rel, gpa);
    96	    defer qrels.deinit(gpa);
    97	
    98	    // ---- Build a bench.QueryPack: keep only queries that have qrels. ----
    99	    const dim: u32 = enc.dump.dim;
   100	    const n_q_total: usize = @intCast(enc.nQueries());
   101	
   102	    var pack_n_q: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
   103	    defer pack_n_q.deinit(gpa);
   104	    var pack_q_offsets: std.ArrayList(u64) = .empty;
   105	    defer pack_q_offsets.deinit(gpa);
   106	    var pack_tokens: std.ArrayList(f32) = .empty;
   107	    defer pack_tokens.deinit(gpa);
   108	    var pack_qrels: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
   109	    defer pack_qrels.deinit(gpa);
   110	    var pack_qrels_lo: std.ArrayList(u64) = .empty;
   111	    defer pack_qrels_lo.deinit(gpa);
   112	
   113	    try pack_q_offsets.append(gpa, 0);
   114	    try pack_qrels_lo.append(gpa, 0);
   115	
   116	    var q: usize = 0;
   117	    while (q < n_q_total) : (q += 1) {
   118	        const qid = enc.qids[q];
   119	        const rels = qrels.forQuery(qid);
   120	        if (rels.len == 0) continue;
   121	
   122	        const range = enc.dump.docTokenRange(@intCast(q));
   123	        const tok_lo: usize = @intCast(range[0]);
   124	        const tok_hi: usize = @intCast(range[1]);
   125	        const n_q_q: u32 = @intCast(tok_hi - tok_lo);
   126	        const flat_lo: usize = tok_lo * dim;
   127	        const flat_hi: usize = tok_hi * dim;
   128	
   129	        try pack_n_q.append(gpa, n_q_q);
   130	        try pack_tokens.appendSlice(gpa, enc.dump.vectors[flat_lo..flat_hi]);
   131	        try pack_q_offsets.append(gpa, @as(u64, pack_tokens.items.len));
   132	        try pack_qrels.appendSlice(gpa, rels);
   133	        try pack_qrels_lo.append(gpa, @as(u64, pack_qrels.items.len));
   134	    }
   135	    if (pack_n_q.items.len == 0) return error.NoOverlap;
   136	
   137	    const pack = tac.retrieval.bench.QueryPack{
   138	        .n_q = pack_n_q.items,
   139	        .q_offsets = pack_q_offsets.items,
   140	        .tokens = pack_tokens.items,
   141	        .qrels = pack_qrels.items,
   142	        .qrels_lo = pack_qrels_lo.items,
   143	    };
   144	
   145	    // ---- Open output CSV + buffered writer. ----
   146	    var out_file = try cwd.createFile(io, args.out_csv_path, .{});
   147	    defer out_file.close(io);
   148	    var write_buf: [16 * 1024]u8 = undefined;
   149	    var fw = out_file.writer(io, &write_buf);
   150	    var w = &fw.interface;
   151	    try w.writeAll(tac.retrieval.bench.csv_header);
   152	    try w.writeByte('\n');
   153	
   154	    var ctx = CsvCtx{
   155	        .writer = w,
   156	        .dataset = args.dataset,
   157	        .git_sha = args.git_sha,
   158	    };
   159	
   160	    try tac.retrieval.bench.runSweep(&index, pack, args.metric, writeRowCallback, &ctx, gpa);
   161	    try w.flush();
   162	}

   271	const synthetic_fixture = @import("../io/synthetic_fixture.zig");
   272	const token_dump = @import("../io/token_dump.zig");
   273	
   274	test "bench: end-to-end smoke — single-cell sweep on synthetic fixture" {
   275	    const gpa = testing.allocator;
   276	
   277	    var fx = try synthetic_fixture.build(gpa, .{
   278	        .seed = 9001,
   279	        .n_docs = 100,
   280	        .dim = 32,
   281	        .vocab_size = 16,
   282	        .avg_doc_len = 8,
   283	    });
   284	    defer fx.deinit(gpa);
   285	
   286	    const fx_bytes = try token_dump.writeAlloc(gpa, fx.toBuild());
   287	    defer gpa.free(fx_bytes);
   288	    const td = try token_dump.parseBytes(fx_bytes);
   289	
   290	    var image = try storage.build(&td, .{
   291	        .kappa_total = 32,
   292	        .seed = 9002,
   293	        .mu = 4,
   294	        .tau = 8,
   295	        .epsilon = 1,
   296	        .theta = 1,
   297	        .hnsw = .{ .ef_construction = 32, .m = 4 },
   298	    }, gpa);
   299	    defer image.deinit(gpa);
   300	
   301	    var idx = try storage.parse(image.bytes, gpa);
   302	    defer idx.deinit(gpa);
   303	
   304	    // Build a tiny query pack: 5 queries, each with 2 tokens drawn from
   305	    // arbitrary centroids. Qrels assign each query one "gold" doc whose
   306	    // first token's centroid happens to coincide with q_0.
   307	    const n_queries: u32 = 5;
   308	    const dim: u32 = idx.header.dim;
   309	    const n_q_per: u32 = 2;
   310	    const tokens_per_query: u32 = n_q_per * dim;
   311	
   312	    var n_q_arr = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_queries);
   313	    defer gpa.free(n_q_arr);
   314	    var q_offs = try gpa.alloc(u64, n_queries + 1);
   315	    defer gpa.free(q_offs);
   316	    var tokens = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_queries * tokens_per_query);
   317	    defer gpa.free(tokens);
   318	
   319	    q_offs[0] = 0;
   320	    var q: u32 = 0;
   321	    while (q < n_queries) : (q += 1) {
   322	        n_q_arr[q] = n_q_per;
   323	        q_offs[q + 1] = q_offs[q] + tokens_per_query;
   324	        // Pick centroids (q % kappa) and ((q+1) % kappa) as the two query tokens.
   325	        const c0_idx: u32 = q % idx.header.kappa;
   326	        const c1_idx: u32 = (q + 1) % idx.header.kappa;
   327	        const lo: usize = q * tokens_per_query;
   328	        @memcpy(
   329	            tokens[lo .. lo + dim],
   330	            idx.centroids[c0_idx * dim ..][0..dim],
   331	        );
   332	        @memcpy(
   333	            tokens[lo + dim .. lo + 2 * dim],
   334	            idx.centroids[c1_idx * dim ..][0..dim],
   335	        );
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    // Qrels: pretend doc q is relevant for query q (deterministic).
   339	    var qrels = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_queries);
   340	    defer gpa.free(qrels);
   341	    var qrels_lo = try gpa.alloc(u64, n_queries + 1);
   342	    defer gpa.free(qrels_lo);
   343	    qrels_lo[0] = 0;
   344	    q = 0;
   345	    while (q < n_queries) : (q += 1) {
   346	        qrels[q] = q;
   347	        qrels_lo[q + 1] = q + 1;
   348	    }
   349	
   350	    const pack = QueryPack{
   351	        .n_q = n_q_arr,
   352	        .q_offsets = q_offs,
   353	        .tokens = tokens,
   354	        .qrels = qrels,
   355	        .qrels_lo = qrels_lo,
   356	    };
   357	
   358	    const row = try runCell(&idx, pack, 16, 50, null, .mrr_at_10, gpa);
   359	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 16), row.kappa_c);
   360	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 50), row.kappa_d);
   361	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(i16, -1), row.alpha_x100);
   362	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, n_queries), row.n_queries);
   363	    try testing.expect(row.quality >= 0.0 and row.quality <= 1.0);
   364	    try testing.expect(std.math.isFinite(row.avg_total_ms));
   365	    try testing.expect(row.avg_total_ms > 0.0);
   366	    // Per-stage breakdown is non-negative.
   367	    try testing.expect(row.avg_gather_ms >= 0.0);
   368	    try testing.expect(row.avg_prune_ms >= 0.0);
   369	    try testing.expect(row.avg_table_ms >= 0.0);
   370	    try testing.expect(row.avg_refine_ms >= 0.0);
   371	}

     1	//! src/eval/latency.zig — single-thread latency benchmarking.
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever. Paper §9: retrieval reported single-core, so the harness
     4	//! pins the benchmark thread to one logical core (best-effort per-OS).
     5	//!
     6	//! Per-stage timings let us localise regressions. Total query time is
     7	//! `gather_ns + prune_ns + table_build_ns + refine_ns`.
     8	//!
     9	//! Required surface (locked, used by #20 harnesses):
    10	//!   pub const StageTimings = struct {
    11	//!       gather_ns:      u64,
    12	//!       prune_ns:       u64,
    13	//!       table_build_ns: u64,    // pq.buildDistanceTable
    14	//!       refine_ns:      u64,
    15	//!       total_ns:       u64,
    16	//!   };
    17	//!   pub fn timeQuery(...) StageTimings;
    18	//!   pub const Report = struct { … see below … };
    19	//!   pub fn report(timings: []const StageTimings) Report;
    20	//!   pub fn pinToCore(core: u32) void;        // best-effort no-op on unsupported OS
    21	//!
    22	//! ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23	//! DESIGN PSEUDOCODE for timeQuery (waits on retrieval.search — task #19):
    24	//!
    25	//! timeQuery(index, pq, query_tokens, n_q, params, gpa) -> StageTimings:
    26	//!   var t = StageTimings{};
    27	//!   var timer = std.time.Timer.start();
    28	//!   candidates = gather.gather(...);    t.gather_ns = timer.lap();
    29	//!   survivors  = prune.prune(...);      t.prune_ns  = timer.lap();
    30	//!   pq.buildDistanceTable(...);         t.table_build_ns = timer.lap();
    31	//!   for s in survivors: refine.refine(...); t.refine_ns = timer.lap();
    32	//!   t.total_ns = t.gather_ns + t.prune_ns + t.table_build_ns + t.refine_ns;
    33	//!   return t;
    34	//!
    35	//! pinToCore(core):
    36	//!   builtin.os.tag == .linux: sched_setaffinity(0, [1u << core])
    37	//!   builtin.os.tag == .macos: thread_policy_set THREAD_AFFINITY_POLICY (best-effort)
    38	//!   else:                     no-op
    39	//! ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40	
    41	const std = @import("std");
    42	const builtin = @import("builtin");
    43	
    44	/// Best-effort pin the calling thread to a single core (paper §9 reports
    45	/// retrieval as single-core wall clock).
    46	///
    47	/// - Linux: `sched_setaffinity(0, [1u << core])` on a singleton CPU set.
    48	/// - macOS: thread_policy_set with THREAD_AFFINITY_POLICY (Apple Silicon
    49	///   ignores real affinity; the tag still groups co-scheduled threads).
    50	/// - Other OS: no-op.
    51	///
    52	/// Errors are swallowed — pinning is best-effort; benchmark numbers are
    53	/// still recorded if the syscall fails. Useful out-of-band signal: the
    54	/// per-stage latency variance in the Report will be wider on a busy
    55	/// machine when affinity didn't take.
    56	pub fn pinToCore(core: u32) void {
    57	    pinToCoreImpl(core);
    58	}
    59	
    60	const pinToCoreImpl = if (builtin.os.tag == .linux) pinToCoreLinux else pinToCoreNoop;
    61	
    62	fn pinToCoreLinux(core: u32) void {
    63	    // CPU set type matches the kernel's cpu_set_t (1024 bits).
    64	    var set: std.posix.cpu_set_t = std.mem.zeroes(std.posix.cpu_set_t);
    65	    const idx = @as(usize, core);
    66	    const word_bits = @sizeOf(@TypeOf(set[0])) * 8;
    67	    const word = idx / word_bits;
    68	    const bit = idx % word_bits;
    69	    if (word < set.len) {
    70	        set[word] |= @as(@TypeOf(set[0]), 1) << @intCast(bit);
    71	        std.posix.sched_setaffinity(0, &set) catch {};
    72	    }
    73	}
    74	
    75	// macOS/Windows/etc — no portable affinity primitive in std. paper-gap §9:
    76	// non-Linux results carry small extra variance vs the paper's 64-thread
    77	// Xeon Linux baseline.
    78	fn pinToCoreNoop(_: u32) void {}
    79	
    80	pub const StageTimings = struct {
    81	    gather_ns: u64 = 0,
    82	    prune_ns: u64 = 0,
    83	    table_build_ns: u64 = 0,
    84	    refine_ns: u64 = 0,
    85	    total_ns: u64 = 0,
    86	};
    87	
    88	pub const Report = struct {
    89	    n_queries: u32,
    90	    avg_total_ms: f64,
    91	    p50_total_ms: f64,
    92	    p95_total_ms: f64,
    93	    avg_gather_ms: f64,
    94	    avg_prune_ms: f64,
    95	    avg_table_ms: f64,
    96	    avg_refine_ms: f64,
    97	};
    98	
    99	/// Compute aggregate latency stats from per-query stage timings.
   100	///
   101	/// Empty input returns an all-zero `Report` (no NaNs) so callers can render
   102	/// it unconditionally. Percentiles use nearest-rank (0-based floor) on the
   103	/// sorted total_ns — simplest deterministic rule, matches what most IR
   104	/// latency tables print.
   105	///
   106	/// Allocates a small page-allocator buffer for the percentile sort when the
   107	/// input exceeds the on-stack threshold; fatal-panics on OOM (this is dev-only
   108	/// reporting, not a production path).
   109	pub fn report(timings: []const StageTimings) Report {
   110	    if (timings.len == 0) return .{
   111	        .n_queries = 0,
   112	        .avg_total_ms = 0,
   113	        .p50_total_ms = 0,
   114	        .p95_total_ms = 0,
   115	        .avg_gather_ms = 0,
   116	        .avg_prune_ms = 0,
   117	        .avg_table_ms = 0,
   118	        .avg_refine_ms = 0,
   119	    };
   120	
   121	    var sum_total: u64 = 0;
   122	    var sum_gather: u64 = 0;
   123	    var sum_prune: u64 = 0;
   124	    var sum_table: u64 = 0;
   125	    var sum_refine: u64 = 0;
   126	    for (timings) |t| {
   127	        sum_total += t.total_ns;
   128	        sum_gather += t.gather_ns;
   129	        sum_prune += t.prune_ns;
   130	        sum_table += t.table_build_ns;
   131	        sum_refine += t.refine_ns;
   132	    }
   133	    const n: f64 = @floatFromInt(timings.len);
   134	
   135	    var stack_buf: [16384]u64 = undefined;
   136	    const slice = if (timings.len <= stack_buf.len)
   137	        stack_buf[0..timings.len]
   138	    else blk: {
   139	        const heap = std.heap.page_allocator.alloc(u64, timings.len) catch
   140	            @panic("latency.report: allocation failed");
   141	        break :blk heap;
   142	    };
   143	    defer if (timings.len > stack_buf.len) std.heap.page_allocator.free(slice);
   144	
   145	    for (timings, 0..) |t, i| slice[i] = t.total_ns;
   146	    std.mem.sort(u64, slice, {}, std.sort.asc(u64));
   147	
   148	    const p50_idx = (timings.len * 50) / 100;
   149	    const p95_idx_raw = (timings.len * 95) / 100;
   150	    const p95_idx = if (p95_idx_raw >= timings.len) timings.len - 1 else p95_idx_raw;
   151	
   152	    const ns_per_ms: f64 = 1_000_000;
   153	    return .{
   154	        .n_queries = @intCast(timings.len),
   155	        .avg_total_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(sum_total)) / n / ns_per_ms,
   156	        .p50_total_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(slice[p50_idx])) / ns_per_ms,
   157	        .p95_total_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(slice[p95_idx])) / ns_per_ms,
   158	        .avg_gather_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(sum_gather)) / n / ns_per_ms,
   159	        .avg_prune_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(sum_prune)) / n / ns_per_ms,
   160	        .avg_table_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(sum_table)) / n / ns_per_ms,
   161	        .avg_refine_ms = @as(f64, @floatFromInt(sum_refine)) / n / ns_per_ms,
   162	    };
   163	}
   164	
   165	const testing = std.testing;
   166	
   167	test "report on empty input → all zeros" {
   168	    const r = report(&.{});
   169	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 0), r.n_queries);
   170	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(f64, 0), r.avg_total_ms);
   171	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(f64, 0), r.p50_total_ms);
   172	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(f64, 0), r.p95_total_ms);
   173	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(f64, 0), r.avg_refine_ms);
   174	}
   175	
   176	test "report computes mean and percentiles in ms" {
   177	    const ms = 1_000_000;
   178	    const samples = [_]StageTimings{
   179	        .{ .gather_ns = 1 * ms, .refine_ns = 4 * ms, .total_ns = 5 * ms },
   180	        .{ .gather_ns = 2 * ms, .refine_ns = 8 * ms, .total_ns = 10 * ms },
   181	        .{ .gather_ns = 3 * ms, .refine_ns = 12 * ms, .total_ns = 15 * ms },
   182	        .{ .gather_ns = 4 * ms, .refine_ns = 16 * ms, .total_ns = 20 * ms },
   183	    };
   184	    const r = report(&samples);
   185	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 4), r.n_queries);
   186	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 12.5), r.avg_total_ms, 1e-9);
   187	    // p50 idx = floor(4*50/100) = 2 → sorted[2] = 15 ms
   188	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 15.0), r.p50_total_ms, 1e-9);
   189	    // p95 idx = floor(4*95/100) = 3 → sorted[3] = 20 ms
   190	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 20.0), r.p95_total_ms, 1e-9);
   191	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 2.5), r.avg_gather_ms, 1e-9);
   192	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 10.0), r.avg_refine_ms, 1e-9);
   193	}

   196	    const samples = [_]StageTimings{
   197	        .{ .gather_ns = 7_000_000, .total_ns = 7_000_000 },
   198	    };
   199	    const r = report(&samples);
   200	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 1), r.n_queries);
   201	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 7.0), r.avg_total_ms, 1e-9);
   202	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 7.0), r.p50_total_ms, 1e-9);
   203	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f64, 7.0), r.p95_total_ms, 1e-9);
   204	}

   206	test "pinToCore is a no-op for unsupported / out-of-range cores" {
   207	    // The function is best-effort: any core ID, including ones the kernel
   208	    // doesn't know about, must not crash. We don't assert real affinity —
   209	    // that requires reading /proc on Linux and isn't portable.
   210	    pinToCore(0);
   211	    pinToCore(1);
   212	    pinToCore(9999); // way past any reasonable CPU count
   213	}

     1	//! benchmarks/msmarco_v1.zig — MS MARCO-v1 (dev.small) harness entry.
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever. Target operating points (paper Table 1):
     4	//!   - MRR@10 = 39.0 → 10 ms/query
     5	//!   - MRR@10 = 39.3 → 14 ms/query
     6	//!
     7	//! Single-thread (paper §9). Sweeps κ_c × κ_d × α over the paper §6 grid via
     8	//! `tac.retrieval.bench.runSweep` and writes one CSV row per cell.
     9	//!
    10	//! Build: `zig build run-bench_msmarco -Doptimize=ReleaseFast -- <args>`.
    11	//!
    12	//! Inputs (produced by `tools/encode.py`):
    13	//!   --index    path/to/msmarco.tac        (tac.index.storage.serialise output)
    14	//!   --queries  path/to/queries.bin        (encoded with --mode queries)
    15	//!   --qids     path/to/queries.bin.qids   (sidecar, u32 LE per query)
    16	//!   --qrels    path/to/qrels.dev.small.tsv  (TREC-style)
    17	//!   --out      path/to/results.csv
    18	
    19	const std = @import("std");
    20	const tac = @import("tac");
    21	const runner = @import("common/runner.zig");
    22	
    23	fn die(msg: []const u8) noreturn {
    24	    std.debug.print("{s}\n", .{msg});
    25	    std.process.exit(2);
    26	}
    27	
    28	fn parseArgs(m: std.process.Init.Minimal, gpa: std.mem.Allocator) !runner.RunArgs {
    29	    var iter = try std.process.Args.Iterator.initAllocator(m.args, gpa);
    30	    defer iter.deinit();
    31	    _ = iter.next(); // program name
    32	
    33	    var index_path: []const u8 = "";
    34	    var queries_path: []const u8 = "";
    35	    var qids_path: []const u8 = "";
    36	    var qrels_path: []const u8 = "";
    37	    var out_csv: []const u8 = "";
    38	    var git_sha: []const u8 = "unknown";
    39	
    40	    while (iter.next()) |a| {
    41	        if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--index")) {
    42	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--index needs a path");
    43	            index_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    44	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--queries")) {
    45	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--queries needs a path");
    46	            queries_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    47	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--qids")) {
    48	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--qids needs a path");
    49	            qids_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    50	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--qrels")) {
    51	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--qrels needs a path");
    52	            qrels_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    53	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--out")) {
    54	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--out needs a path");
    55	            out_csv = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    56	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--git-sha")) {
    57	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--git-sha needs a value");
    58	            git_sha = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    59	        } else {
    60	            std.debug.print("unknown arg: {s}\n", .{a});
    61	            die("unknown arg; required: --index --queries --qids --qrels --out [--git-sha]");
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	    if (index_path.len == 0 or queries_path.len == 0 or qids_path.len == 0 or
    65	        qrels_path.len == 0 or out_csv.len == 0)
    66	    {
    67	        die(
    68	            \\bench_msmarco: missing required args.
    69	            \\  --index PATH    .tac index file
    70	            \\  --queries PATH  encoded queries.bin (token_dump format)
    71	            \\  --qids PATH     queries.bin.qids sidecar (u32 LE per query)
    72	            \\  --qrels PATH    qrels.tsv (qid<TAB>iter<TAB>doc_id<TAB>rel)
    73	            \\  --out PATH      output CSV
    74	            \\  [--git-sha SHA] traceability tag (defaults to "unknown")
    75	        );
    76	    }
    77	    return .{
    78	        .dataset = "msmarco-v1",
    79	        .index_path = index_path,
    80	        .queries_path = queries_path,
    81	        .qids_path = qids_path,
    82	        .qrels_path = qrels_path,
    83	        .out_csv_path = out_csv,
    84	        .git_sha = git_sha,
    85	        .metric = .mrr_at_10,
    86	        .min_rel = 1,
    87	        .pin_core = 0,
    88	    };
    89	}

     6	//! Same shape as msmarco_v1.zig, but uses Success@5 as the metric.

     9	const std = @import("std");
    10	const tac = @import("tac");
    11	const runner = @import("common/runner.zig");
    12	
    13	fn die(msg: []const u8) noreturn {
    14	    std.debug.print("{s}\n", .{msg});
    15	    std.process.exit(2);
    16	}
    17	
    18	fn parseArgs(m: std.process.Init.Minimal, gpa: std.mem.Allocator) !runner.RunArgs {
    19	    var iter = try std.process.Args.Iterator.initAllocator(m.args, gpa);
    20	    defer iter.deinit();
    21	    _ = iter.next();
    22	
    23	    var index_path: []const u8 = "";
    24	    var queries_path: []const u8 = "";
    25	    var qids_path: []const u8 = "";
    26	    var qrels_path: []const u8 = "";
    27	    var out_csv: []const u8 = "";
    28	    var git_sha: []const u8 = "unknown";
    29	
    30	    while (iter.next()) |a| {
    31	        if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--index")) {
    32	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--index needs a path");
    33	            index_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    34	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--queries")) {
    35	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--queries needs a path");
    36	            queries_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    37	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--qids")) {
    38	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--qids needs a path");
    39	            qids_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    40	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--qrels")) {
    41	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--qrels needs a path");
    42	            qrels_path = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    43	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--out")) {
    44	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--out needs a path");
    45	            out_csv = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    46	        } else if (std.mem.eql(u8, a, "--git-sha")) {
    47	            const v = iter.next() orelse die("--git-sha needs a value");
    48	            git_sha = try gpa.dupe(u8, v);
    49	        } else {
    50	            std.debug.print("unknown arg: {s}\n", .{a});
    51	            die("unknown arg; required: --index --queries --qids --qrels --out [--git-sha]");
    52	        }
    53	    }
    54	    if (index_path.len == 0 or queries_path.len == 0 or qids_path.len == 0 or
    55	        qrels_path.len == 0 or out_csv.len == 0)
    56	    {
    57	        die(
    58	            \\bench_lotte: missing required args.
    59	            \\  --index PATH    .tac index file
    60	            \\  --queries PATH  encoded queries.bin (token_dump format)
    61	            \\  --qids PATH     queries.bin.qids sidecar
    62	            \\  --qrels PATH    qrels.tsv
    63	            \\  --out PATH      output CSV
    64	            \\  [--git-sha SHA] traceability tag
    65	        );
    66	    }
    67	    return .{
    68	        .dataset = "lotte-pooled",
    69	        .index_path = index_path,
    70	        .queries_path = queries_path,
    71	        .qids_path = qids_path,
    72	        .qrels_path = qrels_path,
    73	        .out_csv_path = out_csv,
    74	        .git_sha = git_sha,
    75	        .metric = .success_at_5,
    76	        .min_rel = 1,
    77	        .pin_core = 0,
    78	    };
    79	}
    80	
    81	pub fn main(m: std.process.Init.Minimal) !void {
    82	    var gpa_state: std.heap.DebugAllocator(.{}) = .init;
    83	    defer _ = gpa_state.deinit();
    84	    const gpa = gpa_state.allocator();
    85	
    86	    const args = try parseArgs(m, gpa);
    87	    defer {
    88	        gpa.free(args.index_path);
    89	        gpa.free(args.queries_path);
    90	        gpa.free(args.qids_path);
    91	        gpa.free(args.qrels_path);
    92	        gpa.free(args.out_csv_path);
    93	        if (!std.mem.eql(u8, args.git_sha, "unknown")) gpa.free(args.git_sha);
    94	    }
    95	    _ = tac.retrieval.bench.csv_header;
    96	
    97	    try runner.runDatasetSweep(args, gpa);
    98	}

     1	//! src/retrieval/search.zig — Retrieval.search driver (paper §5).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever. Stitches gather → prune → refine into the public
     4	//! retrieval entry point.
     5	//!
     6	//! NOTE: this file is not yet wired into `src/root.zig`'s `retrieval` block.
     7	//! Adding the line is lead-owned (root.zig is a cross-cutting file). Until
     8	//! that lands, search() is callable from in-tree tests but doesn't appear
     9	//! on the public `tac.retrieval.*` namespace.
    10	
    11	const std = @import("std");
    12	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    13	
    14	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    15	const storage = @import("../index/storage.zig");
    16	const pq_mod = @import("../index/pq.zig");
    17	const gather = @import("gather.zig");
    18	const prune = @import("prune.zig");
    19	const refine = @import("refine.zig");
    20	
    21	pub const SearchParams = struct {
    22	    kappa_c: u32,
    23	    kappa_d: u32,
    24	    alpha: ?f32 = null,
    25	    top_k: u32,
    26	};
    27	
    28	pub const ScoredDoc = struct {
    29	    doc_id: u32,
    30	    score: f32,
    31	};
    32	
    33	pub const SearchError = error{
    34	    TopKExceedsKappaD,
    35	} || gather.GatherError || pq_mod.PqError || refine.RefineError;
    36	
    37	fn cmpDescScored(_: void, a: ScoredDoc, b: ScoredDoc) bool {
    38	    return a.score > b.score;
    39	}
    40	
    41	/// Run the full paper §5 retrieval pipeline.
    42	///
    43	/// Returns an allocator-owned slice of `≤ top_k` ScoredDocs sorted descending
    44	/// by exact MaxSim. Caller frees with `gpa.free(result)`.
    45	///
    46	/// Determinism: gather emits the same candidate set for a given query/index;
    47	/// prune sorts deterministically; refine is pure; final sort uses strict `>`
    48	/// so equal-score ties keep input order. Sticky doc-id tie-breaks would
    49	/// need a secondary key — defer until benchmarks reveal a need.
    50	pub fn search(
    51	    index: *const storage.Index,
    52	    query_tokens: []const f32,
    53	    n_q: u32,
    54	    params: SearchParams,
    55	    gpa: Allocator,
    56	) SearchError![]ScoredDoc {
    57	    if (params.top_k > params.kappa_d) return error.TopKExceedsKappaD;
    58	
    59	    // Gather (paper §5.1): walk inverted lists, accumulate S̃(q, d).
    60	    const candidates = try gather.gather(
    61	        index,
    62	        query_tokens,
    63	        n_q,
    64	        .{ .kappa_c = params.kappa_c },
    65	        gpa,
    66	    );
    67	    defer gpa.free(candidates);
    68	
    69	    // Prune (paper §5.2): top-κ_d truncation + optional adaptive CP.
    70	    const survivors = prune.prune(candidates, .{
    71	        .kappa_d = params.kappa_d,
    72	        .alpha = params.alpha,
    73	    });
    74	    if (survivors.len == 0) return gpa.alloc(ScoredDoc, 0);
    75	
    76	    // Build the per-query distance table once; shared across all refine calls.
    77	    const table_len: usize =
    78	        @as(usize, constants.PQ_M) * @as(usize, constants.PQ_CENTROIDS) * @as(usize, n_q);
    79	    const table = try gpa.alloc(f32, table_len);
    80	    defer gpa.free(table);
    81	    try index.pq.buildDistanceTable(query_tokens, n_q, table);
    82	
    83	    // Refine (paper §5.3): exact MaxSim per surviving candidate.
    84	    var refined = try gpa.alloc(ScoredDoc, survivors.len);
    85	    errdefer gpa.free(refined);
    86	    const per_token_max = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_q);
    87	    defer gpa.free(per_token_max);
    88	
    89	    for (survivors, 0..) |s, k| {
    90	        refined[k] = .{
    91	            .doc_id = s.doc_id,
    92	            .score = try refine.refine(index, table, query_tokens, n_q, s, per_token_max),
    93	        };
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    std.sort.pdq(ScoredDoc, refined, {}, cmpDescScored);
    97	
    98	    // Trim to top_k.
    99	    const want: usize = @min(refined.len, @as(usize, params.top_k));
   100	    if (want < refined.len) {
   101	        const trimmed = try gpa.alloc(ScoredDoc, want);
   102	        @memcpy(trimmed, refined[0..want]);
   103	        gpa.free(refined);
   104	        return trimmed;
   105	    }
   106	    return refined;
   107	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! src/retrieval/gather.zig — Gather phase (paper §5.1).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever.
     4	//! See plan 04-retrieval-and-eval.plan.md.
     5	//!
     6	//! For each query token q_i:
     7	//!   - HNSW top-κ_c centroids with ef_s = 1.5·κ_c.
     8	//!   - Walk the inverted list L_j of each hit centroid, accumulate
     9	//!     `s̃_i(d) = max_{ j : d ∈ L_j } ⟨q_i, c_j⟩`.
    10	//! Aggregate across query tokens:
    11	//!   `S̃(q, d) = Σ_{i=1..n_q} s̃_i(d)`.
    12	//!
    13	//! No PQ touches in this phase — that's the whole point of the
    14	//! gather/refine split. Returned candidates are unsorted; `prune.prune`
    15	//! sorts and truncates.
    16	
    17	const std = @import("std");
    18	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    19	
    20	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    21	const vec = @import("../util/vec.zig");
    22	const storage = @import("../index/storage.zig");
    23	const hnsw_mod = @import("../index/hnsw.zig");
    24	
    25	pub const Candidate = struct {
    26	    doc_id: u32,
    27	    score: f32,
    28	};
    29	
    30	pub const GatherParams = struct {
    31	    kappa_c: u32,
    32	};
    33	
    34	pub const GatherError = error{
    35	    QueryShapeMismatch,
    36	    KappaCZero,
    37	} || Allocator.Error || vec.VecError || hnsw_mod.HnswError;
    38	
    39	/// Run the paper §5.1 gather over `query_tokens` (n_q · dim, row-major,
    40	/// L2-normalised). Returns the union of touched docs with their aggregate
    41	/// `S̃(q, d)` scores. Caller frees the slice.
    42	///
    43	/// paper-gap §5.1: the paper writes ef_s = 1.5·κ_c without saying whether
    44	/// the HNSW search runs per-token or per-query. We run per-token — matches
    45	/// the natural reading "for each query token … HNSW top-κ_c". Cross-check
    46	/// vs reference Rust impl tracked in plan 06.
    47	pub fn gather(
    48	    index: *const storage.Index,
    49	    query_tokens: []const f32,
    50	    n_q: u32,
    51	    params: GatherParams,
    52	    gpa: Allocator,
    53	) GatherError![]Candidate {
    54	    if (params.kappa_c == 0) return error.KappaCZero;
    55	    const dim: usize = @intCast(index.header.dim);
    56	    if (n_q == 0 or query_tokens.len != @as(usize, n_q) * dim) {
    57	        return error.QueryShapeMismatch;
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    const n_docs: usize = @intCast(index.header.n_docs);
    61	    if (n_docs == 0) return gpa.alloc(Candidate, 0);
    62	
    63	    const ef_s = constants.hnswEfSearch(params.kappa_c);
    64	
    65	    // Per-token max similarity (s̃_i(d)) and the running aggregate (S̃(q, d)).
    66	    // Dense f32 buffers are faster than a hash map for typical candidate
    67	    // counts (~10–50K on MS MARCO @ κ_c=80). Two parallel u8 "seen" bitmaps
    68	    // record whether a doc was touched in the current query-token pass and
    69	    // whether it ever made it into `touched_global`. We reset per_tok_seen
    70	    // entries from `touched_for_tok` so we never do an O(n_docs) clear.
    71	    const per_token_max = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_docs);
    72	    defer gpa.free(per_token_max);
    73	    const per_tok_seen = try gpa.alloc(bool, n_docs);
    74	    defer gpa.free(per_tok_seen);
    75	    @memset(per_tok_seen, false);
    76	
    77	    const accum = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_docs);
    78	    defer gpa.free(accum);
    79	    @memset(accum, 0.0);
    80	    const global_seen = try gpa.alloc(bool, n_docs);
    81	    defer gpa.free(global_seen);
    82	    @memset(global_seen, false);
    83	
    84	    var touched_for_tok: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
    85	    defer touched_for_tok.deinit(gpa);
    86	    var touched_global: std.ArrayList(u32) = .empty;
    87	    defer touched_global.deinit(gpa);
    88	
    89	    const centroid_buf = try gpa.alloc(u32, params.kappa_c);
    90	    defer gpa.free(centroid_buf);
    91	
    92	    var i: u32 = 0;
    93	    while (i < n_q) : (i += 1) {
    94	        const q_i = query_tokens[@as(usize, i) * dim ..][0..dim];
    95	
    96	        // Reset only the per-tok-seen slots we wrote on the previous token.
    97	        for (touched_for_tok.items) |d| per_tok_seen[d] = false;
    98	        touched_for_tok.clearRetainingCapacity();
    99	
   100	        const n_hit = try hnsw_mod.search(&index.hnsw, q_i, params.kappa_c, ef_s, centroid_buf, gpa);
   101	
   102	        var j_idx: u32 = 0;
   103	        while (j_idx < n_hit) : (j_idx += 1) {
   104	            const j: u32 = centroid_buf[j_idx];
   105	            const c_j = index.centroids[@as(usize, j) * dim ..][0..dim];
   106	            const sim = try vec.dot(q_i, c_j);
   107	            for (index.ilists.list(j)) |d| {
   108	                if (!per_tok_seen[d]) {
   109	                    per_tok_seen[d] = true;
   110	                    per_token_max[d] = sim;
   111	                    try touched_for_tok.append(gpa, d);
   112	                } else if (sim > per_token_max[d]) {
   113	                    per_token_max[d] = sim;
   114	                }
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        for (touched_for_tok.items) |d| {
   119	            if (!global_seen[d]) {
   120	                global_seen[d] = true;
   121	                try touched_global.append(gpa, d);
   122	            }
   123	            accum[d] += per_token_max[d];
   124	        }
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    const out = try gpa.alloc(Candidate, touched_global.items.len);
   128	    for (touched_global.items, 0..) |d, k| {
   129	        out[k] = .{ .doc_id = d, .score = accum[d] };
   130	    }
   131	    return out;
   132	}
   133

   150	/// Build a small Index from a synthetic fixture for end-to-end gather tests.
   151	/// The caller must `built.index.deinit(gpa)`, `gpa.free(built.image)`, and
   152	/// `gpa.free(built.fx_bytes)`. Fixture parameters mirror the indexer's own
   153	/// integration test in src/index/storage.zig — those are the smallest
   154	/// numbers that satisfy TAC budget allocation with permissive thresholds.
   155	fn buildTestIndex(
   156	    gpa: Allocator,
   157	    seed: u64,
   158	    n_docs: u64,
   159	    kappa: u32,
   160	) !struct { index: storage.Index, image: []align(8) u8, fx_bytes: []align(8) u8 } {
   161	    var fx = try synthetic_fixture.build(gpa, .{
   162	        .seed = seed,
   163	        .n_docs = n_docs,
   164	        .dim = 32, // matches PQ_M so sub_dim = 1.
   165	        .vocab_size = 16,
   166	        .avg_doc_len = 8,
   167	    });
   168	    defer fx.deinit(gpa);
   169	
   170	    const fx_bytes = try token_dump.writeAlloc(gpa, fx.toBuild());
   171	    errdefer gpa.free(fx_bytes);
   172	    const td = try token_dump.parseBytes(fx_bytes);
   173	
   174	    var image = try storage.build(&td, .{
   175	        .kappa_total = kappa,
   176	        .seed = seed +% 1,
   177	        // Test-only TAC overrides: paper-strict (μ=128, τ=256, θ=39) is
   178	        // impossible at fixture scale.
   179	        .mu = 4,
   180	        .tau = 8,
   181	        .epsilon = 1,
   182	        .theta = 1,
   183	        .hnsw = .{ .ef_construction = 32, .m = 4 },
   184	    }, gpa);
   185	    errdefer image.deinit(gpa);
   186	
   187	    const idx = try storage.parse(image.bytes, gpa);
   188	    return .{ .index = idx, .image = image.bytes, .fx_bytes = fx_bytes };
   189	}

   191	test "gather: returns deduped candidate set with positive scores" {
   192	    const gpa = std.testing.allocator;
   193	    var built = try buildTestIndex(gpa, 7, 50, 16);
   194	    defer {
   195	        built.index.deinit(gpa);
   196	        gpa.free(built.image);
   197	        gpa.free(built.fx_bytes);
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    // Use the first doc's first 3 tokens as a query — guaranteed to hit.
   201	    const dim = built.index.header.dim;
   202	    const q_doc_lo: usize = @intCast(built.index.doc_index[0]);
   203	    _ = q_doc_lo;
   204	    // Build a synthetic query directly from a centroid — ensures HNSW finds
   205	    // exactly that centroid's posting list. We pick centroid 0.
   206	    const n_q: u32 = 2;
   207	    const c0 = built.index.centroids[0..dim];
   208	    const c1 = built.index.centroids[dim .. 2 * dim];
   209	    var query = try gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, n_q) * dim);
   210	    defer gpa.free(query);
   211	    @memcpy(query[0..dim], c0);
   212	    @memcpy(query[dim..], c1);
   213	
   214	    const cands = try gather(&built.index, query, n_q, .{ .kappa_c = 4 }, gpa);
   215	    defer gpa.free(cands);
   216	
   217	    try std.testing.expect(cands.len > 0);
   218	    // All scores positive: queries are centroids themselves, so ⟨q, c_q⟩ = 1.
   219	    for (cands) |c| {
   220	        try std.testing.expect(c.score > 0.0);
   221	        try std.testing.expect(c.doc_id < built.index.header.n_docs);
   222	    }
   223	    // Doc IDs unique.
   224	    var seen: std.AutoHashMap(u32, void) = .init(gpa);
   225	    defer seen.deinit();
   226	    for (cands) |c| {
   227	        const r = try seen.getOrPut(c.doc_id);
   228	        try std.testing.expect(!r.found_existing);
   229	    }
   230	}
