   259	test "gather: matches brute-force S̃ for kappa_c = kappa (all centroids)" {
   260	    const gpa = std.testing.allocator;
   261	    const kappa: u32 = 16;
   262	    var built = try buildTestIndex(gpa, 21, 50, kappa);
   263	    defer {
   264	        built.index.deinit(gpa);
   265	        gpa.free(built.image);
   266	        gpa.free(built.fx_bytes);
   267	    }
   268	    const idx = &built.index;
   269	    const dim: usize = @intCast(idx.header.dim);
   270	    const n_docs: u32 = @intCast(idx.header.n_docs);
   271	
   272	    // Synthesise 2 query tokens — pick centroids 0 and 2.
   273	    const n_q: u32 = 2;
   274	    var query = try gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, n_q) * dim);
   275	    defer gpa.free(query);
   276	    @memcpy(query[0..dim], idx.centroids[0..dim]);
   277	    @memcpy(query[dim..], idx.centroids[2 * dim .. 3 * dim]);
   278	
   279	    const cands = try gather(idx, query, n_q, .{ .kappa_c = kappa }, gpa);
   280	    defer gpa.free(cands);
   281	
   282	    // Brute force: for each (i, d), compute s̃_i(d) = max over centroids j
   283	    // such that d ∈ L_j of ⟨q_i, c_j⟩. Sum across i.
   284	    const expected = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_docs);
   285	    defer gpa.free(expected);
   286	    @memset(expected, 0.0);
   287	
   288	    var i: u32 = 0;
   289	    while (i < n_q) : (i += 1) {
   290	        const q_i = query[@as(usize, i) * dim ..][0..dim];
   291	        // For each doc, scan centroids it appears in.
   292	        var d: u32 = 0;
   293	        while (d < n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   294	            var best: f32 = 0.0;
   295	            var found = false;
   296	            var j: u32 = 0;
   297	            while (j < kappa) : (j += 1) {
   298	                const lst = idx.ilists.list(j);
   299	                var contains = false;
   300	                for (lst) |x| if (x == d) {
   301	                    contains = true;
   302	                    break;
   303	                };
   304	                if (!contains) continue;
   305	                const c_j = idx.centroids[@as(usize, j) * dim ..][0..dim];
   306	                const sim = try vec.dot(q_i, c_j);
   307	                if (!found or sim > best) {
   308	                    best = sim;
   309	                    found = true;
   310	                }
   311	            }
   312	            if (found) expected[d] += best;
   313	        }
   314	    }
   315	
   316	    // Every candidate score must equal the brute-force aggregate within FP
   317	    // tolerance. Docs not in `cands` must have expected[d] == 0.
   318	    for (cands) |c| {
   319	        try std.testing.expectApproxEqAbs(expected[c.doc_id], c.score, 1e-4);
   320	    }
   321	    var seen = try gpa.alloc(bool, n_docs);
   322	    defer gpa.free(seen);
   323	    @memset(seen, false);
   324	    for (cands) |c| seen[c.doc_id] = true;
   325	    var d: u32 = 0;
   326	    while (d < n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   327	        if (!seen[d]) try std.testing.expectEqual(@as(f32, 0.0), expected[d]);
   328	    }
   329	}
   330

   334	    defer {

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! src/retrieval/refine.zig — Refine phase (paper §5.3).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever.
     4	//! See plan 04-retrieval-and-eval.plan.md.
     5	//!
     6	//! For each surviving candidate document d:
     7	//!   Pass 1: stream centroid IDs → per-(q_i, k) centroid contribution
     8	//!           ⟨q_i, c_{c_k}⟩.
     9	//!   Pass 2: stream PQ codes → residual contribution via the cache-optimised
    10	//!           distance table laid out [M macro][256 code][n_q micro], scaled
    11	//!           by the stored residual norm.
    12	//! Output: exact MaxSim score for the (q, d) pair.
    13	//!
    14	//! Original token vector t_k = c_k + norm_k · r_k (paper §4 "homogeneous
    15	//! compression": residuals normalised, norms saved). So the inner product
    16	//! decomposes:
    17	//!     ⟨q_i, t_k⟩ = ⟨q_i, c_k⟩ + norm_k · ⟨q_i, r_k⟩
    18	//! and r_k is approximated by PQ decode → ⟨q_i, r_k⟩ ≈ Σ_m table[m][code][i].
    19	//! Refine therefore computes:
    20	//!     score_i(k, d) = ⟨q_i, c_{c_k}⟩
    21	//!                   + norm_k · Σ_{m=0..M-1} table[m][PQ_{k,m}][i]
    22	//! Per-token MaxSim:
    23	//!     s_i(d) = max_{k=1..n_d} score_i(k, d)
    24	//! Final:
    25	//!     S(q, d) = Σ_{i=1..n_q} s_i(d)
    26	
    27	const std = @import("std");
    28	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    29	
    30	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    31	const vec = @import("../util/vec.zig");
    32	const storage = @import("../index/storage.zig");
    33	const pq_mod = @import("../index/pq.zig");
    34	const gather = @import("gather.zig");
    35	
    36	pub const RefineError = error{
    37	    QueryShapeMismatch,
    38	    TableSizeMismatch,
    39	    DocOutOfRange,
    40	    DocLayoutCorrupt,
    41	} || vec.VecError;
    42	
    43	/// Decoded view into a doc's payload. Slices alias `Index.doc_payload`.
    44	const DocLayout = struct {
    45	    n_d: u32,
    46	    centroid_ids: []align(1) const u32,
    47	    pq_codes: []const u8,
    48	};
    49	
    50	fn decodeDocLayout(layout_bytes: []const u8) RefineError!DocLayout {
    51	    if (layout_bytes.len < 4) return error.DocLayoutCorrupt;
    52	    const n_d = std.mem.readInt(u32, layout_bytes[0..4], .little);
    53	    const centroid_bytes_len: usize = @as(usize, n_d) * @sizeOf(u32);
    54	    const codes_len: usize = @as(usize, n_d) * @as(usize, constants.PQ_M);
    55	    const expected_min: usize = 4 + centroid_bytes_len + codes_len;
    56	    if (layout_bytes.len < expected_min) return error.DocLayoutCorrupt;
    57	    const centroid_ids: []align(1) const u32 = std.mem.bytesAsSlice(
    58	        u32,
    59	        layout_bytes[4 .. 4 + centroid_bytes_len],
    60	    );
    61	    const pq_codes = layout_bytes[4 + centroid_bytes_len ..][0..codes_len];
    62	    return .{ .n_d = n_d, .centroid_ids = centroid_ids, .pq_codes = pq_codes };
    63	}
    64	
    65	/// Compute exact MaxSim S(q, d) for a single candidate using the
    66	/// pre-built per-query distance table (paper §5.3).
    67	///
    68	/// Args:
    69	///   index            - parsed Index. Refine reads `centroids`, `pq`, the
    70	///                      doc payload via `index.docLayout(d)`, and slices
    71	///                      `residual_norms` via `index.docTokenRange(d)`.
    72	///   distance_table   - PQ_M · 256 · n_q floats, built once per query via
    73	///                      `pq.buildDistanceTable`.
    74	///   query_tokens     - n_q · dim, row-major, L2-normalised. Used for the
    75	///                      Pass-1 centroid term.
    76	///   per_token_max_buf - caller-provided scratch of length ≥ n_q. Reused
    77	///                      across all surviving candidates so refine itself
    78	///                      doesn't allocate.
    79	///
    80	/// The simpler fused-(k,i) loop is implemented first; the cache-optimised
    81	/// outer-m / inner-(k,i) restructuring (paper §5.3, "up to 3.8×") is a
    82	/// later optimisation gated on latency-harness evidence.
    83	pub fn refine(
    84	    index: *const storage.Index,
    85	    distance_table: []const f32,
    86	    query_tokens: []const f32,
    87	    n_q: u32,
    88	    candidate: gather.Candidate,
    89	    per_token_max_buf: []f32,
    90	) RefineError!f32 {
    91	    const dim: usize = @intCast(index.header.dim);
    92	    if (n_q == 0 or query_tokens.len != @as(usize, n_q) * dim) {
    93	        return error.QueryShapeMismatch;
    94	    }
    95	    const expected_table: usize =
    96	        @as(usize, constants.PQ_M) * @as(usize, constants.PQ_CENTROIDS) * @as(usize, n_q);
    97	    if (distance_table.len != expected_table) return error.TableSizeMismatch;
    98	    if (per_token_max_buf.len < n_q) return error.QueryShapeMismatch;
    99	    const n_docs: u32 = @intCast(index.header.n_docs);
   100	    if (candidate.doc_id >= n_docs) return error.DocOutOfRange;
   101	
   102	    const layout = try decodeDocLayout(index.docLayout(candidate.doc_id));
   103	    const n_d = layout.n_d;
   104	    const tok_range = index.docTokenRange(candidate.doc_id);
   105	    const norms_lo: usize = @intCast(tok_range[0]);
   106	    const norms_hi: usize = @intCast(tok_range[1]);
   107	    if (norms_hi - norms_lo != n_d) return error.DocLayoutCorrupt;
   108	    const norms = index.residual_norms[norms_lo..norms_hi];
   109	
   110	    // Initialise per-token max to -inf so the first valid score wins.
   111	    var i: u32 = 0;
   112	    while (i < n_q) : (i += 1) per_token_max_buf[i] = -std.math.inf(f32);
   113	
   114	    // Fused (k, i) loop — paper §5.3 Pass 1 + Pass 2 interleaved per token.
   115	    var k: u32 = 0;
   116	    while (k < n_d) : (k += 1) {
   117	        const c_id: u32 = layout.centroid_ids[k];
   118	        const c_vec = index.centroids[@as(usize, c_id) * dim ..][0..dim];
   119	        const norm_k = norms[k];
   120	        const code_base: usize = @as(usize, k) * @as(usize, constants.PQ_M);
   121	
   122	        i = 0;
   123	        while (i < n_q) : (i += 1) {
   124	            const q_i = query_tokens[@as(usize, i) * dim ..][0..dim];
   125	            const cent_term = try vec.dot(q_i, c_vec);
   126	
   127	            // Residual term: ⟨q_i, r_k⟩ via PQ-decoded codes.
   128	            var res_term: f32 = 0.0;
   129	            var m: u32 = 0;
   130	            while (m < constants.PQ_M) : (m += 1) {
   131	                const code: u8 = layout.pq_codes[code_base + @as(usize, m)];
   132	                res_term += pq_mod.lookup(distance_table, n_q, m, code, i);
   133	            }
   134	
   135	            const score = cent_term + norm_k * res_term;
   136	            if (score > per_token_max_buf[i]) per_token_max_buf[i] = score;
   137	        }
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    // Aggregate. n_d == 0 → no per-token max was written; return 0 so the
   141	    // score sits below any non-empty doc.
   142	    if (n_d == 0) return 0.0;
   143	    var sum: f32 = 0.0;
   144	    i = 0;
   145	    while (i < n_q) : (i += 1) sum += per_token_max_buf[i];
   146	    return sum;
   147	}

   151	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   196	test "refine: exact MaxSim agrees with brute-force baseline (within 1e-3)" {
   197	    const gpa = testing.allocator;
   198	    var built = try buildTestIndex(gpa, 2002, 50, 16);
   199	    defer {
   200	        built.index.deinit(gpa);
   201	        gpa.free(built.image);
   202	        gpa.free(built.fx_bytes);
   203	    }
   204	    const idx = &built.index;
   205	    const dim: usize = @intCast(idx.header.dim);
   206	
   207	    // Synthesise n_q = 2 query tokens from centroids 0 and 2 — guaranteed
   208	    // to produce non-trivial cosine sims with a wide spread of doc tokens.
   209	    const n_q: u32 = 2;
   210	    var query = try gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, n_q) * dim);
   211	    defer gpa.free(query);
   212	    @memcpy(query[0..dim], idx.centroids[0..dim]);
   213	    @memcpy(query[dim..], idx.centroids[2 * dim .. 3 * dim]);
   214	
   215	    const table_len: usize =
   216	        @as(usize, constants.PQ_M) * @as(usize, constants.PQ_CENTROIDS) * n_q;
   217	    const table = try gpa.alloc(f32, table_len);
   218	    defer gpa.free(table);
   219	    try idx.pq.buildDistanceTable(query, n_q, table);
   220	
   221	    const per_token_max = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_q);
   222	    defer gpa.free(per_token_max);
   223	
   224	    // Brute force: reconstruct each doc token as `c_k + norm_k · decode(codes)`
   225	    // and compute MaxSim directly. This validates both the formula and the
   226	    // table layout used by refine.
   227	    var d: u32 = 0;
   228	    var checked: u32 = 0;
   229	    while (d < idx.header.n_docs) : (d += 1) {
   230	        const cand = gather.Candidate{ .doc_id = d, .score = 0.0 };
   231	        const got = try refine(idx, table, query, n_q, cand, per_token_max);
   232	
   233	        const layout = try decodeDocLayout(idx.docLayout(d));
   234	        const tok_range = idx.docTokenRange(d);
   235	        const tok_lo: usize = @intCast(tok_range[0]);
   236	
   237	        var bf_sum: f32 = 0.0;
   238	        var i: u32 = 0;
   239	        while (i < n_q) : (i += 1) {
   240	            const q_i = query[@as(usize, i) * dim ..][0..dim];
   241	            var best: f32 = -std.math.inf(f32);
   242	            var k: u32 = 0;
   243	            while (k < layout.n_d) : (k += 1) {
   244	                const c_id: u32 = layout.centroid_ids[k];
   245	                const c_vec = idx.centroids[@as(usize, c_id) * dim ..][0..dim];
   246	                const norm_k = idx.residual_norms[tok_lo + k];
   247	
   248	                var residual = try gpa.alloc(f32, dim);
   249	                defer gpa.free(residual);
   250	                var m: u32 = 0;
   251	                while (m < constants.PQ_M) : (m += 1) {
   252	                    const code: u8 = layout.pq_codes[k * constants.PQ_M + m];
   253	                    const sub = idx.pq.codebookEntry(m, code);
   254	                    const dst = residual[m * idx.pq.sub_dim ..][0..idx.pq.sub_dim];
   255	                    @memcpy(dst, sub);
   256	                }
   257	
   258	                var sim: f32 = 0.0;
   259	                var dd: usize = 0;
   260	                while (dd < dim) : (dd += 1) {
   261	                    sim += q_i[dd] * (c_vec[dd] + norm_k * residual[dd]);
   262	                }
   263	                if (sim > best) best = sim;
   264	            }
   265	            if (layout.n_d > 0) bf_sum += best;
   266	        }
   267	
   268	        try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(bf_sum, got, 1e-3);
   269	        checked += 1;
   270	    }
   271	    try testing.expect(checked > 0);
   272	}
   273

     1	//! src/retrieval/prune.zig — Candidate pruning (paper §5.2).
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: retriever.
     4	//! See plan 04-retrieval-and-eval.plan.md.
     5	//!
     6	//! Two stages:
     7	//!   1. Top-κ_d truncation by S̃(q, d).
     8	//!   2. Optional adaptive Candidates Pruning (CP) using α ∈ {0.35,0.4,0.45,0.5}.
     9	
    10	const std = @import("std");
    11	const gather = @import("gather.zig");
    12	
    13	pub const PruneParams = struct {
    14	    kappa_d: u32,
    15	    /// `null` disables adaptive Candidates Pruning (top-κ_d only).
    16	    alpha: ?f32 = null,
    17	};
    18	
    19	fn cmpDesc(_: void, a: gather.Candidate, b: gather.Candidate) bool {
    20	    return a.score > b.score;
    21	}
    22	
    23	/// Mutate `candidates` in-place: sort descending by score and truncate to
    24	/// `≤ kappa_d` entries. If `alpha` is set, additionally drop the tail of
    25	/// candidates whose score falls below `alpha * candidates[0].score`.
    26	///
    27	/// Returns the surviving prefix slice (no allocations). Caller still owns
    28	/// `candidates`'s backing memory and should free the original allocation.
    29	///
    30	/// paper-gap §5.2: the paper says "using a fraction α of the running
    31	/// maximum" but doesn't formalise the running-max update rule. We use the
    32	/// simplest deterministic interpretation that matches the wording:
    33	///
    34	///   running_max := candidates[0].score   (the post-sort top)
    35	///   threshold   := alpha * running_max
    36	///   keep iff score >= threshold
    37	///
    38	/// Because Stage 1 leaves `candidates` sorted descending, Stage 2 collapses
    39	/// to a single-pass cutoff: walk until the first score < threshold, drop
    40	/// the rest. Deterministic at every (κ_d, α). Cross-check vs the reference
    41	/// Rust impl tracked in plan 06.
    42	pub fn prune(candidates: []gather.Candidate, params: PruneParams) []gather.Candidate {
    43	    if (candidates.len == 0 or params.kappa_d == 0) return candidates[0..0];
    44	
    45	    // Stage 1 — descending sort by score. pdq is O(n log n) with tiny
    46	    // constants and is deterministic across runs; quickselect to κ_d would
    47	    // shave a log factor but the gather output is bounded by candidate
    48	    // counts in the tens of thousands on MS MARCO @ κ_c=80, well within
    49	    // budget. Revisit if the latency harness shows prune dominating.
    50	    std.sort.pdq(gather.Candidate, candidates, {}, cmpDesc);
    51	
    52	    var keep: usize = @min(candidates.len, @as(usize, params.kappa_d));
    53	
    54	    // Stage 2 — adaptive Candidates Pruning.
    55	    if (params.alpha) |a| {
    56	        const top = candidates[0].score;
    57	        const threshold = a * top;
    58	        var cut: usize = keep;
    59	        var i: usize = 0;
    60	        while (i < keep) : (i += 1) {
    61	            if (candidates[i].score < threshold) {
    62	                cut = i;
    63	                break;
    64	            }
    65	        }
    66	        keep = cut;
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    return candidates[0..keep];
    70	}
    71	
    72	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73	// Tests
    74	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    75	
    76	const testing = std.testing;
    77	
    78	fn makeCands(buf: []gather.Candidate, scores: []const f32) []gather.Candidate {
    79	    for (scores, 0..) |s, i| {
    80	        buf[i] = .{ .doc_id = @intCast(i), .score = s };
    81	    }
    82	    return buf[0..scores.len];
    83	}

    88	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 1000), p.kappa_d);
    89	}

    93	        const p = PruneParams{ .kappa_d = 500, .alpha = a };
    94	        try testing.expect(p.alpha.? == a);
    95	    }
    96	}

codex
On the retrieval side, the code is honest about some known slow paths: `refine` is still the simple fused loop, and `prune` still full-sorts. A more immediate scalability issue is that `gather` allocates and clears dense `n_docs` buffers per query; that is fine for 26k Jira docs but not for MS MARCO-scale claims.

     1	//! src/tac/tac.zig — Token-Aware Clustering (paper §3) four-phase pipeline.
     2	//!
     3	//! Owner: clusterer.
     4	//! See plan 02-tac-clustering.plan.md.
     5	//!
     6	//! TAC is the paper's headline contribution: instead of one global k-means
     7	//! with κ centroids over all N≈600M token vectors, it runs N_T independent
     8	//! per-token k-means with budgets κ_j allocated by a damped weight
     9	//! (paper §3.2 eq: w_j = √(n_j) · s_j). The √n damping shifts allocation from
    10	//! common low-discriminative tokens toward rare but semantically rich ones
    11	//! (paper §2 motivation, §3.4 speedup analysis).
    12	//!
    13	//! Four phases (paper §3.3):
    14	//!   Phase 1: tail handling (micro/small/active split by frequency).
    15	//!   Phase 2: damped scoring κ_j = ⌊(w_j / Σw) · B⌋ for active tokens.
    16	//!   Phase 3: floor (κ_j ≥ ε) and cap (n_j/κ_j ≥ θ).
    17	//!   Phase 4: budget reconciliation to land at Σκ_j = κ_total exactly.
    18	//!
    19	//! This file currently implements Phases 1–4 as pure functions operating on
    20	//! pre-grouped per-token arrays (n_j, s_j). The end-to-end public driver
    21	//! `cluster(td, ...)` lands once `io/token_dump.zig` exposes its grouping
    22	//! API (task #4). All phase logic is unit-testable today.
    23	
    24	const std = @import("std");
    25	const constants = @import("../constants.zig");
    26	const vec = @import("../util/vec.zig");
    27	const kmeans = @import("kmeans.zig");
    28	
    29	const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
    30	
    31	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32	// Public types — frozen by plan 02; consumed by indexer #15 and tests.
    33	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34	
    35	pub const ClusteringParams = struct {
    36	    kappa_total: u32,
    37	    mu: u32 = constants.TAC_MU,
    38	    tau: u32 = constants.TAC_TAU,
    39	    epsilon: u32 = constants.TAC_EPSILON,
    40	    theta: u32 = constants.TAC_THETA,
    41	    max_iters: u32 = 25,
    42	    tol: f32 = 1e-4,
    43	    seed: u64,
    44	    /// Worker threads for the per-token Lloyd loop. `1` is serial
    45	    /// (preserves all existing test invariants and determinism). >1
    46	    /// dispatches via std.Thread.Pool — paper §3 says per-token
    47	    /// k-means subproblems are independent, so this is embarrassingly
    48	    /// parallel; only sync is the final WCSS sum (per-token slot summed
    49	    /// after waitAndWork).
    50	    n_threads: u32 = 1,
    51	};
    52	
    53	pub const ClusteringError = error{
    54	    BudgetTooSmall,
    55	    BudgetTooLarge,
    56	    InvalidThresholds,
    57	    EmptyTokenDump,
    58	} || Allocator.Error || kmeans.KMeansError || std.Thread.SpawnError;
    59	
    60	/// Token classification per paper §3.3 Phase 1.
    61	pub const TokenTier = enum(u8) {
    62	    empty, // n_j == 0 — token never appears in corpus, κ_j = 0
    63	    micro, // n_j < μ      → κ_j = 1
    64	    small, // μ ≤ n_j < τ  → κ_j = 2
    65	    active, // n_j ≥ τ      → enter Phase 2
    66	};
    67	
    68	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    69	// Phase 1: tail handling (paper §3.3).
    70	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    71	
    72	/// Classify a single token by its frequency.
    73	/// paper §3.3: micro<μ, small∈[μ,τ), active≥τ.
    74	pub fn classifyToken(n_j: u32, mu: u32, tau: u32) TokenTier {
    75	    if (n_j == 0) return .empty;
    76	    if (n_j < mu) return .micro;
    77	    if (n_j < tau) return .small;
    78	    return .active;
    79	}
    80	
    81	/// Phase 1 — partition tokens by frequency tier and assign tail κ_j.
    82	///
    83	/// Inputs: `freqs[j]` = n_j for each token j in 0..N_T.
    84	/// Output: `kappa[j]` written for tail tokens (micro=1, small=2, empty=0).
    85	///         Active tokens leave kappa[j]=0; phase 2 fills them in.
    86	///
    87	/// Returns the active-token budget B = kappa_total - Σ_tail κ_j, or
    88	/// `error.BudgetTooSmall` if tail demand alone exceeds kappa_total.
    89	///
    90	/// paper §3.3 Phase 1: `// micro: n<μ → κ_j=1; small: μ≤n<τ → κ_j=2`
    91	pub fn allocateTail(
    92	    freqs: []const u32,
    93	    kappa: []u32,
    94	    mu: u32,
    95	    tau: u32,
    96	    kappa_total: u32,
    97	) ClusteringError!u32 {
    98	    if (mu == 0 or tau == 0 or mu >= tau) return error.InvalidThresholds;
    99	    if (freqs.len != kappa.len) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   100	
   101	    var tail_used: u32 = 0;
   102	    for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   103	        switch (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau)) {
   104	            .empty => kappa[j] = 0,
   105	            .micro => {
   106	                kappa[j] = 1;
   107	                tail_used += 1;
   108	            },
   109	            .small => {
   110	                kappa[j] = 2;
   111	                tail_used += 2;
   112	            },
   113	            .active => kappa[j] = 0, // filled by phase 2
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    if (tail_used > kappa_total) return error.BudgetTooSmall;
   118	    return kappa_total - tail_used;
   119	}
   120	
   121	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122	// Phase 2: damped scoring (paper §3.2).
   123	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   124	
   125	/// Compute the damped weight for one active token.
   126	/// paper §3.2 eq damped weight: `w_j = √(n_j) · s_j`
   127	pub fn dampedWeight(n_j: u32, s_j: f32) f32 {
   128	    return @sqrt(@as(f32, @floatFromInt(n_j))) * s_j;
   129	}
   130	
   131	/// Phase 2 — proportional κ_j allocation for active tokens.
   132	///
   133	/// Inputs:
   134	///   - freqs[j]:    n_j for each token (used to find active tokens)
   135	///   - spreads[j]:  s_j for each active token; ignored for tail tokens
   136	///                  (caller can leave 0 for empty/micro/small).
   137	///   - kappa[j]:    Phase-1 output (tail κ_j filled, active = 0).
   138	///   - B:           active-token budget from Phase 1.
   139	///   - mu, tau:     thresholds, used to identify active tokens.
   140	///
   141	/// Output:
   142	///   - kappa[j] for active tokens written as ⌊(w_j/Σw) · B⌋.
   143	///   - frac[j]:    fractional remainder (w_j/Σw)·B − κ_j, used by Phase 4.
   144	///                 Tail/empty tokens get frac[j] = 0.
   145	///
   146	/// paper §3.2 eq allocation: `κ_j = ⌊(w_j / Σ_i w_i) · B⌋`.
   147	///
   148	/// paper-gap: if every active token has s_j == 0 (Σw = 0), distribute B
   149	/// equally across active tokens (token_id order, leftover to lowest IDs).
   150	/// Pathological synthetic case only; documented for determinism.
   151	pub fn dampedPhase2(
   152	    freqs: []const u32,
   153	    spreads: []const f32,
   154	    kappa: []u32,
   155	    frac: []f32,
   156	    budget_active: u32,
   157	    mu: u32,
   158	    tau: u32,
   159	) ClusteringError!void {
   160	    if (freqs.len != spreads.len or freqs.len != kappa.len or freqs.len != frac.len) {
   161	        return error.InvalidThresholds;
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    @memset(frac, 0.0);
   165	
   166	    // First pass: compute Σw across active tokens.
   167	    var sum_w: f64 = 0.0;
   168	    var n_active: u32 = 0;
   169	    for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   170	        if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) != .active) continue;
   171	        const w = dampedWeight(n_j, spreads[j]);
   172	        if (!std.math.isFinite(w) or w < 0.0) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   173	        sum_w += @floatCast(w);
   174	        n_active += 1;
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    if (n_active == 0) return; // nothing to allocate; Phase 1 handled all tokens.
   178	
   179	    if (sum_w <= 0.0) {
   180	        // paper-gap: zero total spread — uniform fallback in token-id order.
   181	        const base: u32 = if (n_active == 0) 0 else budget_active / n_active;
   182	        var leftover: u32 = budget_active - base * n_active;
   183	        for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   184	            if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) != .active) continue;
   185	            kappa[j] = base;
   186	            if (leftover > 0) {
   187	                kappa[j] += 1;
   188	                leftover -= 1;
   189	            }
   190	        }
   191	        return;
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    // Second pass: floor allocation; record fractional remainder.
   195	    const B_f: f64 = @floatFromInt(budget_active);
   196	    for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   197	        if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) != .active) continue;
   198	        const w: f64 = @floatCast(dampedWeight(n_j, spreads[j]));
   199	        const target: f64 = (w / sum_w) * B_f;
   200	        const floor_target: u32 = @intFromFloat(@floor(target));
   201	        kappa[j] = floor_target;
   202	        frac[j] = @floatCast(target - @as(f64, @floatFromInt(floor_target)));
   203	    }
   204	}
   205	
   206	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   207	// Phase 3: bounding (paper §3.3).
   208	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   209	
   210	/// Phase 3 — enforce κ_j floor (≥ε) and cap (n_j/κ_j ≥ θ ⟺ κ_j ≤ ⌊n_j/θ⌋).
   211	///
   212	/// Mutates `kappa[j]` for active tokens only. Tail/empty tokens are left as-is
   213	/// (their κ_j was set by Phase 1 and is exempt from these bounds).
   214	///
   215	/// paper §3.3: `floor κ_j ≥ ε` and `cap n_j/κ_j ≥ θ`.
   216	///
   217	/// paper-gap: if cap < floor (i.e. n_j < ε·θ) for some active token, the two
   218	/// bounds conflict. With paper defaults ε=4, θ=39, τ=256 this never happens
   219	/// (ε·θ = 156 < τ = 256, so n_j ≥ 256 ⇒ cap = ⌊n_j/39⌋ ≥ 6 > 4 = floor).
   220	/// We assert this precondition; an out-of-spec caller setting `tau < epsilon*theta`
   221	/// gets `error.InvalidThresholds`.
   222	pub fn boundPhase3(
   223	    freqs: []const u32,
   224	    kappa: []u32,
   225	    mu: u32,
   226	    tau: u32,
   227	    epsilon: u32,
   228	    theta: u32,
   229	) ClusteringError!void {
   230	    if (freqs.len != kappa.len) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   231	    if (epsilon == 0 or theta == 0) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   232	    // Reject thresholds that make floor and cap mutually unsatisfiable on
   233	    // the smallest active token (n_j == tau).
   234	    if (tau < epsilon * theta) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   235	
   236	    for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   237	        if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) != .active) continue;
   238	        const cap: u32 = n_j / theta; // floor division — paper uses ⌊·⌋
   239	        if (kappa[j] < epsilon) kappa[j] = epsilon;
   240	        if (kappa[j] > cap) kappa[j] = cap;
   241	    }
   242	}
   243	
   244	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245	// Phase 4: budget reconciliation (paper §3.3, paper-gap on rule).
   246	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247	
   248	/// Phase 4 — drive Σκ_j to exactly `kappa_total` using bounded redistribution.
   249	///
   250	/// paper-gap: paper §3.3 says only "redistribute surplus/deficit". We implement
   251	/// the canonical Hamilton largest-remainder rule, deterministically tie-broken
   252	/// by token_id so two runs always land identically:
   253	///
   254	///   SURPLUS (delta > 0):
   255	///     Among active tokens with κ_j < cap_j (room to grow), give one centroid
   256	///     to whichever has the largest fractional remainder frac[j]. Ties broken
   257	///     by smallest token_id. Repeat until either delta == 0 or every active
   258	///     token is at its cap.
   259	///
   260	///   DEFICIT (delta < 0):

exec
/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/tac/tac.zig | sed -n '260,620p'" in /Users/satan/side/experiments/ir-multivector-retrieval
exec
/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/tac/tac.zig | sed -n '620,980p'" in /Users/satan/side/experiments/ir-multivector-retrieval
 succeeded in 0ms:
   260	///   DEFICIT (delta < 0):
   261	///     Among active tokens with κ_j > epsilon (room to shrink), take one
   262	///     centroid from whichever has the smallest frac[j]. Ties broken by
   263	///     smallest token_id. Repeat until either delta == 0 or every active
   264	///     token is at its floor.
   265	///
   266	///     If we exhaust active room and still have deficit, we degrade tail tokens:
   267	///     small (κ_j=2) → 1, then micro (κ_j=1) → 0. paper-gap; never happens
   268	///     for paper-default parameters in production but kept for robustness.
   269	///
   270	/// Termination: each loop iteration changes exactly one κ_j by ±1 toward the
   271	/// goal. The total work is bounded by |delta| ≤ kappa_total, so worst case
   272	/// O(kappa_total · N_T) — fine since N_T is small (vocab ≤ 30k for ColBERT).
   273	///
   274	/// `error.BudgetTooLarge` if the total cap-room is insufficient for surplus.
   275	pub fn reconcilePhase4(
   276	    freqs: []const u32,
   277	    kappa: []u32,
   278	    frac: []const f32,
   279	    kappa_total: u32,
   280	    mu: u32,
   281	    tau: u32,
   282	    epsilon: u32,
   283	    theta: u32,
   284	) ClusteringError!void {
   285	    if (freqs.len != kappa.len or freqs.len != frac.len) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   286	
   287	    var current: u64 = 0;
   288	    for (kappa) |k| current += k;
   289	
   290	    if (current == kappa_total) return;
   291	
   292	    if (current < kappa_total) {
   293	        // SURPLUS — grow.
   294	        var remaining: u32 = @intCast(kappa_total - current);
   295	        while (remaining > 0) {
   296	            // Find active token with largest frac[j] subject to κ_j < cap_j.
   297	            var best: ?usize = null;
   298	            var best_frac: f32 = -std.math.inf(f32);
   299	            for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   300	                if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) != .active) continue;
   301	                const cap: u32 = n_j / theta;
   302	                if (kappa[j] >= cap) continue;
   303	                if (frac[j] > best_frac or (frac[j] == best_frac and best == null)) {
   304	                    best = j;
   305	                    best_frac = frac[j];
   306	                }
   307	            }
   308	            if (best) |idx| {
   309	                kappa[idx] += 1;
   310	                remaining -= 1;
   311	            } else {
   312	                return error.BudgetTooLarge;
   313	            }
   314	        }
   315	        return;
   316	    }
   317	
   318	    // DEFICIT — shrink.
   319	    var excess: u32 = @intCast(current - kappa_total);
   320	    while (excess > 0) {
   321	        // Step 1: shrink active tokens (κ_j > epsilon) by smallest frac.
   322	        var best: ?usize = null;
   323	        var best_frac: f32 = std.math.inf(f32);
   324	        for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   325	            if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) != .active) continue;
   326	            if (kappa[j] <= epsilon) continue;
   327	            if (frac[j] < best_frac) {
   328	                best = j;
   329	                best_frac = frac[j];
   330	            }
   331	        }
   332	        if (best) |idx| {
   333	            kappa[idx] -= 1;
   334	            excess -= 1;
   335	            continue;
   336	        }
   337	
   338	        // Step 2: degrade small tokens (κ_j == 2) to κ_j == 1.
   339	        // paper-gap: paper does not address; we degrade in token-id order.
   340	        var found: ?usize = null;
   341	        for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   342	            if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) == .small and kappa[j] == 2) {
   343	                found = j;
   344	                break;
   345	            }
   346	        }
   347	        if (found) |idx| {
   348	            kappa[idx] = 1;
   349	            excess -= 1;
   350	            continue;
   351	        }
   352	
   353	        // Step 3: degrade micro tokens (κ_j == 1) to κ_j == 0.
   354	        for (freqs, 0..) |n_j, j| {
   355	            if (classifyToken(n_j, mu, tau) == .micro and kappa[j] == 1) {
   356	                found = j;
   357	                break;
   358	            }
   359	        }
   360	        if (found) |idx| {
   361	            kappa[idx] = 0;
   362	            excess -= 1;
   363	            continue;
   364	        }
   365	
   366	        // No room left anywhere — kappa_total cannot be expressed.
   367	        // This is the symmetric BudgetTooSmall case discovered late.
   368	        return error.BudgetTooSmall;
   369	    }
   370	}
   371	
   372	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   373	// Phase combinator: run 1+2+3+4 on per-token arrays.
   374	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   375	
   376	/// Run all four phases on pre-grouped per-token (freq, spread) arrays.
   377	///
   378	/// Output `kappa[j]` satisfies all paper §3.3 invariants:
   379	///   - tier semantics (micro→1, small→2, active≥ε with cap n_j/θ)
   380	///   - Σ kappa[j] == kappa_total (mod degraded edge cases — see Phase 4)
   381	///
   382	/// `frac` is scratch (len = freqs.len), used by Phase 4. Caller-allocated to
   383	/// avoid hidden allocation in this function.
   384	pub fn allocateBudgets(
   385	    freqs: []const u32,
   386	    spreads: []const f32,
   387	    kappa: []u32,
   388	    frac: []f32,
   389	    kappa_total: u32,
   390	    p: ClusteringParams,
   391	) ClusteringError!void {
   392	    const B = try allocateTail(freqs, kappa, p.mu, p.tau, kappa_total);
   393	    try dampedPhase2(freqs, spreads, kappa, frac, B, p.mu, p.tau);
   394	    try boundPhase3(freqs, kappa, p.mu, p.tau, p.epsilon, p.theta);
   395	    try reconcilePhase4(freqs, kappa, frac, kappa_total, p.mu, p.tau, p.epsilon, p.theta);
   396	}
   397	
   398	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   399	// Public driver — TAC.cluster end-to-end.
   400	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   401	
   402	const token_dump = @import("../io/token_dump.zig");
   403	
   404	pub const ClusteringResult = struct {
   405	    /// `kappa_total · dim`, concatenated in token-id order.
   406	    centroids: []f32,
   407	    /// Length `n_tokens` — global centroid id for input vector `i`, in `[0, kappa_total)`.
   408	    assignments: []u32,
   409	    /// Length `n_distinct_tokens` — Σ == kappa_total.
   410	    kappa_per_token: []u32,
   411	    /// Σ_j WCSS_j across all tokens (regression metric for #10).
   412	    wcss_total: f32,
   413	
   414	    pub fn deinit(self: *ClusteringResult, gpa: Allocator) void {
   415	        gpa.free(self.centroids);
   416	        gpa.free(self.assignments);
   417	        gpa.free(self.kappa_per_token);
   418	        self.* = undefined;
   419	    }
   420	};
   421	
   422	/// Cluster a TokenDump corpus using TAC (paper §3 four-phase pipeline).
   423	/// Thin wrapper over `clusterFlat`; the flat form is the testable contract.
   424	pub fn cluster(
   425	    td: token_dump.TokenDump,
   426	    p: ClusteringParams,
   427	    gpa: Allocator,
   428	) ClusteringError!ClusteringResult {
   429	    return clusterFlat(td.token_ids, td.vectors, td.dim, p, gpa);
   430	}
   431	
   432	/// Cluster a corpus of token vectors using TAC (paper §3 four-phase pipeline).
   433	///
   434	/// Inputs:
   435	///   - `token_ids[i]`: vocabulary id of the i-th vector. Vocabulary size N_T
   436	///     is inferred as max(token_ids)+1.
   437	///   - `vectors[i*dim..(i+1)*dim]`: the i-th token vector.
   438	///   - `p.kappa_total`: global centroid budget κ.
   439	///
   440	/// Output (caller-owned via `ClusteringResult.deinit`):
   441	///   - `centroids`:        kappa_total · dim, concatenated in token-id order
   442	///   - `assignments[i]`:   global centroid id for input vector i (in 0..κ)
   443	///   - `kappa_per_token[j]`: κ_j; Σ == kappa_total
   444	///   - `wcss_total`:       Σ_j WCSS_j over all tokens (regression metric)
   445	///
   446	/// paper §3.3 phase order: Phase 1+2+3+4 produce κ_j, then per-token Lloyd
   447	/// produces centroids. Per-token determinism: seed = p.seed XOR token_id.
   448	
   449	/// Run k-means for one token's vectors. Materialises rows into the
   450	/// caller-provided `gather`, fits, copies centroids+assignments into the
   451	/// global slabs at disjoint indices. Pure from caller's perspective.
   452	fn runOneTokenKmeans(
   453	    j: usize,
   454	    k_j: u32,
   455	    n_j: u32,
   456	    group_offsets: []const u32,
   457	    group_indices: []const u32,
   458	    vectors: []const f32,
   459	    dim: u32,
   460	    p: ClusteringParams,
   461	    global_off: u32,
   462	    centroids: []f32,
   463	    assignments: []u32,
   464	    gather: []f32,
   465	    gpa: Allocator,
   466	) !f32 {
   467	    const start = group_offsets[j];
   468	    const end = group_offsets[j + 1];
   469	
   470	    var w: usize = 0;
   471	    var idx_i: u32 = start;
   472	    while (idx_i < end) : (idx_i += 1) {
   473	        const src_idx: usize = group_indices[idx_i];
   474	        @memcpy(
   475	            gather[w * @as(usize, dim) ..][0..dim],
   476	            vectors[src_idx * @as(usize, dim) ..][0..dim],
   477	        );
   478	        w += 1;
   479	    }
   480	
   481	    var res = try kmeans.fit(
   482	        gather[0 .. @as(usize, n_j) * dim],
   483	        dim,
   484	        .{
   485	            .k = k_j,
   486	            .max_iters = p.max_iters,
   487	            .tol = p.tol,
   488	            .seed = p.seed ^ @as(u64, j),
   489	        },
   490	        gpa,
   491	    );
   492	    defer res.deinit(gpa);
   493	
   494	    @memcpy(
   495	        centroids[@as(usize, global_off) * dim ..][0 .. @as(usize, k_j) * dim],
   496	        res.centroids[0 .. @as(usize, k_j) * dim],
   497	    );
   498	
   499	    w = 0;
   500	    idx_i = start;
   501	    while (idx_i < end) : (idx_i += 1) {
   502	        const src_idx: usize = group_indices[idx_i];
   503	        assignments[src_idx] = global_off + res.assignments[w];
   504	        w += 1;
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    return res.wcss;
   508	}
   509	
   510	/// Worker wrapper for std.Thread.Pool. Allocates its own `gather` slab
   511	/// (size n_j*dim) and writes results to caller-owned slots; errors are
   512	/// captured rather than thrown because Pool.spawnWg expects a
   513	/// void-returning function.
   514	fn runOneTokenWorker(
   515	    j: usize,
   516	    k_j: u32,
   517	    n_j: u32,
   518	    group_offsets: []const u32,
   519	    group_indices: []const u32,
   520	    vectors: []const f32,
   521	    dim: u32,
   522	    p: ClusteringParams,
   523	    global_off: u32,
   524	    centroids: []f32,
   525	    assignments: []u32,
   526	    wcss_out: *f32,
   527	    err_out: *?ClusteringError,
   528	    gpa: Allocator,
   529	) void {
   530	    const gather = gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, n_j) * dim) catch |err| {
   531	        err_out.* = err;
   532	        return;
   533	    };
   534	    defer gpa.free(gather);
   535	
   536	    const wcss = runOneTokenKmeans(
   537	        j,
   538	        k_j,
   539	        n_j,
   540	        group_offsets,
   541	        group_indices,
   542	        vectors,
   543	        dim,
   544	        p,
   545	        global_off,
   546	        centroids,
   547	        assignments,
   548	        gather,
   549	        gpa,
   550	    ) catch |err| {
   551	        err_out.* = err;
   552	        return;
   553	    };
   554	    wcss_out.* = wcss;
   555	}
   556	
   557	pub fn clusterFlat(
   558	    token_ids: []const u32,
   559	    vectors: []const f32,
   560	    dim: u32,
   561	    p: ClusteringParams,
   562	    gpa: Allocator,
   563	) ClusteringError!ClusteringResult {
   564	    if (token_ids.len == 0) return error.EmptyTokenDump;
   565	    if (dim == 0) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   566	    if (vectors.len != token_ids.len * @as(usize, dim)) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   567	    if (p.kappa_total == 0) return error.BudgetTooSmall;
   568	
   569	    // ---- Step 0: vocabulary size + per-token frequencies. ----
   570	    var max_id: u32 = 0;
   571	    for (token_ids) |t| {
   572	        if (t > max_id) max_id = t;
   573	    }
   574	    const n_distinct: usize = @as(usize, max_id) + 1;
   575	
   576	    const freqs = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_distinct);
   577	    defer gpa.free(freqs);
   578	    @memset(freqs, 0);
   579	    for (token_ids) |t| freqs[t] += 1;
   580	
   581	    // ---- Step 1: CSR-grouping of input indices by token id. ----
   582	    const group_offsets = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_distinct + 1);
   583	    defer gpa.free(group_offsets);
   584	    group_offsets[0] = 0;
   585	    for (0..n_distinct) |j| group_offsets[j + 1] = group_offsets[j] + freqs[j];
   586	
   587	    const group_indices = try gpa.alloc(u32, token_ids.len);
   588	    defer gpa.free(group_indices);
   589	    {
   590	        const cursor = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_distinct);
   591	        defer gpa.free(cursor);
   592	        @memcpy(cursor, group_offsets[0..n_distinct]);
   593	        for (token_ids, 0..) |t, i| {
   594	            group_indices[cursor[t]] = @intCast(i);
   595	            cursor[t] += 1;
   596	        }
   597	    }
   598	
   599	    // ---- Step 2: per-token spread s_j (paper §3.2) via Welford's algorithm.
   600	    // Welford gives a numerically stable streaming variance; mathematically
   601	    // equivalent to the paper's `s_j = (1/n_j)·Σ‖t_{j,i} - t̄_j‖²`. We
   602	    // accumulate component-wise M2 then sum to get the trace-of-covariance.
   603	    const spreads = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_distinct);
   604	    defer gpa.free(spreads);
   605	    @memset(spreads, 0.0);
   606	
   607	    {
   608	        const mean_buf = try gpa.alloc(f32, dim);
   609	        defer gpa.free(mean_buf);
   610	        const m2_buf = try gpa.alloc(f32, dim);
   611	        defer gpa.free(m2_buf);
   612	
   613	        for (0..n_distinct) |j| {
   614	            const start = group_offsets[j];
   615	            const end = group_offsets[j + 1];
   616	            const n_j = end - start;
   617	            if (n_j == 0) continue;
   618	            @memset(mean_buf, 0.0);
   619	            @memset(m2_buf, 0.0);
   620	            var count: f32 = 0.0;

 succeeded in 0ms:
   620	            var count: f32 = 0.0;
   621	            var idx_i: u32 = start;
   622	            while (idx_i < end) : (idx_i += 1) {
   623	                const v = vectors[group_indices[idx_i] * @as(usize, dim) ..][0..dim];
   624	                count += 1.0;
   625	                const inv_count: f32 = 1.0 / count;
   626	                for (0..dim) |dd| {
   627	                    const x = v[dd];
   628	                    const old_mean = mean_buf[dd];
   629	                    const delta = x - old_mean;
   630	                    mean_buf[dd] = old_mean + delta * inv_count;
   631	                    m2_buf[dd] += delta * (x - mean_buf[dd]);
   632	                }
   633	            }
   634	            var trace: f32 = 0.0;
   635	            for (m2_buf) |m| trace += m;
   636	            spreads[j] = trace / @as(f32, @floatFromInt(n_j));
   637	        }
   638	    }
   639	
   640	    // ---- Step 3: phases 1+2+3+4 produce κ_j per token. ----
   641	    const kappa_per_token = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_distinct);
   642	    errdefer gpa.free(kappa_per_token);
   643	    const frac = try gpa.alloc(f32, n_distinct);
   644	    defer gpa.free(frac);
   645	
   646	    try allocateBudgets(freqs, spreads, kappa_per_token, frac, p.kappa_total, p);
   647	
   648	    // Post-condition: any token with freq > 0 must have κ_j ≥ 1, otherwise
   649	    // we'd be unable to assign its vectors to any centroid. Phase 4's deficit
   650	    // fallback can degrade micro tokens to κ_j=0; surface that as
   651	    // `error.BudgetTooSmall` (paper-gap) rather than ship corrupt assignments.
   652	    for (freqs, kappa_per_token) |n_j, k_j| {
   653	        if (n_j > 0 and k_j == 0) return error.BudgetTooSmall;
   654	    }
   655	
   656	    // ---- Step 4: per-token Lloyd's, gathering each token's vectors. ----
   657	    const global_offsets = try gpa.alloc(u32, n_distinct + 1);
   658	    defer gpa.free(global_offsets);
   659	    global_offsets[0] = 0;
   660	    for (0..n_distinct) |j| global_offsets[j + 1] = global_offsets[j] + kappa_per_token[j];
   661	
   662	    const total_centroids = global_offsets[n_distinct];
   663	    if (total_centroids != p.kappa_total) {
   664	        return error.BudgetTooSmall;
   665	    }
   666	
   667	    const centroids = try gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, total_centroids) * dim);
   668	    errdefer gpa.free(centroids);
   669	    const assignments = try gpa.alloc(u32, token_ids.len);
   670	    errdefer gpa.free(assignments);
   671	
   672	    var max_n_j: u32 = 0;
   673	    for (freqs) |n_j| {
   674	        if (n_j > max_n_j) max_n_j = n_j;
   675	    }
   676	
   677	    var wcss_total: f32 = 0.0;
   678	
   679	    if (p.n_threads <= 1) {
   680	        // ---- Serial path (original; preserves test determinism). ----
   681	        const gather = try gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, max_n_j) * dim);
   682	        defer gpa.free(gather);
   683	
   684	        for (0..n_distinct) |j| {
   685	            const k_j = kappa_per_token[j];
   686	            if (k_j == 0) continue;
   687	            const n_j = group_offsets[j + 1] - group_offsets[j];
   688	            if (n_j == 0) continue;
   689	
   690	            wcss_total += try runOneTokenKmeans(
   691	                j,
   692	                k_j,
   693	                n_j,
   694	                group_offsets,
   695	                group_indices,
   696	                vectors,
   697	                dim,
   698	                p,
   699	                global_offsets[j],
   700	                centroids,
   701	                assignments,
   702	                gather,
   703	                gpa,
   704	            );
   705	        }
   706	    } else {
   707	        // ---- Parallel path (paper §3 "independent κ_j-means per token"). ----
   708	        // Static chunk distribution: each of n_threads threads owns a
   709	        // contiguous slice of token IDs [lo, hi) and processes them
   710	        // sequentially with one persistent gather buffer per thread. This
   711	        // avoids std.Thread.Pool (gone in Zig 0.16; std.Io.Group replaces it
   712	        // but threading an Io through TAC is invasive). Load balance is
   713	        // good for 8K+ tokens × 10 threads since per-token work varies but
   714	        // averages by law of large numbers; if we ever profile and find
   715	        // tail tokens dominating, switch to a work-stealing queue.
   716	        const n_threads = p.n_threads;
   717	        const ChunkCtx = struct {
   718	            kappa_per_token: []const u32,
   719	            group_offsets: []const u32,
   720	            group_indices: []const u32,
   721	            global_offsets: []const u32,
   722	            vectors: []const f32,
   723	            dim: u32,
   724	            params: ClusteringParams,
   725	            max_n_j: u32,
   726	            centroids: []f32,
   727	            assignments: []u32,
   728	            gpa: Allocator,
   729	            wcss_out: f32,
   730	            err_out: ?ClusteringError,
   731	            lo: usize,
   732	            hi: usize,
   733	        };
   734	
   735	        const ChunkRunner = struct {
   736	            fn run(ctx: *ChunkCtx) void {
   737	                const gather = ctx.gpa.alloc(f32, @as(usize, ctx.max_n_j) * ctx.dim) catch |err| {
   738	                    ctx.err_out = err;
   739	                    return;
   740	                };
   741	                defer ctx.gpa.free(gather);
   742	
   743	                var wcss: f32 = 0.0;
   744	                var j = ctx.lo;
   745	                while (j < ctx.hi) : (j += 1) {
   746	                    const k_j = ctx.kappa_per_token[j];
   747	                    if (k_j == 0) continue;
   748	                    const n_j = ctx.group_offsets[j + 1] - ctx.group_offsets[j];
   749	                    if (n_j == 0) continue;
   750	                    const w = runOneTokenKmeans(
   751	                        j,
   752	                        k_j,
   753	                        n_j,
   754	                        ctx.group_offsets,
   755	                        ctx.group_indices,
   756	                        ctx.vectors,
   757	                        ctx.dim,
   758	                        ctx.params,
   759	                        ctx.global_offsets[j],
   760	                        ctx.centroids,
   761	                        ctx.assignments,
   762	                        gather,
   763	                        ctx.gpa,
   764	                    ) catch |err| {
   765	                        ctx.err_out = err;
   766	                        return;
   767	                    };
   768	                    wcss += w;
   769	                }
   770	                ctx.wcss_out = wcss;
   771	            }
   772	        };
   773	
   774	        const ctxs = try gpa.alloc(ChunkCtx, n_threads);
   775	        defer gpa.free(ctxs);
   776	        const threads = try gpa.alloc(std.Thread, n_threads);
   777	        defer gpa.free(threads);
   778	
   779	        const chunk = (n_distinct + n_threads - 1) / n_threads;
   780	        for (0..n_threads) |t| {
   781	            const lo = t * chunk;
   782	            const hi = @min((t + 1) * chunk, n_distinct);
   783	            ctxs[t] = .{
   784	                .kappa_per_token = kappa_per_token,
   785	                .group_offsets = group_offsets,
   786	                .group_indices = group_indices,
   787	                .global_offsets = global_offsets,
   788	                .vectors = vectors,
   789	                .dim = dim,
   790	                .params = p,
   791	                .max_n_j = max_n_j,
   792	                .centroids = centroids,
   793	                .assignments = assignments,
   794	                .gpa = gpa,
   795	                .wcss_out = 0.0,
   796	                .err_out = null,
   797	                .lo = lo,
   798	                .hi = hi,
   799	            };
   800	            threads[t] = try std.Thread.spawn(.{}, ChunkRunner.run, .{&ctxs[t]});
   801	        }
   802	        for (threads) |th| th.join();
   803	
   804	        for (ctxs) |c| {
   805	            if (c.err_out) |err| return err;
   806	            wcss_total += c.wcss_out;
   807	        }
   808	    }
   809	
   810	    // Final invariant: every assignment is a valid centroid id.
   811	    for (assignments) |a| {
   812	        if (a >= total_centroids) return error.InvalidThresholds;
   813	    }
   814	
   815	    return .{
   816	        .centroids = centroids,
   817	        .assignments = assignments,
   818	        .kappa_per_token = kappa_per_token,
   819	        .wcss_total = wcss_total,
   820	    };
   821	}

   829	test "classifyToken: empty/micro/small/active" {
   830	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.empty, classifyToken(0, 128, 256));
   831	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.micro, classifyToken(1, 128, 256));
   832	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.micro, classifyToken(127, 128, 256));
   833	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.small, classifyToken(128, 128, 256));
   834	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.small, classifyToken(255, 128, 256));
   835	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.active, classifyToken(256, 128, 256));
   836	    try testing.expectEqual(TokenTier.active, classifyToken(10_000, 128, 256));
   837	}
   838	
   839	test "allocateTail: small thresholds (mu=3, tau=5)" {
   840	    // freqs:        [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
   841	    // tier:         [E, M, M, S, S, A, A, A]
   842	    // tail kappa:   [0, 1, 1, 2, 2, 0, 0, 0]
   843	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 };
   844	    var kappa = [_]u32{0} ** 8;
   845	    const B = try allocateTail(&freqs, &kappa, 3, 5, 50);
   846	    // tail_used = 1+1+2+2 = 6; B = 50 - 6 = 44.
   847	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 44), B);
   848	    try testing.expectEqualSlices(u32, &.{ 0, 1, 1, 2, 2, 0, 0, 0 }, &kappa);
   849	}
   850	
   851	test "allocateTail: kappa_total too small for tail" {
   852	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 1, 1, 4 }; // tail_used = 1+1+2 = 4
   853	    var kappa = [_]u32{0} ** 3;
   854	    try testing.expectError(error.BudgetTooSmall, allocateTail(&freqs, &kappa, 3, 5, 3));
   855	}

   858	    const freqs = [_]u32{1};
   859	    var kappa = [_]u32{0};

   861	    try testing.expectError(error.InvalidThresholds, allocateTail(&freqs, &kappa, 6, 5, 10));
   862	    try testing.expectError(error.InvalidThresholds, allocateTail(&freqs, &kappa, 0, 5, 10));
   863	}

   865	test "dampedWeight: paper formula w_j = sqrt(n_j) * s_j" {
   866	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 0.0), dampedWeight(0, 5.0), 1e-6);
   867	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 4.0), dampedWeight(4, 2.0), 1e-6); // sqrt(4)*2 = 4
   868	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 30.0), dampedWeight(100, 3.0), 1e-4); // 10*3
   869	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 0.0), dampedWeight(9, 0.0), 1e-6);
   870	}

   872	test "dampedPhase2: two equal-spread tokens split B in proportion to sqrt(n)" {
   873	    // mu=3, tau=5: active threshold = 5.
   874	    // Token A: n=9,  s=2   → w_A = 3*2 = 6
   875	    // Token B: n=16, s=2   → w_B = 4*2 = 8
   876	    // Σw = 14; B = 70 → κ_A = floor(6/14*70) = floor(30) = 30
   877	    //                    κ_B = floor(8/14*70) = floor(40) = 40
   878	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 9, 16 };
   879	    const spreads = [_]f32{ 2.0, 2.0 };
   880	    var kappa = [_]u32{ 0, 0 };
   881	    var frac = [_]f32{ 0, 0 };
   882	    try dampedPhase2(&freqs, &spreads, &kappa, &frac, 70, 3, 5);
   883	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 30), kappa[0]);
   884	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 40), kappa[1]);
   885	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 0.0), frac[0], 1e-3);
   886	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 0.0), frac[1], 1e-3);
   887	}
   888	
   889	test "dampedPhase2: spread dominates over equal-frequency tokens" {
   890	    // Two tokens with same n, different s — proportional to s.
   891	    // Token A: n=25, s=1 → w=5
   892	    // Token B: n=25, s=4 → w=20
   893	    // Σw = 25; B = 100 → κ_A = 20, κ_B = 80.
   894	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 25, 25 };
   895	    const spreads = [_]f32{ 1.0, 4.0 };
   896	    var kappa = [_]u32{ 0, 0 };
   897	    var frac = [_]f32{ 0, 0 };
   898	    try dampedPhase2(&freqs, &spreads, &kappa, &frac, 100, 3, 5);
   899	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 20), kappa[0]);
   900	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 80), kappa[1]);
   901	}
   902	
   903	test "dampedPhase2: zero-spread fallback splits uniformly" {
   904	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 10, 10, 10 }; // all active under mu=3, tau=5
   905	    const spreads = [_]f32{ 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 };
   906	    var kappa = [_]u32{ 0, 0, 0 };
   907	    var frac = [_]f32{ 0, 0, 0 };
   908	    try dampedPhase2(&freqs, &spreads, &kappa, &frac, 11, 3, 5);
   909	    // 11 / 3 = 3 base, leftover 2 → first two tokens get +1.
   910	    try testing.expectEqualSlices(u32, &.{ 4, 4, 3 }, &kappa);
   911	}
   912	
   913	test "dampedPhase2: fractional remainder recorded" {
   914	    // n=100, s=1 ⇒ w=10. Σw = 10. B=10 → κ = floor(10) = 10, frac = 0.
   915	    // Make it not divide evenly: B=7 → target=7.0, κ=7, frac=0.
   916	    // Better: use 2 tokens.
   917	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 9, 16 };
   918	    const spreads = [_]f32{ 1.0, 1.0 }; // w=3, 4. Σw=7.
   919	    var kappa = [_]u32{ 0, 0 };
   920	    var frac = [_]f32{ 0, 0 };
   921	    try dampedPhase2(&freqs, &spreads, &kappa, &frac, 10, 3, 5);
   922	    // target_A = 3/7*10 = 4.2857… → κ=4, frac≈0.2857
   923	    // target_B = 4/7*10 = 5.7142… → κ=5, frac≈0.7143
   924	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 4), kappa[0]);
   925	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 5), kappa[1]);
   926	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 0.2857), frac[0], 1e-3);
   927	    try testing.expectApproxEqAbs(@as(f32, 0.7143), frac[1], 1e-3);
   928	}
   929	
   930	test "boundPhase3: floor lifts kappa up to epsilon" {
   931	    // Active token with n=200, kappa=1 → after floor (eps=4) kappa=4.
   932	    // Cap = 200/39 = 5; so 4 ≤ 5, accepted.
   933	    const freqs = [_]u32{200};
   934	    var kappa = [_]u32{1};
   935	    try boundPhase3(&freqs, &kappa, 128, 156, 4, 39);
   936	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 4), kappa[0]);
   937	}
   938	
   939	test "boundPhase3: cap caps kappa at floor(n_j/theta)" {
   940	    // n=156, theta=39 → cap = 4. kappa=10 → after cap kappa=4.
   941	    // (paper-default thresholds: tau=256, but we use tau=156 here so this token IS active.)
   942	    const freqs = [_]u32{156};
   943	    var kappa = [_]u32{10};
   944	    try boundPhase3(&freqs, &kappa, 128, 156, 4, 39);
   945	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 4), kappa[0]);
   946	}
   947	
   948	test "boundPhase3: ignores tail tokens" {
   949	    // mu=128, tau=256. Tokens with n<128 (micro) and 128≤n<256 (small) are skipped.
   950	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 50, 200, 500 }; // micro, small, active
   951	    var kappa = [_]u32{ 1, 2, 1 };
   952	    try boundPhase3(&freqs, &kappa, 128, 256, 4, 39);
   953	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 1), kappa[0]); // micro untouched
   954	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 2), kappa[1]); // small untouched
   955	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 4), kappa[2]); // active floored to eps
   956	}
   957	
   958	test "boundPhase3: rejects threshold combo where cap < floor" {
   959	    // tau=100 < epsilon*theta = 4*39 = 156 → conflict possible at smallest active.
   960	    const freqs = [_]u32{100};
   961	    var kappa = [_]u32{0};
   962	    try testing.expectError(error.InvalidThresholds, boundPhase3(&freqs, &kappa, 50, 100, 4, 39));
   963	}
   964	
   965	test "reconcilePhase4: surplus distributed to largest fractional remainder" {
   966	    // Two active tokens (mu=3, tau=5). Phase-2 leaves κ=4,5 (Σ=9), kappa_total=12 → surplus 3.
   967	    // frac = [0.3, 0.8] — most surplus goes to j=1 first (largest frac).
   968	    const freqs = [_]u32{ 10_000, 10_000 }; // big enough caps don't bind
   969	    var kappa = [_]u32{ 4, 5 };
   970	    const frac = [_]f32{ 0.3, 0.8 };
   971	    try reconcilePhase4(&freqs, &kappa, &frac, 12, 3, 5, 4, 39);
   972	    // Largest frac wins each step, but we re-evaluate every iter — j=1 keeps
   973	    // winning because frac doesn't change. So j=1 gets all 3 surplus.
   974	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 4), kappa[0]);
   975	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 8), kappa[1]);
   976	    try testing.expectEqual(@as(u32, 12), kappa[0] + kappa[1]);
   977	}
   978	
   979	test "reconcilePhase4: deficit shrinks smallest-frac token first, respects epsilon floor" {
   980	    // Active tokens (mu=3, tau=5). Phase-3 left κ=[10, 8] Σ=18; kappa_total=14 → deficit 4.

README.md:58:Build was 47.5 s single-thread on the 2 k chunk; we got it to 12.4 s by parallelising the four embarrassingly-parallel stages (TAC per-token, PQ train per-subspace, PQ encode per-token, residuals per-token). HNSW build was the next lever — at 26 k it was 17.0 s serial; we replaced it with a chunked deferred-commit parallel build (paper-strict deterministic) which lands at **2.8 s on 10 threads (≈6× speedup)**, saving 14 s on full-corpus build wall time. PQ training stayed dominant because M=32 subspaces / 10 threads with a static `chunk = ceil(32/10) = 4` left fast workers idle while slow ones ground out the last subspace in their chunk. **Plan-10** swapped the static chunker for a shared `std.atomic.Value(u32)` work-stealing counter — every worker pulls the next subspace via `fetchAdd(1, .monotonic)` until the index reaches `PQ_M`. Lands at **97.1 s on full-Jira (was 115.8 s, ≈16 % off)**; the gain is bounded because most subspaces converge in similar Lloyd-iter counts, so the slack the static chunker left on the table was real but smaller than the 30-40 % ceiling. **Plan-11** then comptime-specialized the distance kernels (`vec.dot`, `vec.l2sq`, `vec.normalizeInPlace`) for the four dims that actually appear in the codebase — 2/4 (PQ subspaces) and 64/128 (jina-colbert-v2-64, ColBERTv2.0). **Full-Jira build dropped from 1:34 → 1:23 (1.13×, ≈12 % off total wall time), with `pq.train` itself going 84.8 s → 74.9 s.** Index file is byte-equal vs the generic kernel (paper §9 single-thread retrieval semantics unchanged) and build is byte-equal across n_threads ∈ {1, 4, 10}. Supporting microbench (median of 5, kmeans-assign k=256, the dominant `vec.l2sq` caller in pq.train): dim=2 1.14×, dim=64 1.53×, dim=128 1.42× — the per-call wins are larger than the full-build delta because the kernels also amortize fixed pq.train scaffolding (codebook init, partition counts, Lloyd convergence detection) that didn't get faster. The remaining levers, in order of payoff:
README.md:60:1. **Within-subspace k-means parallelism** — *infrastructure shipped ([plan-07](.codex/plans/post-hackathon-07-within-subspace-kmeans.plan.md))* but inactive at the typical 10-thread invocation: `kmeans.fit` takes `n_threads` and parallelises the per-vector argmin across static chunks while keeping the f32 reduction order serial-in-vector-order, so n_threads=1 ↔ n_threads=10 produce **byte-equal** centroids (paper-strict). At `--threads 10` on M=32 / 10 cores the subspace-level parallelism already saturates; passing more inner threads only oversubscribes. The within-subspace path gates on `n_threads > M`, so it activates when a user runs e.g. `--threads 64`.
