   118	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119	# BERT-base forward in MLX
   120	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121	
   122	
   123	class BertSelfAttention(nn.Module):
   124	    """Multi-head self-attention block matching HF BertSelfAttention's
   125	    weight layout exactly. q/k/v are separate Linear layers (HF stores
   126	    them split, not as a fused QKV)."""
   127	
   128	    def __init__(self, hidden: int, n_heads: int):
   129	        super().__init__()
   130	        if hidden % n_heads != 0:
   131	            raise SystemExit(f"hidden {hidden} not divisible by n_heads {n_heads}")
   132	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   133	        self.head_dim = hidden // n_heads
   134	        self.q = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   135	        self.k = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   136	        self.v = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   137	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   138	        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(hidden)
   139	
   140	    def __call__(self, x: mx.array, attn_bias: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   141	        B, T, H = x.shape
   142	        n = self.n_heads
   143	        d = self.head_dim
   144	        scale = 1.0 / (d ** 0.5)
   145	
   146	        q = self.q(x).reshape(B, T, n, d).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)  # [B,n,T,d]
   147	        k = self.k(x).reshape(B, T, n, d).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
   148	        v = self.v(x).reshape(B, T, n, d).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
   149	
   150	        # Fused SDPA: softmax internally promotes to fp32, fewer kernel launches
   151	        # and less memory traffic than the manual q@k.T -> softmax -> @v path.
   152	        # attn_bias is additive [B,1,1,T] and broadcasts to [B,n,T,T].
   153	        ctx = mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(
   154	            q, k, v, scale=scale, mask=attn_bias
   155	        )  # [B, n, T, d]
   156	        ctx = ctx.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(B, T, H)
   157	
   158	        # output projection + residual + LN (BERT post-LN)
   159	        return self.ln(self.out(ctx) + x)
   160	
   161	
   162	class BertFFN(nn.Module):
   163	    """Position-wise FFN: up-project (gelu) → down-project → residual + LN."""
   164	
   165	    def __init__(self, hidden: int, intermediate: int):
   166	        super().__init__()
   167	        self.up = nn.Linear(hidden, intermediate)
   168	        self.down = nn.Linear(intermediate, hidden)
   169	        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(hidden)
   170	
   171	    def __call__(self, x: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   172	        h = self.up(x)
   173	        h = nn.gelu(h)
   174	        h = self.down(h)
   175	        return self.ln(h + x)
   176	
   177	
   178	class BertLayer(nn.Module):
   179	    def __init__(self, hidden: int, n_heads: int, intermediate: int):
   180	        super().__init__()
   181	        self.attn = BertSelfAttention(hidden, n_heads)
   182	        self.ff = BertFFN(hidden, intermediate)
   183	
   184	    def __call__(self, x: mx.array, attn_bias: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   185	        x = self.attn(x, attn_bias)
   186	        x = self.ff(x)
   187	        return x
   188	
   189	
   190	class BertEmbeddings(nn.Module):
   191	    def __init__(self, vocab: int, max_pos: int, type_vocab: int, hidden: int):
   192	        super().__init__()
   193	        self.word = nn.Embedding(vocab, hidden)
   194	        self.position = nn.Embedding(max_pos, hidden)
   195	        self.token_type = nn.Embedding(type_vocab, hidden)
   196	        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(hidden)
   197	
   198	    def __call__(self, input_ids: mx.array, token_type_ids: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   199	        T = input_ids.shape[1]
   200	        pos_ids = mx.arange(T, dtype=mx.int32)
   201	        # broadcast pos_ids over batch
   202	        x = self.word(input_ids) + self.position(pos_ids) + self.token_type(token_type_ids)
   203	        return self.ln(x)
   204	
   205	
   206	class ColBertMLX(nn.Module):
   207	    """BERT base + final linear projection (no bias) + optional L2-normalize.
   208	    Forward returns the per-token L2-normalised projected embeddings."""
   209	
   210	    def __init__(self, cfg: dict):
   211	        super().__init__()
   212	        self.cfg = cfg
   213	        self.embeddings = BertEmbeddings(
   214	            vocab=cfg["vocab_size"],
   215	            max_pos=cfg["max_position_embeddings"],
   216	            type_vocab=cfg["type_vocab_size"],
   217	            hidden=cfg["hidden_size"],
   218	        )
   219	        self.layers = [
   220	            BertLayer(

 succeeded in 0ms:
   220	            BertLayer(
   221	                hidden=cfg["hidden_size"],
   222	                n_heads=cfg["n_heads"],
   223	                intermediate=cfg["intermediate_size"],
   224	            )
   225	            for _ in range(cfg["n_layers"])
   226	        ]
   227	        self.projection = nn.Linear(
   228	            cfg["projection_in"], cfg["projection_out"], bias=cfg["projection_has_bias"]
   229	        )
   230	
   231	    def __call__(
   232	        self,
   233	        input_ids: mx.array,
   234	        attention_mask: mx.array,
   235	        token_type_ids: mx.array,
   236	    ) -> mx.array:
   237	        # attention bias: 0 where mask=1, -inf where mask=0 (so softmax→0).
   238	        # shape: [B, 1, 1, T] to broadcast over (n_heads, query_seq).
   239	        bias = (1.0 - attention_mask.astype(input_ids.dtype)).astype(mx.float32) * -1e4
   240	        bias = bias.reshape(bias.shape[0], 1, 1, bias.shape[1])
   241	
   242	        x = self.embeddings(input_ids, token_type_ids)
   243	        # Cast bias to x's dtype so attention math stays in compute precision.
   244	        bias = bias.astype(x.dtype)
   245	
   246	        for layer in self.layers:
   247	            x = layer(x, bias)
   248	
   249	        # 768 -> 128 projection
   250	        x = self.projection(x)
   251	        # L2-normalize per token along last axis
   252	        norm = mx.sqrt(mx.sum(x * x, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)
   253	        return x / norm
   254	
   255	
   256	def _load_weights_into_model(model: ColBertMLX, weights: dict) -> None:
   257	    """Bind the flat safetensors dict produced by export_colbert_to_mlx.py
   258	    into the MLX module tree."""
   259	    # Build params tree matching mlx.nn's expected layout.
   260	    params = {
   261	        "embeddings": {
   262	            "word": {"weight": weights["embeddings.word.weight"]},
   263	            "position": {"weight": weights["embeddings.position.weight"]},
   264	            "token_type": {"weight": weights["embeddings.token_type.weight"]},
   265	            "ln": {
   266	                "weight": weights["embeddings.ln.weight"],
   267	                "bias": weights["embeddings.ln.bias"],
   268	            },
   269	        },
   270	        "layers": [],
   271	        "projection": {"weight": weights["projection.weight"]},
   272	    }
   273	    if "projection.bias" in weights:
   274	        params["projection"]["bias"] = weights["projection.bias"]
   275	
   276	    n_layers = model.cfg["n_layers"]
   277	    for i in range(n_layers):
   278	        params["layers"].append(
   279	            {
   280	                "attn": {
   281	                    "q": {
   282	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.q.weight"],
   283	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.q.bias"],
   284	                    },
   285	                    "k": {
   286	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.k.weight"],
   287	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.k.bias"],
   288	                    },
   289	                    "v": {
   290	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.v.weight"],
   291	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.v.bias"],
   292	                    },
   293	                    "out": {
   294	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.out.weight"],
   295	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.out.bias"],
   296	                    },
   297	                    "ln": {
   298	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.ln.weight"],
   299	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.attn.ln.bias"],
   300	                    },
   301	                },
   302	                "ff": {
   303	                    "up": {
   304	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.ff.up.weight"],
   305	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.ff.up.bias"],
   306	                    },
   307	                    "down": {
   308	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.ff.down.weight"],
   309	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.ff.down.bias"],
   310	                    },
   311	                    "ln": {
   312	                        "weight": weights[f"layers.{i}.ff.ln.weight"],
   313	                        "bias": weights[f"layers.{i}.ff.ln.bias"],
   314	                    },
   315	                },
   316	            }
   317	        )
   318	
   319	    model.update(params)
   320	
   321	
   322	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   323	# Pipeline
   324	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   325	
   326	
   327	def read_docs_jsonl(path: Path, max_docs: int | None) -> list[dict]:
   328	    out: list[dict] = []
   329	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   330	        for lineno, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
   331	            line = line.strip()
   332	            if not line:
   333	                continue
   334	            try:
   335	                obj = json.loads(line)
   336	            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
   337	                raise SystemExit(f"{path}:{lineno}: malformed JSON: {e}") from e
   338	            if "doc_id" not in obj or "text" not in obj:
   339	                raise SystemExit(
   340	                    f"{path}:{lineno}: each line needs 'doc_id' and 'text' fields"
   341	                )
   342	            out.append(obj)
   343	            if max_docs is not None and len(out) >= max_docs:
   344	                break
   345	    return out
   346	
   347	
   348	def encode_docs_mlx(
   349	    docs: list[dict],
   350	    *,
   351	    model_name: str,
   352	    weights_path: Path,
   353	    dtype: str,
   354	    batch_size: int,
   355	    trust_remote_code: bool,
   356	    sort_by_length: bool = False,
   357	) -> tuple[list[EncodedDoc], list[tuple[str, str]], int]:
   358	    """Encode docs through MLX. Returns (encoded, dropped, dim).
   359	
   360	    Tokenizer + skiplist come from pylate (CPU, deterministic, fast).
   361	    Forward pass + L2-norm run in MLX. Output rows match the keep-mask
   362	    (skiplist & attention_mask), exactly as encode.py does.
   363	
   364	    When sort_by_length=True, docs are reordered by tokenized length before
   365	    batching so each padded batch's max-T shrinks. Output order is restored
   366	    to input order before return so doc_id_map / qid_map remain unchanged.
   367	    """
   368	    # Tokenizer comes from pylate. We don't run model.forward through pylate
   369	    # — only model.tokenize and model.skiplist_mask.
   370	    from pylate import models  # type: ignore
   371	    import numpy as np
   372	
   373	    pl = models.ColBERT(
   374	        model_name_or_path=model_name,
   375	        trust_remote_code=trust_remote_code,
   376	        device="cpu",
   377	    )
   378	    pl.eval()
   379	    skiplist = pl.skiplist  # list[int]
   380	
   381	    # Read MLX safetensors + config.
   382	    if not weights_path.exists():
   383	        raise SystemExit(
   384	            f"weights file {weights_path} missing — run tools/export_colbert_to_mlx.py first"
   385	        )
   386	    config_path = weights_path.parent / "config.json"
   387	    if not config_path.exists():
   388	        raise SystemExit(f"config.json missing next to {weights_path}")
   389	    cfg = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   390	
   391	    compute_dtype = mx.float16 if dtype == "fp16" else mx.float32
   392	    # Prefer a dtype-specific export sibling so we don't pay an astype on
   393	    # every weight at startup. Falls back to the legacy single-file export
   394	    # with a per-tensor astype if the sibling isn't there.
   395	    dtype_specific = weights_path.parent / f"mlx_weights.{dtype}.safetensors"
   396	    if dtype_specific.exists():
   397	        weights = mx.load(str(dtype_specific))
   398	        if cfg.get("weight_dtype") != dtype:
   399	            # Sibling exists but config disagrees with its dtype (mixed
   400	            # export). Be safe and re-cast.
   401	            weights = {k: v.astype(compute_dtype) for k, v in weights.items()}
   402	    else:
   403	        weights = mx.load(str(weights_path))
   404	        # Skip the per-tensor astype if the file already matches runtime dtype.
   405	        if cfg.get("weight_dtype") != dtype:
   406	            weights = {k: v.astype(compute_dtype) for k, v in weights.items()}
   407	
   408	    model = ColBertMLX(cfg)
   409	    _load_weights_into_model(model, weights)
   410	    model.eval()
   411	
   412	    # Vocab-size bool LUT: skip_lut[v] == True iff token id v is in skiplist.
   413	    # Lets us compute the per-batch keep mask via fancy indexing rather than
   414	    # a Python "int(id) in skip_set" loop.
   415	    vocab_size = int(cfg["vocab_size"])
   416	    skip_lut = np.zeros(vocab_size, dtype=bool)
   417	    for s in skiplist:
   418	        si = int(s)
   419	        if 0 <= si < vocab_size:
   420	            skip_lut[si] = True
   421	
   422	    dim = cfg["projection_out"]
   423	
   424	    # Build the iteration order. Default: input order (range). When
   425	    # sort_by_length is on, tokenize the full corpus once to learn per-doc
   426	    # lengths, then sort indices ascending by length (np.argsort is stable
   427	    # via kind='stable'). Output order is restored at the end via slot fill.
   428	    n = len(docs)
   429	    if sort_by_length and n > 1:
   430	        all_features = pl.tokenize([d["text"] for d in docs], is_query=False)
   431	        # attention_mask sum per row gives unpadded token count.
   432	        lengths = all_features["attention_mask"].sum(dim=1).cpu().numpy()
   433	        order = np.argsort(lengths, kind="stable").tolist()
   434	    else:
   435	        order = list(range(n))
   436	
   437	    # Slot-keyed output so we can restore input order regardless of the
   438	    # iteration order. None = not yet encoded; later replaced with EncodedDoc
   439	    # or the doc was dropped (recorded separately, position left None).
   440	    slots: list[EncodedDoc | None] = [None] * n
   441	    dropped: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   442	
   443	    for batch_start in range(0, n, batch_size):
   444	        batch_indices = order[batch_start : batch_start + batch_size]
   445	        batch = [docs[i] for i in batch_indices]
   446	        texts = [d["text"] for d in batch]
   447	        try:
   448	            features = pl.tokenize(texts, is_query=False)
   449	            input_ids_pt = features["input_ids"]
   450	            attn_pt = features["attention_mask"]
   451	            type_ids_pt = features.get("token_type_ids")
   452	            if type_ids_pt is None:
   453	                # If absent (some tokenizers), default to zeros.
   454	                import torch  # type: ignore
   455	
   456	                type_ids_pt = torch.zeros_like(input_ids_pt)
   457	
   458	            input_ids = mx.array(input_ids_pt.cpu().numpy().astype("int32"))
   459	            attn = mx.array(attn_pt.cpu().numpy().astype("int32"))
   460	            type_ids = mx.array(type_ids_pt.cpu().numpy().astype("int32"))

exec

   463	            # Force evaluation so we have concrete arrays for slicing.
   464	            mx.eval(tok_emb)
   465	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   466	            for d in batch:
   467	                dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), f"batch encode failed: {e!r}"))
   468	            continue
   469

   474	        # mlx.array → host: numpy view (zero-copy when supported by MLX).
   475	        emb_arr = np.array(emb_np, copy=False)
   476

   478	        # vocab-LUT so the "in skiplist?" check is a single fancy index.
   479	        keep_mask = (attn_np == 1) & (~skip_lut[ids_np])
   480	
   481	        for b, (orig_i, d) in enumerate(zip(batch_indices, batch)):
   482	            try:
   483	                row_keep = keep_mask[b]
   484	                if not row_keep.any():
   485	                    dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), "0-token output after masking"))
   486	                    continue
   487	                kept_ids = ids_np[b][row_keep].astype(int).tolist()
   488	                # f32-contiguous numpy [n_kept, dim]; the writer's bulk path
   489	                # consumes this without re-packing per float.
   490	                kept_vecs = np.ascontiguousarray(emb_arr[b][row_keep], dtype=np.float32)
   491	                slots[orig_i] = EncodedDoc(
   492	                    doc_id=str(d["doc_id"]),
   493	                    token_ids=kept_ids,
   494	                    vectors=kept_vecs,
   495	                )
   496	            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   497	                dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), f"post-process failed: {e!r}"))
   498	
   499	    encoded = [s for s in slots if s is not None]
   500	    if not encoded:
   501	        raise SystemExit("no docs encoded successfully — aborting before writing")
   502	    return encoded, dropped, dim
   503

   505	def main() -> None:
   506	    args = parse_args()
   507	
   508	    if args.weights is None:
   509	        from export_colbert_to_mlx import _safe_name  # type: ignore
   510	
   511	        args.weights = (
   512	            Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".cache" / _safe_name(args.model) / "mlx_weights.safetensors"
   513	        )
   514	
   515	    docs = read_docs_jsonl(args.docs, args.max_docs)
   516	    if not docs:
   517	        raise SystemExit(f"{args.docs}: no records found")
   518	    print(f"loaded {len(docs)} docs from {args.docs}", file=sys.stderr)
   519	
   520	    if args.encoder_batch_size is not None:
   521	        batch_size = args.encoder_batch_size
   522	    elif args.batch is not None:
   523	        batch_size = args.batch
   524	    else:
   525	        # Defaults from plan-13 rec-04: 128 fp16, 64 fp32. Larger batches
   526	        # amortize fixed kernel/dispatch cost on Apple Silicon.
   527	        batch_size = 128 if args.dtype == "fp16" else 64
   528	
   529	    encoded, dropped, dim = encode_docs_mlx(
   530	        docs,
   531	        model_name=args.model,
   532	        weights_path=args.weights,
   533	        dtype=args.dtype,
   534	        batch_size=batch_size,
   535	        trust_remote_code=args.trust_remote_code,
   536	        sort_by_length=args.sort_by_length,
   537	    )
   538	    print(
   539	        f"encoded {len(encoded)} docs (dim={dim}, dropped={len(dropped)})",
   540	        file=sys.stderr,
   541	    )
   542	
   543	    stats = write_tokens_bin(args.out, encoded, dim)
   544	    print(
   545	        f"wrote {args.out} "
   546	        f"(n_docs={stats['n_docs']}, n_tokens={stats['n_tokens']}, dim={stats['dim']})",
   547	        file=sys.stderr,
   548	    )
   549	
   550	    meta_path = args.meta_out or args.out.with_suffix(args.out.suffix + ".meta.json")
   551	    _write_metadata_shared(
   552	        meta_path,
   553	        mode="docs",
   554	        model_name=args.model,
   555	        dim=dim,
   556	        n_docs=stats["n_docs"],
   557	        n_tokens=stats["n_tokens"],
   558	        doc_ids=[d.doc_id for d in encoded],
   559	        dropped=dropped,
   560	        docs_jsonl=args.docs,
   561	        tool="tools/encode_mlx.py",
   562	        tool_version="0.1",
   563	    )
   564	    print(f"wrote metadata {meta_path}", file=sys.stderr)
   565	
   566	
   567	if __name__ == "__main__":
   568	    main()

414:    # a Python "int(id) in skip_set" loop.

417:    for s in skiplist:

431:        # attention_mask sum per row gives unpadded token count.
432:        lengths = all_features["attention_mask"].sum(dim=1).cpu().numpy()

464:            mx.eval(tok_emb)

477:        # Vectorized keep mask: attn==1 AND not in skiplist. Uses the
478:        # vocab-LUT so the "in skiplist?" check is a single fancy index.
479:        keep_mask = (attn_np == 1) & (~skip_lut[ids_np])

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""tools/export_colbert_to_mlx.py — convert pylate ColBERT weights to MLX.
     3	
     4	Owner: post-hackathon plan-12 (mlx-encoder-engineer).
     5	
     6	Loads `colbert-ir/colbertv2.0` (or `jinaai/jina-colbert-v2-64`) via pylate,
     7	walks the underlying BERT base + the final pylate `Dense` linear projection,
     8	and saves an MLX-safetensors file to
     9	`tools/.cache/<model>/mlx_weights.safetensors`.
    10	
    11	The MLX encoder (tools/encode_mlx.py) hand-rolls the BERT forward pass via
    12	`mlx.nn.Linear` + `mlx.nn.LayerNorm` etc. (mlx_lm has no encoder-only BERT
    13	model class today). We rename the keys here so the MLX side can `mx.load`
    14	the file directly into a flat dict and bind weights by name.
    15	
    16	Key rename map (pylate/sbert → MLX-encoder):
    17	  0.auto_model.embeddings.word_embeddings.weight    → embeddings.word.weight
    18	  0.auto_model.embeddings.position_embeddings.weight → embeddings.position.weight
    19	  0.auto_model.embeddings.token_type_embeddings.weight → embeddings.token_type.weight
    20	  0.auto_model.embeddings.LayerNorm.{weight,bias}   → embeddings.ln.{weight,bias}
    21	  0.auto_model.encoder.layer.<i>.attention.self.{q,k,v}.{weight,bias}
    22	                                                    → layers.<i>.attn.{q,k,v}.{weight,bias}
    23	  0.auto_model.encoder.layer.<i>.attention.output.dense.{weight,bias}
    24	                                                    → layers.<i>.attn.out.{weight,bias}
    25	  0.auto_model.encoder.layer.<i>.attention.output.LayerNorm.{weight,bias}
    26	                                                    → layers.<i>.attn.ln.{weight,bias}
    27	  0.auto_model.encoder.layer.<i>.intermediate.dense.{weight,bias}
    28	                                                    → layers.<i>.ff.up.{weight,bias}
    29	  0.auto_model.encoder.layer.<i>.output.dense.{weight,bias}
    30	                                                    → layers.<i>.ff.down.{weight,bias}
    31	  0.auto_model.encoder.layer.<i>.output.LayerNorm.{weight,bias}
    32	                                                    → layers.<i>.ff.ln.{weight,bias}
    33	  1.linear.weight                                   → projection.weight
    34	
    35	The pylate.Dense projection has no bias on ColBERTv2 (`bias=False`), so
    36	no projection.bias key is emitted. We assert this on export and store it
    37	in the sidecar config so the MLX encoder builds a no-bias `nn.Linear`.
    38	
    39	Pooler weights (0.auto_model.pooler.*) are dropped — ColBERT token-level
    40	outputs never go through the pooler. We log them as "intentionally
    41	discarded" rather than failing.
    42	
    43	Output:
    44	  tools/.cache/<model_safe>/mlx_weights.safetensors  -- flat key -> mx.array
    45	  tools/.cache/<model_safe>/config.json              -- arch hyperparams
    46	"""
    47	from __future__ import annotations
    48	
    49	import argparse
    50	import json
    51	import re
    52	import sys
    53	from pathlib import Path
    54	
    55	
    56	def _safe_name(model_name: str) -> str:
    57	    """Filesystem-safe slug for cache subdirs (e.g. 'colbert-ir/colbertv2.0'
    58	    → 'colbert-ir__colbertv2.0')."""
    59	    return re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+", "__", model_name)
    60

    90	            "fp16 halves the file. The MLX encoder up-casts to the dtype "
    91	            "selected at runtime, so this only affects on-disk size."
    92	        ),
    93	    )
    94	    return p.parse_args()

    96	
    97	def remap_key(k: str) -> str | None:
    98	    """Map a pylate state_dict key to its MLX-encoder name. Returns None if
    99	    the key is intentionally discarded (e.g. BERT pooler).
   100	
   101	    The mapping is exact: any unknown key triggers a SystemExit so we never
   102	    silently drop something the MLX side relies on.
   103	    """
   104	    # pooler: present but unused for token-level ColBERT.
   105	    if k.startswith("0.auto_model.pooler."):
   106	        return None
   107	
   108	    # embeddings
   109	    m = re.fullmatch(r"0\.auto_model\.embeddings\.(\w+)_embeddings\.weight", k)
   110	    if m:
   111	        return f"embeddings.{m.group(1)}.weight"
   112	    m = re.fullmatch(r"0\.auto_model\.embeddings\.LayerNorm\.(weight|bias)", k)
   113	    if m:
   114	        return f"embeddings.ln.{m.group(1)}"
   115	
   116	    # encoder layers
   117	    m = re.fullmatch(
   118	        r"0\.auto_model\.encoder\.layer\.(\d+)\.attention\.self\.(query|key|value)\.(weight|bias)",
   119	        k,
   120	    )
   121	    if m:
   122	        idx, qkv, wb = m.groups()
   123	        short = {"query": "q", "key": "k", "value": "v"}[qkv]
   124	        return f"layers.{idx}.attn.{short}.{wb}"
   125	
   126	    m = re.fullmatch(
   127	        r"0\.auto_model\.encoder\.layer\.(\d+)\.attention\.output\.dense\.(weight|bias)",
   128	        k,
   129	    )
   130	    if m:
   131	        return f"layers.{m.group(1)}.attn.out.{m.group(2)}"
   132	
   133	    m = re.fullmatch(
   134	        r"0\.auto_model\.encoder\.layer\.(\d+)\.attention\.output\.LayerNorm\.(weight|bias)",
   135	        k,
   136	    )
   137	    if m:
   138	        return f"layers.{m.group(1)}.attn.ln.{m.group(2)}"
   139	
   140	    m = re.fullmatch(
   141	        r"0\.auto_model\.encoder\.layer\.(\d+)\.intermediate\.dense\.(weight|bias)",
   142	        k,
   143	    )
   144	    if m:
   145	        return f"layers.{m.group(1)}.ff.up.{m.group(2)}"
   146	
   147	    m = re.fullmatch(
   148	        r"0\.auto_model\.encoder\.layer\.(\d+)\.output\.dense\.(weight|bias)",
   149	        k,
   150	    )
   151	    if m:
   152	        return f"layers.{m.group(1)}.ff.down.{m.group(2)}"
   153	
   154	    m = re.fullmatch(
   155	        r"0\.auto_model\.encoder\.layer\.(\d+)\.output\.LayerNorm\.(weight|bias)",
   156	        k,
   157	    )
   158	    if m:
   159	        return f"layers.{m.group(1)}.ff.ln.{m.group(2)}"
   160	
   161	    # final pylate Dense projection (768 -> 128)
   162	    if k == "1.linear.weight":
   163	        return "projection.weight"
   164	    if k == "1.linear.bias":
   165	        return "projection.bias"
   166	
   167	    raise SystemExit(f"unknown ColBERT state_dict key: {k!r} — refusing to silently drop")
   168	
   169	
   170	def main() -> None:
   171	    args = parse_args()
   172	
   173	    import mlx.core as mx  # noqa: E402
   174	    import torch  # noqa: E402
   175	    from pylate import models  # noqa: E402
   176	
   177	    print(f"loading {args.model} via pylate (CPU)…", file=sys.stderr)
   178	    model = models.ColBERT(
   179	        model_name_or_path=args.model,
   180	        trust_remote_code=args.trust_remote_code,
   181	        device="cpu",
   182	    )
   183	    model.eval()

   186	    # Transformer module. Layout: model[0].auto_model is the BERT model.
   187	    bert = model[0].auto_model
   188	    cfg = bert.config
   189	    n_layers = cfg.num_hidden_layers
   190	    hidden = cfg.hidden_size
   191	    n_heads = cfg.num_attention_heads
   192	    intermediate = cfg.intermediate_size
   193	    vocab_size = cfg.vocab_size
   194	    max_pos = cfg.max_position_embeddings
   195	    type_vocab = cfg.type_vocab_size
   196	    ln_eps = cfg.layer_norm_eps
   197	    hidden_act = cfg.hidden_act
   198	    if hidden_act != "gelu":
   199	        # BERT base uses gelu; flag any drift so the MLX encoder doesn't
   200	        # silently substitute the wrong activation.
   201	        raise SystemExit(
   202	            f"unexpected hidden activation {hidden_act!r}; MLX encoder is "
   203	            "hard-coded for gelu."
   204	        )
   205	
   206	    # pylate.Dense projection. ColBERTv2 uses bias=False; we assert and record.
   207	    projection = model[1].linear
   208	    proj_in, proj_out = projection.in_features, projection.out_features
   209	    proj_has_bias = projection.bias is not None

   211	        # ColBERTv2 expects bias=False; if a future pylate bumps this we want to know.
   212	        print(
   213	            f"WARN: pylate Dense projection has bias=True (unusual for ColBERTv2)",
   214	            file=sys.stderr,
   215	        )
   216	
   217	    # Walk + remap state_dict.
   218	    sd = model.state_dict()
   219	    out_weights: dict[str, mx.array] = {}
   220	    discarded: list[str] = []

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""tests/live/mlx_parity.py — 4-gate parity contract for tools/encode_mlx.py.
     3	
     4	Run:
     5	    tools/.venv/bin/python tests/live/mlx_parity.py --mlx-parity
     6	
     7	Gates (each must pass; none skippable):
     8	  1. Token-id byte equality between encode.py and encode_mlx.py.
     9	  2. Per-token cosine ≥ 0.998 mean and ≥ 0.99 min on the live fixture.
    10	  3. Two-run MLX determinism — byte-equal, or ≤ small ULP drift per float.
    11	     (Note: the PyTorch encode.py baseline is itself non-deterministic
    12	     across runs by ~1.5e-6 abs, owing to transformers 4.57's
    13	     mean_resizing path firing during pylate's vocab expansion. This
    14	     gate measures MLX's own self-consistency, not parity vs that drift.)
    15	  4. Downstream TAC clustering: build a tac index from each token_dump
    16	     with identical params/seed; assert headers (kappa_total, dim, n_*),
    17	     and centroid arrays agree within 1e-3 cosine per centroid.
    18	
    19	The script does NOT auto-install MLX, the weight cache, or the venv —
    20	it asserts they exist and gives a clean error if not.
    21	
    22	Args:
    23	  --mlx-parity   required gate flag (mirrors -Dlive in zig)
    24	  --max-docs     subset for fast smoke runs (default: all 100 fixture docs)
    25	  --dtype        fp32 or fp16 (default fp16 — matches encode.py MPS default)
    26	"""
    27	from __future__ import annotations
    28	
    29	import argparse
    30	import json
    31	import math
    32	import os
    33	import struct
    34	import subprocess
    35	import sys
    36	import time
    37	from pathlib import Path
    38	
    39	REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    40	TOOLS = REPO / "tools"
    41	VENV_PY = TOOLS / ".venv" / "bin" / "python"
    42	FIXTURE = REPO / "tests" / "fixtures" / "live" / "docs.jsonl"
    43	WEIGHTS_DIR = TOOLS / ".cache" / "colbert-ir__colbertv2.0"
    44	MLX_WEIGHTS = WEIGHTS_DIR / "mlx_weights.safetensors"
    45	
    46	
    47	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48	# tokens.bin parser (mirrors src/io/token_dump.zig::parseBytes structurally;
    49	# kept dependency-free so this script doesn't pull torch / mlx in itself).
    50	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51	
    52	
    53	def parse_tokens_bin(path: Path) -> dict:
    54	    with path.open("rb") as f:
    55	        data = f.read()
    56	    if data[:8] != b"TAC_TKN1":
    57	        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: bad magic {data[:8]!r}")
    58	    version = struct.unpack("<I", data[8:12])[0]
    59	    if version != 2:
    60	        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: bad version {version}")
    61	    dim = struct.unpack("<I", data[12:16])[0]
    62	    n_docs = struct.unpack("<Q", data[16:24])[0]
    63	    n_tok = struct.unpack("<Q", data[24:32])[0]
    64	    if data[32] != 0:
    65	        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: dtype != f32")
    66	
    67	    pos = 40
    68	    offsets = list(struct.unpack(f"<{n_docs+1}Q", data[pos : pos + (n_docs + 1) * 8]))
    69	    pos += (n_docs + 1) * 8
    70	    ids = list(struct.unpack(f"<{n_tok}I", data[pos : pos + n_tok * 4]))
    71	    pos += n_tok * 4
    72	    vec_bytes = n_tok * dim * 4
    73	    vecs = struct.unpack(f"<{n_tok*dim}f", data[pos : pos + vec_bytes])
    74	    return {
    75	        "dim": dim,
    76	        "n_docs": n_docs,
    77	        "n_tok": n_tok,
    78	        "offsets": offsets,
    79	        "ids": ids,
    80	        "vecs": vecs,
    81	    }
    82	
    83	
    84	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    85	# tac index (.tac) header + centroids parser.
    86	#
    87	# Layout (src/index/storage.zig):
    88	#   header (112 bytes inside a 128-byte region)
    89	#     [8] magic "TAC_IDX1"
    90	#     u32 version
    91	#     u32 dim
    92	#     u32 kappa
    93	#     u32 pq_M
    94	#     u8  pq_bits
    95	#     u8[3] _pad
    96	#     u32 sub_dim
    97	#     u64 n_docs
    98	#     u64 n_tokens
    99	#     u64 centroids_off
   100	#     u64 hnsw_off, pq_off, ilist_off, doc_off, doc_tok_off, norms_off, footer_off
   101	#   <padding to 128>
   102	#   centroids: kappa * dim f32 row-major
   103	#   ...
   104	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105	
   106	
   107	def parse_tac_header_centroids(path: Path) -> dict:
   108	    with path.open("rb") as f:
   109	        data = f.read()
   110	    if data[:8] != b"TAC_IDX1":
   111	        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: bad TAC index magic {data[:8]!r}")
   112	    pos = 8
   113	    version = struct.unpack("<I", data[pos : pos + 4])[0]; pos += 4
   114	    dim = struct.unpack("<I", data[pos : pos + 4])[0]; pos += 4
   115	    kappa = struct.unpack("<I", data[pos : pos + 4])[0]; pos += 4
   116	    pq_M = struct.unpack("<I", data[pos : pos + 4])[0]; pos += 4
   117	    pq_bits = data[pos]; pos += 1
   118	    pos += 3  # _pad
   119	    sub_dim = struct.unpack("<I", data[pos : pos + 4])[0]; pos += 4
   120	    n_docs = struct.unpack("<Q", data[pos : pos + 8])[0]; pos += 8
   121	    n_tokens = struct.unpack("<Q", data[pos : pos + 8])[0]; pos += 8
   122	    centroids_off = struct.unpack("<Q", data[pos : pos + 8])[0]; pos += 8
   123	
   124	    cent_bytes = kappa * dim * 4
   125	    centroids = struct.unpack(
   126	        f"<{kappa*dim}f", data[centroids_off : centroids_off + cent_bytes]
   127	    )
   128	    return {
   129	        "version": version,
   130	        "dim": dim,
   131	        "kappa": kappa,
   132	        "pq_M": pq_M,
   133	        "pq_bits": pq_bits,
   134	        "sub_dim": sub_dim,
   135	        "n_docs": n_docs,
   136	        "n_tokens": n_tokens,
   137	        "centroids": centroids,
   138	    }
   139	
   140	
   141	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142	# Math helpers
   143	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144	
   145	
   146	def cosine(a: tuple[float, ...], b: tuple[float, ...]) -> float:
   147	    if len(a) != len(b):
   148	        raise SystemExit(f"length mismatch {len(a)} vs {len(b)}")
   149	    na = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in a))
   150	    nb = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in b))
   151	    if na == 0.0 or nb == 0.0:
   152	        return 0.0
   153	    return sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b)) / (na * nb)
   154	
   155	
   156	def cosines_per_token(pt: dict, mlx: dict) -> list[float]:
   157	    if pt["n_tok"] != mlx["n_tok"] or pt["dim"] != mlx["dim"]:
   158	        raise SystemExit(
   159	            f"shape mismatch: pt n_tok={pt['n_tok']} dim={pt['dim']} vs "
   160	            f"mlx n_tok={mlx['n_tok']} dim={mlx['dim']}"
   161	        )
   162	    dim = pt["dim"]
   163	    out = []
   164	    for i in range(pt["n_tok"]):
   165	        a = pt["vecs"][i * dim : (i + 1) * dim]
   166	        b = mlx["vecs"][i * dim : (i + 1) * dim]
   167	        out.append(cosine(a, b))
   168	    return out
   169

   176	def run(cmd: list[str], *, cwd: Path | None = None) -> tuple[float, str]:
   177	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   178	    res = subprocess.run(
   179	        cmd, cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None, capture_output=True, text=True
   180	    )
   181	    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
   182	    if res.returncode != 0:
   183	        raise SystemExit(
   184	            f"command failed (exit {res.returncode}):\n  {' '.join(cmd)}\n"
   185	            f"stderr:\n{res.stderr}\nstdout:\n{res.stdout}"
   186	        )
   187	    return elapsed, res.stderr
   188	
   189	
   190	def encode_pt(out: Path, max_docs: int | None, dtype: str) -> float:
   191	    args = [
   192	        str(VENV_PY),
   193	        str(TOOLS / "encode.py"),
   194	        "--docs", str(FIXTURE),
   195	        "--out", str(out),
   196	        "--device", "cpu",
   197	        "--dtype", dtype,
   198	        "--batch", "16",
   199	    ]
   200	    if max_docs is not None:
   201	        args += ["--max-docs", str(max_docs)]
   202	    elapsed, _ = run(args, cwd=REPO)
   203	    return elapsed

   223	    tac_bin = REPO / "zig-out" / "bin" / "tac"
   224	    if not tac_bin.exists():

   229	    run([
   230	        str(tac_bin), "index",
   231	        str(tokens_bin), str(out_tac),
   232	        "--kappa", str(kappa), "--seed", str(seed),
   233	    ], cwd=REPO)

 succeeded in 0ms:
   240	
   241	def gate1_token_ids(pt: dict, mlx: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   242	    if pt["ids"] != mlx["ids"]:
   243	        diffs = sum(1 for a, b in zip(pt["ids"], mlx["ids"]) if a != b)
   244	        return False, f"token_ids differ: {diffs}/{len(pt['ids'])} positions"
   245	    if pt["offsets"] != mlx["offsets"]:
   246	        return False, "doc_offsets (CSR) differ"
   247	    return True, f"token_ids byte-equal ({len(pt['ids'])} ids; offsets equal)"
   248	
   249	
   250	def gate2_cosine(pt: dict, mlx: dict, mean_floor: float, min_floor: float) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   251	    cos = cosines_per_token(pt, mlx)
   252	    cos_sorted = sorted(cos)
   253	    n = len(cos)
   254	    mean = sum(cos) / n
   255	    cmin = cos_sorted[0]
   256	    p1 = cos_sorted[max(0, n // 100)]
   257	    msg = (
   258	        f"cosine mean={mean:.6f} min={cmin:.6f} p1={p1:.6f} "
   259	        f"max={cos_sorted[-1]:.6f} (n={n})"
   260	    )
   261	    if mean < mean_floor:
   262	        return False, msg + f" — mean below floor {mean_floor}"
   263	    if cmin < min_floor:
   264	        return False, msg + f" — min below floor {min_floor}"
   265	    return True, msg
   266	
   267	
   268	def gate3_determinism(out_a: Path, out_b: Path, max_docs: int | None, dtype: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   269	    encode_mlx(out_a, max_docs, dtype)
   270	    encode_mlx(out_b, max_docs, dtype)
   271	    a = parse_tokens_bin(out_a)
   272	    b = parse_tokens_bin(out_b)
   273	    if a["ids"] != b["ids"] or a["offsets"] != b["offsets"]:
   274	        return False, "two-run determinism: token_ids/offsets differ"
   275	
   276	    # Compare floats with strict byte-equality first; if that fails, fall
   277	    # back to ULP-class threshold (≤ 4 ULPs ≈ ~5e-7 abs for fp32 values
   278	    # near 0.1, OK for L2-normalised ColBERT vectors).
   279	    diffs = 0
   280	    max_abs = 0.0
   281	    for x, y in zip(a["vecs"], b["vecs"]):
   282	        if x != y:
   283	            diffs += 1
   284	            d = abs(x - y)
   285	            if d > max_abs:
   286	                max_abs = d
   287	    n = len(a["vecs"])
   288	    if diffs == 0:
   289	        return True, f"two-run byte-identical ({n} floats)"
   290	    # ULP-class threshold for fp32 vectors with |x| ≤ 1 (L2-normalised, dim=128):
   291	    # 1 ULP ≈ 2**-23 ≈ 1.19e-7. Allow ≤ 4 ULPs.
   292	    threshold = 5e-7
   293	    if max_abs <= threshold:
   294	        return True, (
   295	            f"two-run within ULP-class threshold "
   296	            f"({diffs}/{n} floats differ; max abs {max_abs:.2e} ≤ {threshold:.2e})"
   297	        )
   298	    return False, (
   299	        f"two-run determinism failed: {diffs}/{n} floats differ; "
   300	        f"max abs {max_abs:.2e} > {threshold:.2e}"
   301	    )
   302	
   303	
   304	def gate4_clustering(pt_bin: Path, mlx_bin: Path, kappa: int, seed: int) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   305	    pt_tac = pt_bin.with_suffix(".pt.tac")
   306	    mlx_tac = mlx_bin.with_suffix(".mlx.tac")
   307	    tac_index(pt_bin, pt_tac, kappa=kappa, seed=seed)
   308	    tac_index(mlx_bin, mlx_tac, kappa=kappa, seed=seed)
   309	
   310	    pt_idx = parse_tac_header_centroids(pt_tac)
   311	    mlx_idx = parse_tac_header_centroids(mlx_tac)
   312	
   313	    # Header agreement on kappa/dim/n_*.
   314	    for key in ("dim", "kappa", "n_docs", "n_tokens"):
   315	        if pt_idx[key] != mlx_idx[key]:
   316	            return False, f"tac header field {key!r} differs: pt={pt_idx[key]} mlx={mlx_idx[key]}"
   317	
   318	    # Per-centroid cosine similarity. Centroid c maps the same vocab id
   319	    # cluster on both sides (kappa_per_token is determined by token_id
   320	    # frequencies, which gate 1 already proved equal), so centroid i on
   321	    # both sides clusters the same token-id subset.
   322	    dim = pt_idx["dim"]
   323	    kappa_v = pt_idx["kappa"]
   324	    pt_c = pt_idx["centroids"]
   325	    mlx_c = mlx_idx["centroids"]
   326	    cos_per_centroid = []
   327	    for i in range(kappa_v):
   328	        a = pt_c[i * dim : (i + 1) * dim]
   329	        b = mlx_c[i * dim : (i + 1) * dim]
   330	        cos_per_centroid.append(cosine(a, b))
   331	
   332	    cmin = min(cos_per_centroid)
   333	    cmean = sum(cos_per_centroid) / len(cos_per_centroid)
   334	    msg = (
   335	        f"tac kappa={kappa_v} dim={dim} centroid cosine mean={cmean:.6f} min={cmin:.6f}"
   336	    )
   337	    # Plan threshold: centroid means agree within 1e-3 cosine (so ≥ 1 - 1e-3 = 0.999).
   338	    if cmin < 0.999:
   339	        return False, msg + f" — min < 0.999"
   340	    return True, msg
   341	
   342

   384	def main() -> None:
   385	    args = parse_args()
   386	
   387	    # Sanity: required files exist.
   388	    for p in (FIXTURE, MLX_WEIGHTS, VENV_PY):
   389	        if not p.exists():
   390	            raise SystemExit(f"required path missing: {p}")
   391	
   392	    print(f"[parity] fixture: {FIXTURE} (max_docs={args.max_docs}) dtype={args.dtype}", flush=True)
   393	
   394	    pt_bin = args.out_dir / "parity_pt.bin"
   395	    mlx_bin = args.out_dir / "parity_mlx.bin"
   396	    mlx_a = args.out_dir / "parity_mlx_a.bin"
   397	    mlx_b = args.out_dir / "parity_mlx_b.bin"
   398	
   399	    print("[parity] encoding via encode.py (pylate, cpu)…", flush=True)
   400	    t_pt = encode_pt(pt_bin, args.max_docs, args.dtype if args.dtype == "fp32" else "fp32")
   401	    # Note: encode.py on cpu doesn't accept fp16 the same way; we pass fp32 there.
   402	    # MLX side runs at the requested dtype.
   403	    print(f"[parity] encode.py: {t_pt:.2f}s", flush=True)
   404	
   405	    print(f"[parity] encoding via encode_mlx.py ({args.dtype})…", flush=True)
   406	    t_mlx = encode_mlx(mlx_bin, args.max_docs, args.dtype)
   407	    print(f"[parity] encode_mlx.py: {t_mlx:.2f}s (vs {t_pt:.2f}s pt → {t_pt/max(t_mlx, 1e-9):.2f}x)", flush=True)
   408	
   409	    pt = parse_tokens_bin(pt_bin)
   410	    mlx = parse_tokens_bin(mlx_bin)
   411	
   412	    results: dict[str, tuple[bool, str]] = {}
   413	
   414	    print("[parity] gate 1: token-id byte equality")
   415	    results["gate1_token_ids"] = gate1_token_ids(pt, mlx)
   416	    print(f"  {'PASS' if results['gate1_token_ids'][0] else 'FAIL'}: {results['gate1_token_ids'][1]}")
   417	
   418	    # Gate 2 thresholds: when we compare fp32 PT vs fp{16,32} MLX the floors are 0.998 mean / 0.99 min.
   419	    print("[parity] gate 2: per-token cosine")
   420	    results["gate2_cosine"] = gate2_cosine(pt, mlx, mean_floor=0.998, min_floor=0.99)
   421	    print(f"  {'PASS' if results['gate2_cosine'][0] else 'FAIL'}: {results['gate2_cosine'][1]}")
   422	
   423	    print(f"[parity] gate 3: two-run MLX determinism ({args.dtype})")
   424	    results["gate3_determinism"] = gate3_determinism(mlx_a, mlx_b, args.max_docs, args.dtype)
   425	    print(f"  {'PASS' if results['gate3_determinism'][0] else 'FAIL'}: {results['gate3_determinism'][1]}")
   426	
   427	    if args.skip_gate4:
   428	        print("[parity] gate 4: skipped (--skip-gate4)")
   429	        results["gate4_clustering"] = (True, "skipped via --skip-gate4")
   430	    else:
   431	        print(f"[parity] gate 4: TAC clustering overlap (kappa={args.kappa}, seed={args.seed})")
   432	        try:
   433	            results["gate4_clustering"] = gate4_clustering(pt_bin, mlx_bin, args.kappa, args.seed)
   434	        except SystemExit as e:
   435	            results["gate4_clustering"] = (False, f"gate4 setup failed: {e}")
   436	        print(f"  {'PASS' if results['gate4_clustering'][0] else 'FAIL'}: {results['gate4_clustering'][1]}")
   437	
   438	    print()
   439	    print("[parity] summary:")
   440	    all_pass = True
   441	    for name, (ok, msg) in results.items():
   442	        status = "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"
   443	        print(f"  {name}: {status}  {msg}")
   444	        if not ok:
   445	            all_pass = False
   446	
   447	    if not all_pass:
   448	        sys.exit(2)
   449	    print("[parity] all gates PASS")

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""tools/encode.py — ColBERTv2 → token-dump binary (format v2, real vocab IDs).
     3	
     4	Owner: primitives-engineer.
     5	See plan 01-primitives-and-io.plan.md and docs/token-dump-format.md.
     6	
     7	Reads docs.jsonl (one JSON object per line with at least "doc_id" and "text"
     8	fields), encodes each document with a ColBERT-style model (default
     9	`colbert-ir/colbertv2.0` via pylate), L2-normalises the per-token
    10	embeddings, and writes the flat-binary format defined in
    11	docs/token-dump-format.md plus a sidecar `tokens.meta.json`.
    12	
    13	Format v2 (this version) emits **real BERT vocabulary IDs** in
    14	`token_ids[i]` so paper §3 TAC bucketing aggregates by surface vocabulary
    15	("the" across docs lands in one cluster). v1 stored positional ids per
    16	doc and degenerated TAC; the Zig parser rejects v1 files outright since
    17	the constant flipped to 2.
    18	
    19	Usage:
    20	    python tools/encode.py \\
    21	        --docs   path/to/docs.jsonl \\
    22	        --out    data/msmarco_v1/tokens.bin \\
    23	        --model  colbert-ir/colbertv2.0 \\
    24	        --device cpu \\
    25	        --batch  16
    26	
    27	The model defaults to `colbert-ir/colbertv2.0` to match the paper. A
    28	sibling project (ir-expo) found `jinaai/jina-colbert-v2-64` works
    29	identically through the same pylate path; pass `--model` (with
    30	`--trust-remote-code` for jina) to override.
    31	
    32	We bypass `pylate.models.ColBERT.encode` because that wrapper does not
    33	return per-token vocab IDs in lockstep with the token embeddings.
    34	Instead we drive `model.tokenize` → `model.forward` → `model.skiplist_mask`
    35	ourselves and apply the exact same keep-mask to both the input_ids and
    36	the token_embeddings. This mirrors the embedding-keep logic from
    37	`pylate/models/colbert.py::encode` (lines ~688–720) one-for-one so the
    38	emitted vectors are byte-identical to what `model.encode` would have
    39	produced.
    40	
    41	Determinism: pylate/transformers pin model weights by revision; the
    42	output bytes for a given (model, revision, doc list) are reproducible
    43	modulo CPU/GPU non-determinism in the encoder. We do not introduce extra
    44	randomness here.
    45	
    46	Best-effort robust to encoder failures: a doc that fails encoding is
    47	skipped with a warning printed to stderr, and the metadata file records
    48	both the surviving and dropped doc IDs.
    49	"""
    50	
    51	from __future__ import annotations
    52	
    53	import argparse
    54	import json
    55	import sys
    56	from pathlib import Path
    57

   148	        ),
   149	    )
   150	    p.add_argument(
   151	        "--max-docs",
   152	        type=int,
   153	        default=None,
   154	        help="optional cap on docs (smoke testing)",
   155	    )
   156	    p.add_argument(
   157	        "--trust-remote-code",
   158	        action="store_true",
   159	        help="pass through to pylate.models.ColBERT (needed for jina models)",
   160	    )
   161	    p.add_argument(
   162	        "--meta-out",
   163	        type=Path,
   164	        default=None,
   165	        help="sidecar metadata path; defaults to <out>.meta.json",
   166	    )
   167	    p.add_argument(
   168	        "--sort-by-length",
   169	        action="store_true",
   170	        help=(
   171	            "sort docs by tokenizer length before batching. Reduces padding "
   172	            "waste 20-30%% on heterogeneous corpora at the cost of one extra "
   173	            "tokenize pass. Encoded order is restored at write time so "
   174	            "doc_id_map / qid_map remain in input order."
   175	        ),
   176	    )
   177	    return p.parse_args()
   178	
   179	
   180	def read_docs_jsonl(path: Path, max_docs: int | None, mode: str) -> list[dict]:
   181	    """Load JSONL records. In `docs` mode each line is `{doc_id, text}`;
   182	    in `queries` mode each line is `{qid, text}`. The id key is normalised to
   183	    `doc_id` in the returned dicts so the rest of the pipeline (encode_docs,
   184	    write_metadata) doesn't need to branch."""
   185	    id_key = "doc_id" if mode == "docs" else "qid"
   186	    out: list[dict] = []
   187	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   188	        for lineno, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
   189	            line = line.strip()
   190	            if not line:
   191	                continue
   192	            try:
   193	                obj = json.loads(line)
   194	            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
   195	                raise SystemExit(f"{path}:{lineno}: malformed JSON: {e}") from e
   196	            if id_key not in obj or "text" not in obj:
   197	                raise SystemExit(
   198	                    f"{path}:{lineno}: each line needs '{id_key}' and 'text' fields"
   199	                )
   200	            # Normalise: downstream uses obj["doc_id"]; in queries mode that
   201	            # holds the qid value.
   202	            if mode == "queries":
   203	                obj = {"doc_id": obj[id_key], "text": obj["text"]}
   204	            out.append(obj)
   205	            if max_docs is not None and len(out) >= max_docs:
   206	                break
   207	    return out
   208	
   209	
   210	def encode_docs(
   211	    docs: list[dict],
   212	    *,
   213	    mode: str,
   214	    model_name: str,
   215	    device: str,
   216	    batch_size: int,
   217	    trust_remote_code: bool,
   218	    sort_by_length: bool = False,
   219	    use_fp16: bool = False,
   220	) -> tuple[list[EncodedDoc], list[tuple[str, str]], int]:
   221	    """Returns (encoded, dropped, dim).
   222	
   223	    `dropped` is a list of (doc_id, reason) for docs that failed to encode.
   224	
   225	    Per-doc shape contract (mirrors `pylate.models.ColBERT.encode` for
   226	    is_query=False, normalize=True):
   227	        features = model.tokenize(texts, is_query=False)
   228	            features["input_ids"]:      [B, T]   real BERT vocab IDs,
   229	                                                  with [D] prefix inserted
   230	            features["attention_mask"]: [B, T]
   231	        out = model.forward(features)
   232	            out["token_embeddings"]:    [B, T, dim]
   233	            out["attention_mask"]:      [B, T]
   234	        skip = model.skiplist_mask(features["input_ids"], model.skiplist)
   235	        keep = (skip & out["attention_mask"]).bool()
   236	        for b in range(B):
   237	            kept_ids[b] = features["input_ids"][b, keep[b]]
   238	            kept_emb[b] = out["token_embeddings"][b, keep[b], :]
   239	            kept_emb[b] = F.normalize(kept_emb[b], p=2, dim=1)
   240	
   241	    The keep-mask is applied identically to both input_ids and embeddings,
   242	    so token_ids[i] is the BERT vocab id of the same row as vectors[i].
   243	    """
   244	    import torch  # type: ignore
   245	    from pylate import models  # type: ignore
   246	
   247	    model = models.ColBERT(
   248	        model_name_or_path=model_name,
   249	        trust_remote_code=trust_remote_code,
   250	        device=device,
   251	    )
   252	    model.eval()
   253	
   254	    # FP16 weights for the M-series MPS speedup (4-6× on Apple Silicon).
   255	    # Output tokens.bin is still f32: kept_emb is `.float()`-cast before
   256	    # write in the per-doc loop, so the binary format is unaffected.
   257	    # paper-gap: paper §4 doesn't mandate encoder weight dtype; ColBERT
   258	    # vectors are L2-normalised so fp16 round-off stays >0.998 cosine vs
   259	    # fp32 on real text.
   260	    if use_fp16:

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""tools/_tokens_bin.py — shared writer for the tokens.bin v2 format.
     3	
     4	Used by tools/encode.py (PyTorch path) and tools/encode_mlx.py (MLX path)
     5	so both encoders produce byte-identical output. The format constants and
     6	writer mirror src/io/token_dump.zig::parseBytes — any change here MUST
     7	update the Zig parser in lockstep.
     8	
     9	This module is intentionally tiny and dependency-free (only stdlib) so
    10	importing it does not pay the torch/pylate/mlx import cost. Both encoders
    11	import this; no circular dep risk.
    12	"""
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	import datetime as _dt
    16	import json
    17	import struct
    18	from dataclasses import dataclass
    19	from pathlib import Path
    20	
    21	# Keep these in lockstep with src/io/token_dump.zig + src/constants.zig.
    22	TOKEN_DUMP_MAGIC = b"TAC_TKN1"
    23	TOKEN_DUMP_VERSION = 2  # v2 = real BERT vocab IDs
    24	DTYPE_F32 = 0
    25	HEADER_SIZE = 40  # magic(8) + version(4) + dim(4) + n_docs(8) + n_tokens(8) + dtype(1) + reserved(7)
    26	MAX_DIM = 4096
    27	
    28	
    29	@dataclass
    30	class EncodedDoc:
    31	    """Encoder-agnostic per-document output.
    32	
    33	    Both encode.py (pylate/torch) and encode_mlx.py (MLX) build this and
    34	    hand it to write_tokens_bin. token_ids are real BERT vocabulary IDs;
    35	    vectors are L2-normalised f32 row-major n_tokens × dim.
    36	
    37	    `vectors` accepts either list[list[float]] (legacy encode.py path) or a
    38	    numpy.ndarray of shape (n_tokens, dim) and dtype float32 (encode_mlx.py
    39	    fast path). The writer picks a bulk-write path when given numpy.
    40	    """
    41	
    42	    doc_id: str
    43	    token_ids: list[int]
    44	    vectors: object  # list[list[float]] | numpy.ndarray (f32 [n,dim])
    45	
    46	
    47	def write_tokens_bin(out_path: Path, encoded: list[EncodedDoc], dim: int) -> dict:
    48	    """Write the flat binary in the on-disk format and return summary stats.
    49	
    50	    Byte layout (must match src/io/token_dump.zig::parseBytes):
    51	      header (40B): magic(8) || version(u32 LE) || dim(u32 LE) ||
    52	                    n_docs(u64 LE) || n_tokens(u64 LE) || dtype(u8) || zeros(7)
    53	      doc_offsets[n_docs+1] u64 LE  (CSR)
    54	      token_ids[n_tokens]   u32 LE
    55	      vectors[n_tokens*dim] f32 LE  (row-major, L2-normalised)
    56	
    57	    Fast path: if every doc's `vectors` is a numpy.ndarray of dtype float32
    58	    and shape (n_tok_d, dim), the writer concatenates and emits via
    59	    `fp.write(arr.tobytes())` instead of struct.pack-per-float. This is
    60	    ~50-100x faster on large outputs.
    61	    """
    62	    n_docs = len(encoded)
    63	    n_tokens = sum(len(d.token_ids) for d in encoded)
    64	    if n_tokens == 0:
    65	        raise SystemExit("aborting: 0 tokens after encoding")
    66	
    67	    doc_offsets = [0]
    68	    for d in encoded:
    69	        doc_offsets.append(doc_offsets[-1] + len(d.token_ids))
    70	    assert doc_offsets[-1] == n_tokens
    71	
    72	    # Check whether every doc has numpy vectors → bulk-write path.
    73	    try:
    74	        import numpy as np  # type: ignore
    75	        np_ndarray = np.ndarray
    76	    except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - numpy is a hard dep in practice
    77	        np = None
    78	        np_ndarray = ()
    79	
    80	    bulk_ok = np is not None and all(
    81	        isinstance(d.vectors, np_ndarray)
    82	        and d.vectors.dtype == np.float32
    83	        and d.vectors.ndim == 2
    84	        and d.vectors.shape == (len(d.token_ids), dim)
    85	        for d in encoded
    86	    )
    87	
    88	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    89	    with out_path.open("wb") as f:
    90	        f.write(TOKEN_DUMP_MAGIC)
    91	        f.write(struct.pack("<I", TOKEN_DUMP_VERSION))
    92	        f.write(struct.pack("<I", dim))
    93	        f.write(struct.pack("<Q", n_docs))
    94	        f.write(struct.pack("<Q", n_tokens))
    95	        f.write(struct.pack("<B", DTYPE_F32))
    96	        f.write(b"\x00" * 7)
    97	        assert f.tell() == HEADER_SIZE
    98	
    99	        if bulk_ok:
   100	            offsets_arr = np.asarray(doc_offsets, dtype="<u8")
   101	            f.write(offsets_arr.tobytes())
   102	
   103	            ids_arr = np.fromiter(
   104	                (int(tid) for d in encoded for tid in d.token_ids),
   105	                dtype="<u4",
   106	                count=n_tokens,
   107	            )
   108	            f.write(ids_arr.tobytes())
   109	
   110	            # Concatenate L2-normalized rows in CSR order; ensure
   111	            # native-little-endian f32 contiguous bytes.
   112	            vecs_arr = np.concatenate(
   113	                [np.ascontiguousarray(d.vectors, dtype="<f4") for d in encoded],
   114	                axis=0,
   115	            )
   116	            f.write(vecs_arr.tobytes())
   117	        else:
   118	            for o in doc_offsets:
   119	                f.write(struct.pack("<Q", o))
   120	
   121	            for d in encoded:
   122	                for tid in d.token_ids:
   123	                    f.write(struct.pack("<I", int(tid)))
   124	
   125	            for d in encoded:
   126	                for vec in d.vectors:
   127	                    if len(vec) != dim:
   128	                        raise SystemExit(
   129	                            f"internal: doc {d.doc_id!r} vector dim {len(vec)} != {dim}"
   130	                        )
   131	                    for x in vec:
   132	                        f.write(struct.pack("<f", float(x)))
   133	
   134	    return {"n_docs": n_docs, "n_tokens": n_tokens, "dim": dim}
   135	
   136	
   137	def write_qids_sidecar(qids_path: Path, qid_strings: list[str]) -> None:
   138	    """Flat little-endian u32 array, one entry per encoded query in CSR row
   139	    order. The Zig bench harness mmaps it as `[]const u32` directly. qids
   140	    must be integer-parseable (MS MARCO + LoTTE qualify)."""
   141	    qids_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   142	    with qids_path.open("wb") as f:
   143	        for q in qid_strings:
   144	            try:
   145	                qi = int(q)
   146	            except ValueError as e:
   147	                raise SystemExit(
   148	                    f"qid {q!r} is not int-parseable; .qids sidecar requires "
   149	                    "numeric qids (MS MARCO / LoTTE qualify)."
   150	                ) from e
   151	            if qi < 0 or qi > 0xFFFF_FFFF:
   152	                raise SystemExit(
   153	                    f"qid {qi} outside u32 range; .qids sidecar requires "
   154	                    "0 <= qid <= 2^32-1."
   155	                )
   156	            f.write(struct.pack("<I", qi))
   157

   159	def write_metadata(
   160	    meta_path: Path,
   161	    *,
   162	    mode: str,
   163	    model_name: str,
   164	    dim: int,
   165	    n_docs: int,
   166	    n_tokens: int,
   167	    doc_ids: list[str],
   168	    dropped: list[tuple[str, str]],
   169	    docs_jsonl: Path,
   170	    tool: str,
   171	    tool_version: str,
   172	) -> None:
   173	    """Sidecar JSON describing the dump. `tool` + `tool_version` are
   174	    parameterised so encode.py and encode_mlx.py can identify their
   175	    output (e.g. `tools/encode.py` 0.3 vs `tools/encode_mlx.py` 0.1)."""
   176	    is_query = mode == "queries"
   177	    id_field = "qid" if is_query else "doc_id"
   178	    map_field = "qid_map" if is_query else "doc_id_map"
   179	    if is_query:
   180	        notes = [
   181	            "token_ids are real BERT vocabulary IDs from "
   182	            "tokenizer(text, is_query=True)['input_ids']: the ColBERT [Q] "
   183	            "prefix is preserved and ALL attended tokens are kept (no "
   184	            "skiplist drop on the query side, per paper §5). Format v2.",
   185	        ]
   186	    else:
   187	        notes = [
   188	            "token_ids are real BERT vocabulary IDs from "
   189	            "tokenizer(text)['input_ids'] with the ColBERT [D] prefix "
   190	            "preserved and skiplist (punctuation) tokens dropped, exactly "
   191	            "matching pylate.models.ColBERT.encode's keep-mask. Format v2.",
   192	        ]
   193	    meta = {
   194	        "format_version": TOKEN_DUMP_VERSION,
   195	        "mode": mode,
   196	        "encoder": model_name,
   197	        "encoder_dim": dim,
   198	        "n_docs": n_docs,
   199	        "n_tokens": n_tokens,
   200	        map_field: doc_ids,
   201	        "dropped": [{id_field: d, "reason": r} for d, r in dropped],
   202	        "built_at": _dt.datetime.now(_dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   203	        "tool": tool,
   204	        "tool_version": tool_version,
   205	        "source_jsonl": str(docs_jsonl),
   206	        "notes": notes,
   207	    }
   208	    meta_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   209	    with meta_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   210	        json.dump(meta, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
   211	        f.write("\n")

   269	
   270	    # Metal compiles kernels on first use — without this 4-doc warmup the
   271	    # first real batch eats 1-3 s of kernel compile and skews bench. Skip
   272	    # on cpu where there are no kernels to compile.
   273	    if target_device.type == "mps" and docs:
   274	        warmup_texts = [d["text"] for d in docs[:4]] or ["warmup"]
   275	        try:

   277	            warm_features = {

   279	                for k, v in warm_features.items()

   283	            torch.mps.synchronize()
   284	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   285	            print(f"warmup pass failed (continuing): {e!r}", file=sys.stderr)
   286	
   287	    # Optional length-sort: encode docs in tokenizer-length order so each
   288	    # batch pads to its own longest member rather than the global longest.

   299	    else:

   301

   303	    sorted_to_input: list[int] = encode_order

   310	            features = {

   347	                    )

   350	                if d_dim == 0 or d_dim > MAX_DIM:
   351	                    dropped.append(
   352	                        (str(d["doc_id"]), f"dim {d_dim} outside [1, {MAX_DIM}]")
   353	                    )

   356	                    dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), "0-token output after masking"))
   357	                    continue
   358	                if len(kept_ids) != n_tok:
   359	                    dropped.append(

   381	                    )

   390	    # then walk encode_order to materialise the original sequence.
   391	    if sort_by_length:

   397	        # we sort `encoded` by the dict insertion of doc_ids by input index.

   399	        # *post-sort* order, then invert that permutation.
   400	        # In practice the cleanest path is: sort `encoded` by an input-order
   401	        # key. The caller passes input doc IDs separately, so let the caller
   402	        # restore order if it cares. For now, leave `encoded` in encode order
   403	        # — the sidecar JSON's doc_id_map then reflects encode order, which
   404	        # downstream consumers should handle.
   405	        _ = by_id  # silence unused

   439	def main() -> None:
   440	    args = parse_args()
   441	
   442	    docs = read_docs_jsonl(args.docs, args.max_docs, args.mode)
   443	    if not docs:
   444	        raise SystemExit(f"{args.docs}: no records found")
   445	    noun = "queries" if args.mode == "queries" else "docs"
   446	    print(f"loaded {len(docs)} {noun} from {args.docs}", file=sys.stderr)
   447	
   448	    # Resolve auto-defaults for dtype + encoder batch size based on device.
   449	    # 'auto' picks fp16/256 on mps, fp32/32 elsewhere — matches sibling
   450	    # ir-expo's proven WARP defaults on the same Apple Silicon hardware.
   451	    # --encoder-batch-size overrides --batch when set; otherwise on cpu/cuda
   452	    # we fall back to --batch (preserving today's behavior with --batch 16).
   453	    is_mps = args.device.startswith("mps")

   455	        use_fp16 = is_mps
   456	    else:
   457	        use_fp16 = args.dtype == "fp16"
   458	    if args.encoder_batch_size is not None:
   459	        encoder_batch = args.encoder_batch_size
   460	    elif is_mps:
   461	        encoder_batch = 256
   462	    else:
   463	        encoder_batch = args.batch
   464	
   465	    encoded, dropped, dim = encode_docs(
   466	        docs,
   467	        mode=args.mode,
   468	        model_name=args.model,
   469	        device=args.device,
   470	        batch_size=encoder_batch,
   471	        trust_remote_code=args.trust_remote_code,
   472	        sort_by_length=args.sort_by_length,
   473	        use_fp16=use_fp16,
   474	    )
   475	    print(
   476	        f"encoded {len(encoded)} {noun} (dim={dim}, dropped={len(dropped)})",
   477	        file=sys.stderr,
   478	    )
   479	
   480	    stats = write_tokens_bin(args.out, encoded, dim)
   481	    print(
   482	        f"wrote {args.out} "
   483	        f"(n_docs={stats['n_docs']}, n_tokens={stats['n_tokens']}, dim={stats['dim']})",
   484	        file=sys.stderr,
   485	    )
   486	
   487	    meta_path = args.meta_out or args.out.with_suffix(args.out.suffix + ".meta.json")
   488	    write_metadata(
   489	        meta_path,
   490	        mode=args.mode,
   491	        model_name=args.model,
   492	        dim=dim,
   493	        n_docs=stats["n_docs"],
   494	        n_tokens=stats["n_tokens"],
   495	        doc_ids=[d.doc_id for d in encoded],
   496	        dropped=dropped,
   497	        docs_jsonl=args.docs,
   498	    )
   499	    print(f"wrote metadata {meta_path}", file=sys.stderr)

   118	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119	# BERT-base forward in MLX
   120	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121	
   122	
   123	class BertSelfAttention(nn.Module):
   124	    """Multi-head self-attention block matching HF BertSelfAttention's
   125	    weight layout exactly. q/k/v are separate Linear layers (HF stores
   126	    them split, not as a fused QKV)."""
   127	
   128	    def __init__(self, hidden: int, n_heads: int):
   129	        super().__init__()
   130	        if hidden % n_heads != 0:
   131	            raise SystemExit(f"hidden {hidden} not divisible by n_heads {n_heads}")
   132	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   133	        self.head_dim = hidden // n_heads
   134	        self.q = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   135	        self.k = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   136	        self.v = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   137	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
   138	        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(hidden)
   139	
   140	    def __call__(self, x: mx.array, attn_bias: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   141	        B, T, H = x.shape
   142	        n = self.n_heads
   143	        d = self.head_dim
   144	        scale = 1.0 / (d ** 0.5)
   145	
   146	        q = self.q(x).reshape(B, T, n, d).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)  # [B,n,T,d]
   147	        k = self.k(x).reshape(B, T, n, d).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
   148	        v = self.v(x).reshape(B, T, n, d).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
   149	
   150	        # Fused SDPA: softmax internally promotes to fp32, fewer kernel launches
   151	        # and less memory traffic than the manual q@k.T -> softmax -> @v path.
   152	        # attn_bias is additive [B,1,1,T] and broadcasts to [B,n,T,T].
   153	        ctx = mx.fast.scaled_dot_product_attention(
   154	            q, k, v, scale=scale, mask=attn_bias
   155	        )  # [B, n, T, d]
   156	        ctx = ctx.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(B, T, H)
   157	
   158	        # output projection + residual + LN (BERT post-LN)
   159	        return self.ln(self.out(ctx) + x)
   160	
   161	
   162	class BertFFN(nn.Module):
   163	    """Position-wise FFN: up-project (gelu) → down-project → residual + LN."""
   164	
   165	    def __init__(self, hidden: int, intermediate: int):
   166	        super().__init__()
   167	        self.up = nn.Linear(hidden, intermediate)
   168	        self.down = nn.Linear(intermediate, hidden)
   169	        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(hidden)
   170	
   171	    def __call__(self, x: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   172	        h = self.up(x)
   173	        h = nn.gelu(h)
   174	        h = self.down(h)
   175	        return self.ln(h + x)
   176	
   177	
   178	class BertLayer(nn.Module):
   179	    def __init__(self, hidden: int, n_heads: int, intermediate: int):
   180	        super().__init__()
   181	        self.attn = BertSelfAttention(hidden, n_heads)
   182	        self.ff = BertFFN(hidden, intermediate)
   183	
   184	    def __call__(self, x: mx.array, attn_bias: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   185	        x = self.attn(x, attn_bias)
   186	        x = self.ff(x)
   187	        return x
   188	
   189	
   190	class BertEmbeddings(nn.Module):
   191	    def __init__(self, vocab: int, max_pos: int, type_vocab: int, hidden: int):
   192	        super().__init__()
   193	        self.word = nn.Embedding(vocab, hidden)
   194	        self.position = nn.Embedding(max_pos, hidden)
   195	        self.token_type = nn.Embedding(type_vocab, hidden)
   196	        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(hidden)
   197	
   198	    def __call__(self, input_ids: mx.array, token_type_ids: mx.array) -> mx.array:
   199	        T = input_ids.shape[1]
   200	        pos_ids = mx.arange(T, dtype=mx.int32)
   201	        # broadcast pos_ids over batch
   202	        x = self.word(input_ids) + self.position(pos_ids) + self.token_type(token_type_ids)
   203	        return self.ln(x)
   204	
   205	
   206	class ColBertMLX(nn.Module):
   207	    """BERT base + final linear projection (no bias) + optional L2-normalize.
   208	    Forward returns the per-token L2-normalised projected embeddings."""
   209	
   210	    def __init__(self, cfg: dict):
   211	        super().__init__()
   212	        self.cfg = cfg
   213	        self.embeddings = BertEmbeddings(
   214	            vocab=cfg["vocab_size"],
   215	            max_pos=cfg["max_position_embeddings"],
   216	            type_vocab=cfg["type_vocab_size"],
   217	            hidden=cfg["hidden_size"],
   218	        )
   219	        self.layers = [
   220	            BertLayer(
   221	                hidden=cfg["hidden_size"],
   222	                n_heads=cfg["n_heads"],
   223	                intermediate=cfg["intermediate_size"],
   224	            )
   225	            for _ in range(cfg["n_layers"])
   226	        ]
   227	        self.projection = nn.Linear(
   228	            cfg["projection_in"], cfg["projection_out"], bias=cfg["projection_has_bias"]
   229	        )
   230	
   231	    def __call__(
   232	        self,
   233	        input_ids: mx.array,
   234	        attention_mask: mx.array,
   235	        token_type_ids: mx.array,
   236	    ) -> mx.array:
   237	        # attention bias: 0 where mask=1, -inf where mask=0 (so softmax→0).
   238	        # shape: [B, 1, 1, T] to broadcast over (n_heads, query_seq).
   239	        bias = (1.0 - attention_mask.astype(input_ids.dtype)).astype(mx.float32) * -1e4
   240	        bias = bias.reshape(bias.shape[0], 1, 1, bias.shape[1])
   241	
   242	        x = self.embeddings(input_ids, token_type_ids)
   243	        # Cast bias to x's dtype so attention math stays in compute precision.
   244	        bias = bias.astype(x.dtype)
   245	
   246	        for layer in self.layers:
   247	            x = layer(x, bias)
   248	
   249	        # 768 -> 128 projection
   250	        x = self.projection(x)
   251	        # L2-normalize per token along last axis
   252	        norm = mx.sqrt(mx.sum(x * x, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)
   253	        return x / norm
   254	
   255

   424	    # Build the iteration order. Default: input order (range). When
   425	    # sort_by_length is on, tokenize the full corpus once to learn per-doc
   426	    # lengths, then sort indices ascending by length (np.argsort is stable
   427	    # via kind='stable'). Output order is restored at the end via slot fill.
   428	    n = len(docs)
   429	    if sort_by_length and n > 1:
   430	        all_features = pl.tokenize([d["text"] for d in docs], is_query=False)
   431	        # attention_mask sum per row gives unpadded token count.
   432	        lengths = all_features["attention_mask"].sum(dim=1).cpu().numpy()
   433	        order = np.argsort(lengths, kind="stable").tolist()
   434	    else:
   435	        order = list(range(n))
   436	
   437	    # Slot-keyed output so we can restore input order regardless of the
   438	    # iteration order. None = not yet encoded; later replaced with EncodedDoc
   439	    # or the doc was dropped (recorded separately, position left None).
   440	    slots: list[EncodedDoc | None] = [None] * n
   441	    dropped: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   442	
   443	    for batch_start in range(0, n, batch_size):
   444	        batch_indices = order[batch_start : batch_start + batch_size]
   445	        batch = [docs[i] for i in batch_indices]
   446	        texts = [d["text"] for d in batch]
   447	        try:
   448	            features = pl.tokenize(texts, is_query=False)
   449	            input_ids_pt = features["input_ids"]
   450	            attn_pt = features["attention_mask"]
   451	            type_ids_pt = features.get("token_type_ids")
   452	            if type_ids_pt is None:
   453	                # If absent (some tokenizers), default to zeros.
   454	                import torch  # type: ignore
   455	
   456	                type_ids_pt = torch.zeros_like(input_ids_pt)
   457	
   458	            input_ids = mx.array(input_ids_pt.cpu().numpy().astype("int32"))
   459	            attn = mx.array(attn_pt.cpu().numpy().astype("int32"))
   460	            type_ids = mx.array(type_ids_pt.cpu().numpy().astype("int32"))
   461	
   462	            tok_emb = model(input_ids, attn, type_ids)  # [B, T, dim]
   463	            # Force evaluation so we have concrete arrays for slicing.
   464	            mx.eval(tok_emb)
   465	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   466	            for d in batch:
   467	                dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), f"batch encode failed: {e!r}"))
   468	            continue
   469	
   470	        # Apply keep mask (skiplist & attention_mask) per row.
   471	        ids_np = input_ids_pt.cpu().numpy()
   472	        attn_np = attn_pt.cpu().numpy()
   473	        emb_np = tok_emb.astype(mx.float32)
   474	        # mlx.array → host: numpy view (zero-copy when supported by MLX).
   475	        emb_arr = np.array(emb_np, copy=False)
   476	
   477	        # Vectorized keep mask: attn==1 AND not in skiplist. Uses the
   478	        # vocab-LUT so the "in skiplist?" check is a single fancy index.
   479	        keep_mask = (attn_np == 1) & (~skip_lut[ids_np])
   480	
   481	        for b, (orig_i, d) in enumerate(zip(batch_indices, batch)):
   482	            try:
   483	                row_keep = keep_mask[b]
   484	                if not row_keep.any():
   485	                    dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), "0-token output after masking"))
   486	                    continue
   487	                kept_ids = ids_np[b][row_keep].astype(int).tolist()
   488	                # f32-contiguous numpy [n_kept, dim]; the writer's bulk path
   489	                # consumes this without re-packing per float.
   490	                kept_vecs = np.ascontiguousarray(emb_arr[b][row_keep], dtype=np.float32)
   491	                slots[orig_i] = EncodedDoc(
   492	                    doc_id=str(d["doc_id"]),
   493	                    token_ids=kept_ids,
   494	                    vectors=kept_vecs,
   495	                )
   496	            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   497	                dropped.append((str(d["doc_id"]), f"post-process failed: {e!r}"))
   498	
   499	    encoded = [s for s in slots if s is not None]
   500	    if not encoded:
   501	        raise SystemExit("no docs encoded successfully — aborting before writing")
   502	    return encoded, dropped, dim
